### [1] [Feasibility of a collider-based detector search for upward-going fermions produced from gravitationally-bound dark matter within the Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09194)
*Tamas Almos Vami,Sanjit Masanam,Matt Bellis,Josephine Swann,Philip Tanedo,Adam Green*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了利用LHC级别通用探测器搜寻地球内暗物质湮灭产生的暗光子及其衰变产物（向上μ子）的可行性，发现预期灵敏度显著优于现有极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质理论预言其可被地球引力俘获并湮灭为暗光子，暗光子与标准模型光子动能混合后可在近地表衰变为可观测费米子对，这为探测地球束缚暗物质提供了可能途径，但目前尚缺乏针对LHC级通用探测器的系统灵敏度评估。

Method: 计算假设通用探测器中源自该暗物质模型的向上μ子的预期信号率，分析显著本底对灵敏度的影响，并考虑探测器的实验效率以确定拟议分析的预期极限。

Result: 所得到的预期极限较现有极限有显著改善，强烈支持开展针对源自地球内束缚暗物质的向上μ子的搜寻。

Conclusion: 利用LHC级通用探测器搜寻地球内暗物质湮灭产生的向上μ子具有良好前景，其预期灵敏度显著优于现有极限，值得推进相关实验搜寻。

Abstract: Dark matter is theorized to be gravitationally captured within the Earth and to subsequently annihilate into dark photons. The dark photons kinetically mix with the Standard Model photons which would allow the decay of the dark photon to pairs of observable fermions near Earth's surface. We examine the feasibility and sensitivity of an LHC-class general-purpose detector to observe muons originating from these dark matter models. We calculate the expected signal rates at a hypothetical general-purpose detector and examine the effect of significant backgrounds on sensitivity. We also consider experimental efficiencies of such a detector to determine expected limits for the proposed analysis. We find these expected limits constitute a significant improvement over existing ones, strongly motivating a search for upward-going muons stemming from dark matter bound within the Earth.

</details>


### [2] [The Masses of Bosons and Usual Fermions on Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(4)_{L}\times U(1)_{X}$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09199)
*M. C. Rodriguez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文详细研究了最小超对称 $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(4)_{L}\times U(1)_{X}$ 模型中玻色子与常规费米子的质量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型的玻色子与费米子质量谱此前缺乏系统、详细的分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于最小超对称 $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(4)_{L}\times U(1)_{X}$ 规范模型，对玻色子与常规费米子的质量谱进行详细的理论推导与计算。

Result: 给出了该超对称模型中玻色子与常规费米子的质量谱。摘要未提供具体数值或解析结果。

Conclusion: 建立 / 给出了最小超对称 $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(4)_{L}\times U(1)_{X}$ 模型中玻色子与常规费米子的质量谱结构。

Abstract: We will study in details the masses spectrum of bosons and usual fermions of the Minimal Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(4)_{L}\times U(1)_{X}$ Model.

</details>


### [3] [On the analytic continuation and matching of threshold singularities from Vacuum Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09202)
*Selomit Ramírez-Uribe,Prasanna K. Dhani,German F. R. Sborlini,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文详细讨论了Loop-Tree Duality中用于从真空振幅生成散射振幅干涉项的、初始态在壳能量解析延拓到负值的所有运动学构型，并扩展了阈值奇点匹配的讨论，引入角度平均策略以在味约束下实现局域相消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Loop-Tree Duality框架下，从真空振幅生成散射振幅干涉项需要对初始态在壳能量作解析延拓至负值，但相关运动学构型的系统讨论不足；同时，当阈值奇点匹配受味（flavour）约束时，如何实现局域相消仍缺乏有效策略。

Method: 对初始态在壳能量解析延拓到负值的所有运动学构型进行详细讨论；扩展此前关于阈值奇点匹配的分析；引入角度平均（angular averaging）作为实现局域相消的策略。

Result: 给出了解析延拓到负初始态能量的全部运动学构型的完整讨论；阐明了阈值奇点匹配的扩展分析；提出角度平均方法，可在真空振幅阈值奇点匹配受味约束时实现局域相消。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Loop-Tree Duality中通过真空振幅生成干涉项的解析延拓框架，并提供角度平均这一高效手段，在味约束导致阈值奇点匹配受限时仍可实现局域相消。

Abstract: We provide a detailed discussion for all kinematical configurations of the analytic continuation to negative values of initial-state on-shell energies used to generate interferences of scattering amplitudes from vacuum amplitudes in the Loop-Tree Duality. We also extend previous discussions on the matching of threshold singularities and introduce angular averaging as an efficient strategy to achieve a local cancellation when the matching of threshold singularities from vacuum amplitudes is constrained by flavour.

</details>


### [4] [Nuclear interference versus dark sector excitation in the 248 keV LUX-ZEPLIN recoil candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09230)
*Imtiaz Khan,Salvatore Capozziello,G. Mustafa,Farruh Atamurotov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验LZ高能核反冲单事件能否由弹性相互作用中的核响应而非吸热暗区激发来解释，通过协变匹配、壳模型计算及Ar/Xe对比给出判别性预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 2026高能核反冲搜寻报告一个能量约248 keV的事件，并发现弹性同位旋矢量相互作用在重暗物质区被约3.3σ地局域偏好，需要判别该反冲尺度来自核响应还是暗区非弹性（吸热）过程。

Method: 采用协变匹配关联四个Galilean算符，代数消去纵向自旋响应并固定密度与轨道自旋轨道振幅的干涉；使用GCN与JJ55氙壳模型计算天然氙相消位置；对40Ar直接计算并与吸收型暗扇区激发做对比；结合晕模型分析年度调制。

Result: GCN与JJ55氙壳模型给出211和214 keV的第二个天然氙相消；重质量区位置随DM质量由200 GeV到渐近极限变化小于0.01 keV；曲率加权同位素质心再现其位置与残余深度；40Ar计算将核特征移至约350与425 keV；吸热激发随暗态劈裂按约化质量标度，1 TeV时Ar/Xe尺度比为1.082，而核计算为1.63–2.01；弹性散射年度调制仅几个百分点，吸热解在最大实验室晕速附近季节变化大得多。

Conclusion: 尺度与时间行为提供了区分目标依赖核相消与暗区运动学设定激发能的检验，可用于判断LZ事件来源。

Abstract: The 2026 LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) high energy nuclear recoil search reports one event at $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}~\mathrm{keV}$ and finds the elastic isovector interaction $\mathcal L_6^v$ locally favored at about $3.3σ$ in the heavy dark matter regime. We examine whether this recoil scale can arise from resolved nuclear response within the elastic interaction and compare its spectral, target, and annual modulation behavior with endothermic dark sector excitation. Covariant matching correlates four Galilean operators, removes the longitudinal spin response algebraically, and fixes interference between density and orbital spin orbit amplitudes. The GCN and JJ55 xenon shell model calculations give a second natural xenon cancellation at $211$ and $214~\mathrm{keV}$. In the heavy mass regime, its position changes by less than $0.01~\mathrm{keV}$ from a dark matter mass of $200~\mathrm{GeV}$ to the asymptotic limit. A curvature weighted isotope centroid reproduces the position and residual depth of the natural xenon minimum. Direct $^{40}$Ar calculations shift the nuclear feature to about $350$ and $425~\mathrm{keV}$, whereas endothermic excitation follows reduced mass scaling set by the dark state splitting. At a dark matter mass of $1~\mathrm{TeV}$, the endothermic Ar to Xe scale ratio is $1.082$, compared with $1.63$ to $2.01$ for the nuclear calculations. Elastic scattering gives a few percent annual modulation in the adopted halo model, while endothermic solutions near the maximum laboratory halo speed show much larger seasonal variation. These scaling and timing behaviors provide tests of a target-dependent nuclear cancellation against an excitation energy set by dark sector kinematics.

</details>


### [5] [Level-Four Modular Symmetry Selected by the Harmonic Pattern of Quark and Lepton Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09262)
*Vernon Barger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于模4级有限群 $S_4$ 与 theta 函数结构的味物理框架，用极少数假设和零连续参数统一描述夸克与轻子质量比、CKM 混合及中微子参数，并与实验数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克与带电轻子的质量比呈现规律性：对数代间隔呈 2:1（八度关系），且上-下夸克与下夸克-轻子的排序互为镜像。作者希望用模形式/有限群结构而非连续自由参数来解释这一模式。

Method: 把质量比模式解读为 $Z_N$ 时钟上的电荷计数，发现该结构仅在 level 4 及其倍数出现，从而引入模4级与有限群 $S_4$：三代置于 $T$ 荷 $(3,1,0)$ 的三重态，镜像置于符号单态。带电轻子质量比落在 theta 修饰格点上（精度 $0.02\%$），定出基 $\varepsilon=0.18664=14/75$ 与调和模 $\tau=1.0685i$。由此提出5条关于权重1/2 theta 常数的假设，从三个轻子质量与电弱标度重现六个夸克质量；再加两条假设与一条经验关系约束 $|V_{td}|$，闭合 CKM 矩阵。中微子部分用单一 theta 插入给出 $\sin\theta_{13}$，并用调和条件确定谱。

Result: CKM 预言：$|V_{us}|=0.2255$，$|V_{cb}|=0.04197$，$|V_{ub}|=0.003721$，$\delta=1.138$（实验 $1.139\pm0.023$）。幺正三角形顶点 $(0.161,0.348)$，实验 $(0.161\pm0.010,0.347\pm0.010)$；Jarlskog 不变量 $J=3.11\times10^{-5}$，实验 $(3.09\pm0.07)\times10^{-5}$。中微子：$\sin\theta_{13}=\theta_2(\tau)m_2/m_3$ 在 207 天 JUNO 数据上符合 $0.7\sigma$；给出正序谱 $m_1=0.25$ meV，$\Sigma m_\nu=0.0589$ eV，CP 守恒相位 $\delta_{CP}=\pi$，有效 Majorana 质量量子化为 $1.3$–$3.9$ meV。全部预言不含连续参数。

Conclusion: 该框架以模4级 $S_4$ 与 theta 常数结构统一刻画夸克与轻子味结构，预言与实验一致；其紫外完备属于磁化环面类，Pati–Salam 为自然的规范嵌入。

Abstract: The mass ratios of the quarks and charged leptons follow a simple pattern. The logarithmic generation steps come in a two to one ratio, a note to its octave, and the ordering is mirrored between the up-down and down-lepton comparisons. The pattern, read as charge counting on a $Z_N$ clock, is realized at level four and at no level that is not a multiple of four, which motivates modular level four and the finite group $S_4$, with the generations in the triplet at $T$ charges $(3,1,0)$ and the mirror in the sign singlet. The charged-lepton mass ratios lie on a theta-dressed lattice at $0.02\%$, which sets the base $\varepsilon=0.18664$, taken as $14/75$, and the harmonic modulus $τ=1.0685i$. Five postulates in the weight one-half theta constants, identified on the same data, reproduce the six quark masses from three lepton masses and the electroweak scale. Two more and one empirical relation for $|V_{td}|$ close the CKM matrix, giving $|V_{us}|=\sqrt{m_d/m_s}=0.2255$, $|V_{cb}|=0.04197$, $|V_{ub}|=0.003721$, and $δ=1.138$ against the measured $1.139\pm0.023$, with no continuous parameter. The unitarity triangle follows, with apex $(0.161,0.348)$ against the measured $(0.161\pm0.010,0.347\pm0.010)$, and $J=3.11\times10^{-5}$ against $(3.09\pm0.07)\times10^{-5}$. The same structure carries the neutrinos. A single theta insertion gives $\sinθ_{13}=θ_2(τ)m_2/m_3$, which holds at $0.7σ$ on the 207-day JUNO data, and the harmonic condition completes a normal-ordered spectrum with $m_1=0.25$~meV, $Σm_ν=0.0589$~eV, a CP-conserving phase $δ_{CP}=π$, and effective Majorana masses quantized at $1.3$ to $3.9$~meV. The ultraviolet completion belongs to the magnetized torus class, with Pati--Salam the natural gauge embedding.

</details>


### [6] [Nucleon Stron-Interactions Size From Charmonium Photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09318)
*Xiangdong Ji,Sylvester Joosten,Zein-Eddine Meziani,Dimitra A. Pefkou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 结合 Jefferson Lab 近阈 $J/\psi$ 产生数据与夸克部门结果，提取核子内部色场分布，发现其强相互作用空间尺度达 $0.94\pm0.09$ fm，超过电荷与质量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子与中子电磁尺寸差异显著，却因强（色）力束缚而质量近乎相同，如同同一"核子"的两个孪生态。要揭示核子的整体空间延展，必须借助强相互作用而非电磁作用进行探测。

Method: 以 $J/\psi$ 粒子（粲夸克-反夸克构成的紧凑色荷偶极）作为理想探针，利用 Jefferson Lab 近阈 $J/\psi$ 产生的高精度测量数据，并结合近期夸克部门的结果，提取核子中支撑质量生成的色场分布。

Result: 提取出的色场分布范围超过电荷分布与质量分布，给出核子整体强相互作用空间尺度为 $0.94\pm0.09$ fm。

Conclusion: 近阈 $J/\psi$ 产生为探测核子胶子空间结构提供了更精确手段，所揭示的强相互作用空间延展比电磁与质量分布更大，深化了对核子整体结构的理解。

Abstract: Protons and neutrons differ dramatically in their electromagnetic sizes, but are bound by the strong (color) force and have nearly identical masses, like two twin states of the same ``nucleon". To reveal the overall spatial extent, it must be examined through the strong interaction rather than electromagnetism. The $J/ψ$ particle, a compact color-charge dipole of a charm quark and antiquark, provides an ideal probe. Recent high-precision measurements of near-threshold $J/ψ$ production at Jefferson Lab offer a more precise glimpse of the proton's gluonic spatial structure. Combining these data with recent quark-sector results, we extract the distribution of the color field underlying mass generation in the nucleon and find it extends beyond both charge and mass distributions, revealing the nucleon's overall strong-interaction spatial extent of $0.94\pm 0.09$ fm.

</details>


### [7] [Constraints on Neutron-Electron Quantum Dark Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09336)
*V. Kurmangaliyeva,B. Massak,O. Agyl-Mussapar,S. Amangeldinova,W. M. Snow*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将暗物质通过量子涨落与标准模型粒子双线性耦合产生的相互作用（量子暗力）约束，从Brax等人研究的中子-核子情形推广到中子-电子之间的标量双线性交换，并给出新的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brax、Fichet和Pignol已提出“量子暗力”概念并约束了核子间暗标量、暗矢量、暗费米子双线性交换的质量与耦合，但尚未涉及中子与电子之间的标量双线性交换，该参数空间存在空白。

Method: 将Brax等人的量子暗力分析框架推广至中子与电子之间的标量双线性交换，通过计算量子涨落诱导的相互作用势并利用现有实验数据推导约束。

Result: 给出了中子-电子标量双线性交换诱导的量子暗力对暗物质粒子质量与耦合的新约束。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了量子暗力的适用范围，补充了中子-电子通道的约束，为探测此类暗物质相互作用提供了新的参数空间限制。

Abstract: Interactions induced by dark matter particles which couple bilinearly to standard model particles through quantum fluctuations were termed quantum dark forces by Brax, Fichet, and Pignol, who constrained the mass and couplings of dark scalar, dark vector, and dark fermion bilinear exchange between nucleons. We extend their results to derive new constraints on quantum dark forces from bilinear scalar exchange between neutrons and electrons

</details>


### [8] [Heavy Higgsino Interpretation of the LZ Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09385)
*Kevin Langhoff*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事件，纯higgsino通过Z玻色子非弹性散射可解释该事件，但热冻结产生的higgsino（m_χ=1.1 TeV）与空边带和IceCube太阳中微子束缚矛盾。拟合事件、空边带及太阳俘获束缚得到m_χ≈(10^5,10^6) GeV、δ≈(330,480) keV，须通过早期物质主导时期的冻结机制产生，并给出基于Kim-Nilles机制的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个位于低本底区域的248 keV核反冲事件，纯higgsino非弹性散射是一个令人兴奋的可能解释，但需检验其与现有约束（空高能边带、IceCube太阳中微子搜索）的相容性，并探讨其宇宙学产生机制。

Method: 将higgsino质量m_χ与中性态质量劈裂δ拟合到该事件及空边带数据，并重新计算作为m_χ函数的太阳俘获束缚；结合空边带与太阳俘获束缚确定允许参数空间，并考察其对暗物质晕建模的依赖。进一步提出一个通过Kim-Nilles机制在早期物质主导时期冻结产生higgsino的证明性模型。

Result: LZ数据单独最佳拟合于低质量m_χ≈(240,450) GeV，但被太阳俘获束缚排除；与事件、空边带和太阳俘获束缚最一致的参数空间为m_χ≈(10^5,10^6) GeV、δ≈(330,480) keV（对暗物质晕建模有一定依赖）。该higgsino不能由标准冻结产生。

Conclusion: 该higgsino须由标准冻结以外的方法产生，可通过在早期物质主导时期冻结、并以T_RH≈1 TeV再加热宇宙实现；作者给出一个基于Kim-Nilles机制的简单证明性模型，从而将Higgsino质量标度的起源与强CP问题的解决联系起来。

Abstract: The LZ experiment has recently reported a $248$ keV nuclear recoil event in a region where backgrounds are expected to be low. A pure higgsino scattering inelastically through a $Z$-boson is an exciting possible interpretation of this event, but at the mass required for thermal freeze-out to account for all dark matter, $m_χ= 1.1$ TeV, it predicts several events in the empty high-energy sideband corresponding to $E_R\gtrsim350$ keV and is further excluded by searches for high energy neutrinos coming from the Sun by the IceCube detector. We fit the higgsino mass scale and neutral state mass splitting, $m_χ$ and $δ$, to the event and the empty sideband. We additionally recompute solar capture bounds as a function of $m_χ$. The LZ data alone are best fit at low mass, $m_χ\approx (240,\,450)$ GeV, but is excluded by solar capture. The parameter space most consistent with the event, the empty sideband and the solar capture bound is $m_χ\approx (10^5,\,10^6) $ GeV with $δ\approx (330,\,480)$ keV, with some dependence on DM halo modeling. Such a higgsino must be produced by some other method than standard freeze-out; this can be achieved by freezing-out during a period of early matter domination which ends by reheating the universe to temperature $T_{\rm RH}\approx 1$ TeV. We give a simple proof of concept model which does this via a Kim-Nilles mechanism and therefore connects to the origin of the Higgsino mass scale and solves the strong CP problem.

</details>


### [9] [Cutoff Scales in the Type-I 2HDM with Strongly First-Order Electroweak Phase Transitions: One-Step versus Multistep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09444)
*Jin-Hwan Cho,Dongjoo Kim,Jinheung Kim,Jeonghyeon Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Type-I 2HDM中强一阶电弱相变的紫外可行性，通过双圈重整化群分析确定截断能标，发现单步相变存在相变强度与紫外有效性的张力，而双步相变（仅IS）可同时满足高截断能标与大相变强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强一阶电弱相变是电弱重子生成的关键条件，但其在紫外高能标下的理论自洽性（微扰性、幺正性、真空稳定性）常受威胁。本文旨在系统考察Type-I 2HDM在正常与反转情景下，SFOEWPT与紫外截断能标之间的相容性。

Abstract: We investigate the UV viability of strongly first-order electroweak phase transitions (SFOEWPTs) in the Type-I two-Higgs-doublet model (2HDM), considering both the Normal and Inverted Scenarios (NS and IS). In particular, increasing the minimum allowed cutoff scale for two-step SFOEWPTs in the IS favors a light scalar spectrum and moderate $\tanβ$, providing a promising target for current and future collider searches.

Abstract: A critical component of relativistic three-body scattering amplitudes is the one-particle exchange (OPE) process, wherein a single particle is exchanged between incoming and outgoing two-body subsystems. Method: 本文引入一种强化学习方法，将寻找能解释反常的SMEFT算符视为需要决策以达成目标的序列任务，利用RL在巨大算符空间中进行自动搜索与决策。

Abstract: Finding new physics (NP) is the most important problem in particle physics today. Studying ``anomalies'', i.e., measurements of low-energy observables whose values disagree with the predictions of the Standard Model (SM), is a powerful search strategy. The SM Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) provides a general model-independent framework for parameterizing NP; it is natural to try to find the SMEFT operator(s) that can explain such anomalies. This is a challenging task because (i) the number of SMEFT operators is enormous, and (ii) at loop level there are very complicated correlations among the operators. Analyses by humans typically rely on phenomenological intuition to decide which operators are relevant. This is often biased and does not explore the complete SMEFT operator space. Interestingly, reinforcement learning (RL) techniques excel at tasks that require decision making to achieve their goals. In this paper, we introduce an RL method that can be used to find the SMEFT operators that explain any anomalies. We test it on the CDF $W$-mass anomaly, and show that it reproduces (and improves upon) known results. We then consider a far more complicated situation with multiple anomalies and show that, even here, this method is able to find the SMEFT operators that explain the data. Our RL method can therefore be used to efficiently search for NP at the level of SMEFT.

</details>

We also prove that the Clausen expression in arXiv:2608.04107 equals an alternating Euler sum and an absolutely convergent integral, giving closed evaluations for two rational-argument Euler-sum families.

The index coefficients reduce to two rational generators; one admits modular-product formulas reproducing every available coefficient and suggesting an all-order completion. In such cases, we provide a classical superpotential whose critical loci generate the expected ring relations, but which is not obtained by KK reduction from four dimensions, though it shares many characteristics with such KK reductions. Abstract: Violations of Bell's inequalities offer a definitive signal of non-classical (quantum) behavior in local deterministic systems. Yet, in many physical settings where quantum mechanics is expected to play an important role, one cannot construct a Bell-type test using the available observables. Nevertheless, recent work suggests that quantum and classical time evolution may still be differentiated by the pattern of statistical correlations of these observables. In this paper, we explore and generalize the use of correlations as Bell-type test of the nature of time evolution. We show that quantum vacuum fluctuations of closed systems undergoing Hamiltonian evolution produce unique correlations that are not mimicked by classical Hamiltonian systems. Correlations in the quantum vacuum are generally time-independent and are controlled by the energy gap between the ground and exited states. Classical systems exhibit apparent poles at physical frequencies that do not arise in the quantum vacuum. As one approaches the poles, the evolution becomes dominated by a resonant Hamiltonian giving rise to time dependent correlations that eventually decay through dephasing. Finally, we show that this behavior is distinct from other signals of quantum evolution, including applications to quantum optics, quantum walks, and quantum search.

Second, for periodic volumes, we prove that almost-sure convergence under every joint coupling of the finite-volume disorder arrays is equivalent to complete convergence of the one-volume pressure laws. Abstract: We define a derived stack $\mathscr{Rep}_n(X)$ which generalizes the assignment $A\leadsto \operatorname{Rep}_n(A)$ to an algebra of its derived $GL_n$-representation variety of arXiv:1112.1449 from algebras $A$ to perfect stacks $X$ over characteristic 0 fields. In the case of a quasi-projective classical scheme $X$, we show that $\mathscr{Rep}_n(X)$ admits a subfunctor $\operatorname{Quot}^{n,\text{fr}}_{\mathscr{O}_X^n}(X)\subset \mathscr{Rep}_n(X)$, which is in fact represented by a derived scheme almost of finite type. We further construct a Fourier-Mukai integral transform between the derived categories of quasi-coherent sheaves on $X$ and $\operatorname{Quot}^{n,\text{fr}}_{\mathscr{O}_X^n}(X)$ which induces a trace map at the level of Hochschild homology generalizing the trace morphism constructed in arXiv:1112.1449 (for finitely presented commutative algebras). We show that the subfunctor $\operatorname{Quot}^{n,\text{fr}}_{\mathscr{O}_X^n}(X)$ is a derived enhancement of the framed locus of the Quot scheme of points and that this derived scheme is a $GL_n$-torsor over the stack of coherent length $n$ torsion sheaves. Hence, this stack is an analog of the derived character stack for quasi-projective schemes, and we show that for smooth, Calabi-Yau $X$, it inherits a shifted symplectic structure in the sense of arXiv:1111.3209 from the one constructed in arXiv:1812.11913 on the moduli stack of perfect complexes with proper support. Abstract: We study compact Kähler varieties admitting int-amplified endomorphisms from the viewpoint of positivity of tangent sheaves. Abstract: We explore the homogeneous structures of Albanese morphisms and maximal rationally chain-connected fibrations of algebraic varieties with nef anticanonical divisors. Abstract: We prove that, for every smooth complex del Pezzo surface of degree at most four, the enhanced right orthogonal to the structure sheaf determines the surface up to isomorphism. Abstract: We characterize equality in the finite free Stam and entropy-power inequalities, proving that Hermite polynomials are the unique extremizers among simple real-rooted inputs, up to independent translations and scalings. The proof turns this classification into a rigidity problem for projective plane curves. Using hyperbolicity and the Helton-Vinnikov theorem, we express the Jacobian defect as an off-diagonal squared norm in a definite symmetric pencil. Together with score transport, this yields a matrix proof of Stam for all real-rooted inputs. For each simple base pair, the directions in which the defect vanishes are the independent translations and common dilation, forming a three-dimensional subspace in every degree whose curves split into $n$ projective lines. At a collision, the leading configurations are again finite free convolutions of normalized derivatives of the velocity polynomials of the colliding input clusters. Combined with incidence counting, this local formula shows that at most one real fiber is singular, every real singularity is an ordinary totally real multiple point, and the ordered collision multiplicities determine the real normalization covering. For $n\geq3$, every non-split curve has at least $2n-2$ non-real projective discriminant zeros, counted with multiplicity, with equality attained by irreducible curves of geometric genus zero through every simple input pair. The leading Fisher-information coefficient is determined by the colliding tangent configurations, while the finite entropy term retains the gaps between clusters. For $n\geq3$, maximal logarithmic entropy divergence along the optimally weighted score direction is equivalent to Stam equality.

Abstract: We say that an affine variety $X$ is (semi)rigid if all nontrivial actions of the additive group on $X$ have the same invariant ring. Then all such actions comprise the abelian subgroup denoted $\mathrm{SAut}(X)$.

Abstract: We provide a counterexample to the Pierce-Birkhoff conjecture: a continuous function that is piecewise quadratic on a finite collection of semialgebraic sets that partition $\mathbb{R}^n$ but that cannot be expressed as a finite lattice combination of polynomials. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局部Gromov-Witten差分方程究竟编码了哪些几何与枚举信息，特别是量子差分方程中额外λ依赖是否携带亏格零Gopakumar-Vafa谱的信息，从而厘清几何信息与枚举信息之间的层级关系。

Method: 从Bryan-Katz-Leung多重覆盖分解出发，按支撑的最大公约数将例外射线上的非零本原次数组织为单个中心差分算子，得到由显式Fejér型Laurent核控制的方程；对有限支撑情形引入最细公共平移格，并确定按整除性极小的Laurent多项式分母清去算子，进而通过其分圆分解定义局部枚举理论的粗化影子。

Result: 一旦固定例外射线及其格归一化，全量子强迫项、其在λ=0处的经典特化与局部亏格零GV谱相互决定；经典强迫项通过显式除子反演已决定全部局部GV重数，量子差分方程的额外λ依赖不包含亏格零GV谱的更多信息。分圆因子化给出严格更粗的影子。长度二、E6、E7、E8 flop模型显示三种重构失效：相同Kollár长度可给出不同强迫项，不同GV支撑可有相同分圆骨架，相同的局部差分数据不必决定flop的解析类型。

Conclusion: 局部Gromov-Witten差分方程所保留的几何与枚举信息存在精确层级：亏格零GV谱已由经典强迫项完全决定，量子位移轮廓虽非平凡但不增加新信息；分圆骨架仅是严格更粗的影子，且在flop模型中存在多种重构失效。

Abstract: We study the information encoded by difference equations for the local Gromov-Witten theory of contractible rational curves in Calabi-Yau threefolds. Starting from the Bryan-Katz-Leung multiple-cover decomposition, we organize all nonzero primitive degrees on an exceptional ray by a single central-difference operator associated with the greatest common divisor of their support. The resulting equation is governed by explicit Fejér-type Laurent kernels.
  Our main structural result shows that, once the exceptional ray and its lattice normalization are fixed, the full quantum forcing term, its classical specialization at $λ=0$, and the local genus-zero Gopakumar-Vafa spectrum mutually determine one another. In particular, the classical forcing already determines every local GV multiplicity through an explicit divisor inversion. Thus the quantum difference equation carries a nontrivial shift profile, but this additional $λ$-dependence contains no further information about the genus-zero GV spectrum.
  For finite support, we further introduce the finest common shift lattice and determine the minimal Laurent-polynomial denominator-clearing operator with respect to divisibility. Its cyclotomic factorization defines a strictly coarser shadow of the local enumerative theory. Explicit length-two, $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$ flop models exhibit three distinct failures of reconstruction: the same Kollár length can give different forcing terms, different GV supports can have the same cyclotomic skeleton, and identical local difference data need not determine the analytic type of the flop. These results give a precise hierarchy of the geometric and enumerative information retained by local Gromov-Witten difference equations.

</details>

### [76] [Probing entanglement scaling across a quantum phase transition on a quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18602)
*Qiang Miao,Tianyi Wang,Kenneth R. Brown,Thomas Barthel,Marko Cetina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用多尺度纠缠重整化拟设（MERA）与全连通囚禁离子量子计算机，实现全息子系统层析方案，在临界点附近高效提取可观测量和纠缠性质，并首次在数字量子计算机上观测到子系统纠缠熵的对数律标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子物质因维度灾难和复杂纠缠结构而难以研究，在连续量子相变附近尤为突出：量子涨落横跨所有长度尺度。量子模拟器虽可受控访问强关联体系，但临界现象研究受发散关联长度导致的有限尺寸效应阻碍；多体纠缠的实验研究也受测量协议限制。

Method: 采用多尺度纠缠重整化拟设（MERA），在全连通囚禁离子量子计算机上实现子系统层析的全息方案。该方法用少量量子比特精确表示无限体系与长程关联，从而在临界性下也能高效提取可观测量和纠缠性质。

Result: 观测到具有自发对称性破缺的量子相变，揭示了纠缠性质跨越临界点的演化；首次在数字量子计算机上展示了临界时子系统纠缠熵的对数律标度。

Conclusion: 该成果凸显了MERA在量子计算机上研究强关联多体系统的潜力。

Abstract: The investigation of strongly-correlated quantum matter is difficult due to the curse of dimensionality and intricate entanglement structures. These challenges are particularly pronounced in the vicinity of continuous quantum phase transitions, where quantum fluctuations manifest across all length scales. While quantum simulators give controlled access to a number of strongly correlated systems, the study of critical phenomena has been hampered by finite-size effects arising from diverging correlation lengths. Moreover, the experimental investigation of entanglement in many-body systems has been hindered by limitations in measurement protocols. To address these challenges, we employ the multiscale entanglement renormalization ansatz (MERA) and implement a holographic scheme for subsystem tomography on a fully-connected trapped-ion quantum computer. Our method accurately represents infinite systems and long-range correlations with few qubits, facilitating the efficient extraction of observables and entanglement properties, even at criticality. We observe a quantum phase transition with spontaneous symmetry breaking and reveal the evolution of entanglement properties across the critical point. For the first time, we demonstrate log-law scaling of subsystem entanglement entropies at criticality on a digital quantum computer. This achievement highlights the potential of MERA for the investigation of strongly-correlated many-body systems on quantum computers.

</details>


### [77] [Finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill state-enhanced optical interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09227)
*Ashmita Roy,R. Srikanth,Deepak Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限能量GKP态增强的光学干涉测量，比较其在SU(2)与SU(1,1)干涉仪中相对于压缩真空注入的相位灵敏度。结果表明，包络足够宽的GKP态可超越压缩真空，但光子数降低会削弱该优势，且光学损耗会缩小二者差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩真空态被认为是与相干态配对时的最优高斯资源。作者希望探讨非高斯GKP态能否在光学干涉测量中进一步提升相位灵敏度，并系统比较其与高斯资源在损耗情形下的性能差异。

Method: 构建以相干光加GKP态为输入的SU(2)与SU(1,1)干涉仪方案，分析相位灵敏度；采用量子Fisher信息（QFI）估计方法，将有限能量GKP态与压缩真空注入进行对比，并考虑光学损耗与平均光子数变化的影响。

Result: 包络足够宽的有限能量GKP态优于压缩真空注入，且该优势不依赖于是否存在光学损耗，源于GKP态多压缩峰带来的鲁棒性。但平均光子数降低会减小GKP包络宽度，此时在等光子数或更低光子数条件下压缩真空表现更优。光学损耗会使两种输入态的相对优势均减弱，因为二者渐近趋于无压缩真空态。

Conclusion: 本文展示了有限能量GKP态在光学干涉测量中的直接应用，提出QFI估计方法，并给出了使用非高斯资源进行相位估计的流程，与传统高斯态进行了对比。

Abstract: We present the case of a Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) state-enhanced optical interferometry with detailed analysis of the phase sensitivity for both the SU(2) and SU(1,1) interferometers. The conventional quantum-enhanced SU(2) interferometer, employing coherent light at one input port and squeezed light at the other, is compared with a modified configuration using coherent light and a GKP state. While it is known that the squeezed vacuum state is the optimal Gaussian resource input mode when paired with the coherent state, we show that the finite-energy GKP state with sufficiently broad envelope outperforms the squeezed vacuum injection, irrespective of the presence of optical losses. This can be attributed to the enhanced robustness coming from the availability of multiple squeezed peaks in the GKP case. However, because lowering the mean photon number reduces the GKP envelope width, the squeezed vacuum input performs better when compared with a GKP state of equal or lower mean photon number. We also observe that optical losses tend to diminish the relative advantage of either input state, since both states approach the (unsqueezed) vacuum state asymptotically. Our work demonstrates the direct application of finite-energy GKP states in optical interferometry along with a methodology for estimating the quantum Fisher information (QFI) and presents a phase estimation procedure using non-Gaussian resources in comparison with conventional Gaussian states.

</details>


### [78] [The Geometry of Transport in Quantum Walks and Parrondo's Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09269)
*Jose Alfredo de Leon,Mariana Pérez-Muralles,Jan Neuser,Carlos Pineda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在最小离散时间量子行走中研究Parrondo悖论，引入由硬币稳态编码的"传输向量"，其与初始硬币态的内积给出行走者的渐近速度，并以此给出悖论出现的几何判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parrondo悖论——组合失败策略却得到获胜策略——在量子行走中的机制尚缺乏统一的几何解释，尤其难以说明为何某些组合方式（如单步内复合两个硬币算符）能产生悖论，而简单交替却不能。作者希望给出一个适用于任意策略组合的普适判据。

Method: 在最小离散时间量子行走框架下，定义传输向量（由硬币的稳态编码），其与初始硬币态内积即为渐近速度；进而提出几何判据：当组合策略的传输向量落在各单独策略张成的锥之外时，悖论出现。

Result: 几何判据对任意策略组合成立；据此解释了单步内复合两个硬币算符可产生悖论，而简单交替不能，因为交替使组合向量被限制在锥内。悖论集合具有非零测度，并在若干代表性情形下显式计算出其概率。

Conclusion: 该工作将Parrondo悖论重新诠释为量子行走中"可达传输设计"的一个实例，为理解与构造此类悖论提供了统一的几何视角。

Abstract: We study Parrondo's paradox -- the phenomenon where combining losing strategies yields a winning one -- in a minimal discrete-time quantum walk. We introduce the transport vector, encoded in the coin's steady state, whose inner product with the initial coin state gives the walker's asymptotic velocity. More generally, the paradox emerges exactly when the transport vector of the combined strategy falls outside the cone spanned by the individual ones -- a geometric criterion valid for any combination of strategies. It explains, for instance, why composing two coin operators within a single step can produce the paradox while simple alternation between them cannot, since alternation keeps the combined vector confined to the cone. The paradoxical set has nonzero measure, and we compute its probability explicitly in representative cases. This casts the paradox as one instance of designing reachable transport in quantum walks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建变分量子算法（VQA），采用时空编码方案，将整个离散时空解编码于单个变分量子态中，一次性重构整个时空域而无需时间推进，从而对所有时间点联合优化。代价函数由两部分组成：稀疏测量失配项与物理信息PDE违背项，使数据与支配方程同时对解施加约束。通过数值模拟在一维Burgers方程和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上演示。

Result: 数值模拟结果表明，该方法能够在一维Burgers与Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上重构非线性PDE动力学，验证了时空编码变分量子算法在稀疏测量条件下重构非线性PDE解的有效性。

Conclusion: 具有时空编码方案的变分量子算法为重构非线性PDE动力学提供了一个紧凑的框架，可联合利用稀疏数据与物理方程约束，实现全时空解的一体化重构。

Abstract: Reconstructing fields governed by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) from sparse measurements is a challenging task because the governing equations are strongly nonlinear and observations are available at only a few locations. Fluid velocity fields are a representative case. In this paper, we propose a variational quantum algorithm that reconstructs the solution over the entire spacetime domain at once. Rather than marching in time, the method encodes the full discrete spacetime solution in a single variational quantum state, so that all time points are optimized jointly. The cost function combines a sparse-measurement mismatch term with a physics-informed PDE violation term, letting data and the governing equation constrain the solution simultaneously. We demonstrate the method on the one-dimensional Burgers and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equations using numerical simulations. The results suggest that variational quantum algorithms with a spacetime encoding scheme offer a compact framework for reconstructing nonlinear PDE dynamics.

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) is a machine learning approach which employs the internal dynamics of a physical system (the reservoir) to encode and process information. In this work, we explore the use of dual-unitary circuits in a brickwork architecture as a platform for QRC, well suited to current noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices. Dual-unitary circuits present both practical and conceptual advantages. Our results indicate that, under appropriate conditions, dual-unitarity can lead to an enhanced regime of operation: we numerically verify that it improves memory effects and nonlinear processing, and shields against finite-shot noise, mitigating exponential concentration. Moreover, dual unitarity offers an intuitive picture of how operator dynamics gives rise to memory and nonlinear processing in circuit-based reservoirs.

</details>


### [81] [Python in the front, party in the Backline: compiling quantum workloads across CPUs, GPUs, and FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09270)
*Joseph K. L. Lee,Mehrdad Malekmohammadi,Hong-Sheng Zheng,Shuli Shu,Cheick Doumbia,Kalman Szenes,Mehran Zamani Abnili,Thomas Ainsworth,Matthew Seymour,Thomas Germain,Leonhard Neuhaus,Josh Izaac,Lee J. O'Riordan*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Backline——一个构建于 PennyLane 与 Catalyst 之上的异构编译与运行时框架，支持从 Python 前端经 MLIR 直接编译量子-经典工作负载，并在 CPU、GPU、FPGA 及分布式远端硬件上实现微秒级低延迟协同执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从量子研发走向生产级、容错量子工作负载执行是量子平台构建者的重大挑战：Python 框架易用但解释执行延迟高，无法满足实时量子纠错（QEC）的低延迟要求；FPGA/ASIC 性能高但编程模型专有、开发僵化耗时；CPU/GPU 等加速器则面临异构基础设施下跨设备编程与抽象复杂的问题。

Method: 提出 Backline 异构编译与运行时框架，内建于 PennyLane 和 Catalyst 之中。它允许以 Python 高级语言编写量子-经典工作负载，经由 MLIR 编译直接映射到高性能、低延迟设备，支持跨 CPU、GPU、FPGA 的混合目标以及本地与分布式远程硬件，且前端保持厂商无关。

Result: 以 AMD VPK120 FPGA 板作为控制器，由其硬件握手引擎发起每一轮；在 RoCE v2 上、每条路径 $10^6-1$ 轮测试中测得中位稳态往返延迟：到 AMD Ryzen Threadripper PRO CPU 为 2.305 μs，到 AMD Instinct MI210 GPU 为 4.5 μs，展示了微秒级同步协同处理能力。

Conclusion: Backline 证明了可从厂商无关的 Python 前端出发，为多样化的分布式异构目标编译并执行低延迟量子工作负载，实现微秒级同步协处理，为实用规模量子系统所需的关键基础设施奠定基础。

Abstract: Moving from quantum research and development to production-grade, fault-tolerant quantum workload execution remains one of the most significant challenges facing quantum platform builders. While Python frameworks have enabled an easy entry point for quantum algorithm design, the low-latency requirements for real-time quantum error correction (QEC) demand performance that traditional interpreted environments cannot provide. FPGAs and ASICs play a central role at these layers, but their specialized programming models make development rigid and time-consuming. CPUs, GPUs, and other accelerators introduce a different challenge: as infrastructure becomes increasingly heterogeneous, programming across different devices and their associated abstractions becomes more complex. Allowing researchers to write workloads in high-level languages that map to low-latency execution across diverse distributed target platforms will enable the development of key infrastructure for utility-scale quantum systems. For this, we introduce $\textit{Backline}$, a heterogeneous compilation and runtime framework built within PennyLane and Catalyst. Backline allows us to design and build quantum-classical workloads for high-performance and low-latency devices, with compilation directly from a Python interface through MLIR. We demonstrate the compilation and execution of several quantum workloads with low-latency data movement across a mix of CPUs, GPUs, and FPGAs, for both local and distributed remote hardware targets, all from a vendor-agnostic Python frontend. With an AMD VPK120 FPGA board as the controller, issuing each round from its hardware-handshake engine, we measured median steady-state round-trip latencies over RoCE v2 of $2.305~μ$s to an AMD Ryzen Threadripper PRO CPU and $4.5~μ$s to an AMD Instinct MI210 GPU across $10^6-1$ rounds per path, demonstrating microsecond-scale synchronous co-processing.

</details>


### [82] [Sub-extensive non-stabilizerness in the Dyck-Fredkin spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09545)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子Rényi熵是衡量非稳定子性（magic）的定量指标，此前对多体量子态通常表现为随系统尺寸N的广延标度Θ(N)。作者希望探究具有非寻常谱隙标度的局域无阻挫模型中该量是否呈现不同行为，并考察其是否能提供区别于纠缠熵的新视角。

Method: 以自旋-1/2 Dyck-Fredkin链及其t-形变为研究对象，该模型为局域无阻挫且具有异常谱隙标度。利用其组合结构，进行数值精确的有限尺寸计算，分析基态稳定子Rényi熵的渐近行为。

Result: 数值精确计算表明渐近行为依赖于参数t：t<1时为Θ(N)，t=1时为Θ(log N)，t>1时为Θ(1)。

Conclusion: t=1处的标度可能是模型非传统临界性的又一表现；其与纠缠熵行为的对比表明，非稳定子性可能为理解量子多体系统提供新的窗口。

Abstract: The stabilizer Rényi entropy is a quantitative measure of non-stabilizerness, or magic, and has typically been found to scale extensively with system size $N$ (i.e., $Θ(N)$) for a variety of many-body quantum states. In this note, we study the stabilizer Rényi entropy of the ground state of the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Dyck-Fredkin chain and its $t$-deformation, a local frustration-free model with unusual spectral-gap scaling. Exploiting the combinatorial structure, we carry out numerically exact finite-size calculations, which indicate asymptotic behavior depending on $t$: $Θ(N)$ for $t<1$, $Θ(\log N)$ at $t=1$, and $Θ(1)$ for $t>1$. The scaling at $t=1$ could be another manifestation of the unconventional criticality of the model, while the contrast with the behavior of the entanglement entropy suggests that non-stabilizerness might provide a new window into quantum many-body systems.

</details>

*Ian Low,Navin McGinnis*

TL;DR: 相对本征相位环面上的纠缠能力函数在 $2^{n-1}$ 个"角点"（各相对相位为 $0$ 或 $\pi$）处静止，该处 $U$ 等价于广义反射 $R=\mathbb{I}-2Q$，纠缠能力由 $Q$ 的七个局域酉不变量表达；可实现为角点的充分必要条件为 $U^2\propto\mathbb{I}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠能力是衡量酉算符从乘积态生成纠缠的核心量，但其在谱分解参数空间（本征相位环面）上的整体结构尚不清楚。作者希望刻画该函数在相位空间中的驻点结构，从而为门电路的纠缠资源评估提供一般性框架。

Method: 固定谱投影算符、去掉整体相位后，将纠缠能力视为 $(n-1)$ 维相对本征相位环面上的函数；通过在该环面上对相对相位求极值（驻点分析），识别出所有相对相位取 $0$ 或 $\pi$ 的 $2^{n-1}$ 个角点，并证明其间驻定性与 $U^2\propto\mathbb{I}$ 的等价条件；进而以 $Q$ 的局域酉不变量重构角点处的纠缠能力表达式。

Result: 证明了纠缠能力在全部 $2^{n-1}$ 个角点处驻定；角点处 $U$ 等价于广义反射 $R=\mathbb{I}-2Q$（$R^2=\mathbb{I}$），纠缠能力可由 $Q$ 的七个局域酉不变量表示。$U$ 能作为某投影族角点的充要条件是 $U^2\propto\mathbb{I}$，该条件被大量 Clifford 与非 Clifford 门满足。以两量子比特门、$SU(N)$ 信道分解和双格点自旋链为例，给出极小、极大与鞍点实例；并发现某些在全相位环面上为鞍点的角点，沿不同时间演化轨迹可表现为局部极大或极小。

Conclusion: 角点构成纠缠能力在相位环面上的普适驻点集合，为纠缠能力的分析与优化提供了结构化工具，并揭示了谱相位自由度与纠缠生成之间的一般关系。

Abstract: The entangling power of a unitary operator acting on a bipartite Hilbert space measures the entanglement it generates from product states, averaged over the inputs. A finite-dimensional unitary has a spectral decomposition $U=\sum_{a=1}^{n}e^{iθ_a}P_a$, where $e^{iθ_a}$ are the eigenvalues, $P_a$ the corresponding eigen-projectors, and $n$ is the number of distinct eigenvalues. After removing an overall phase, the entangling power is a function on the $(n-1)$-torus of relative eigenphases at fixed spectral projectors. We prove that this function is stationary at all $2^{n-1}$ points on the torus where every relative phase is $0$ or $π$, which we define as \textit{corners}. Up to an overall phase, $U$ at each corner is a generalized reflection $R=\mathbb{I}-2Q$ satisfying $R^2=\mathbb{I}$, where $Q$ is the sum of spectral projectors whose relative phase is $π$. At the corner the entangling power is expressed in terms of seven local-unitary invariants of $Q$. A unitary gate $U$ can be realized as a corner of some projector family if and only if $U^2\propto\mathbb{I}$, a condition satisfied by many Clifford and non-Clifford gates. We illustrate the theorem with two-qubit gates, $SU(N)$ channel decompositions, and two-site spin chains, obtaining examples of minima, maxima, and saddle points. In addition, a corner that is a saddle point on the full phase torus can appear as a local maximum or minimum along different time-evolution trajectories.

</details>

Abstract: Spatiotemporal fluctuations across multiple scales govern and characterize the emergent properties, phase boundaries, and low-energy excitations of strongly correlated matter. However, capturing these dynamics has remained a major experimental challenge, as conventional probes typically offer either high spatial or temporal resolution, but not both simultaneously. Here, we leverage high-fidelity wide-field imaging of dense diamond nitrogen vacancy center ensembles to measure the momentum and frequency power spectral density of magnetic fluctuations. To access spatial wavevectors below the diffraction limit, we tune the sensing volume continuously through optical depletion. This approach enables study of equilibrium and driven fluctuations across three orders of magnitude in spatial scale and tunable frequency bands, providing a direct means to map low-energy, long-wavelength fluctuations in correlated systems.

*Rui-Hao Li*

Motivation: 制备量子吉布斯态需同时复现热分布与多体本征空间，现有方法缺乏对谱结构的系统分解与误差归因，故需建立可分离误差来源并具备微扰可控性的统一框架。

Result: 在均匀局域性与可解性假设下，一阶约化残差有界且关于形变强度二次；数值实验显示微扰区中哈密顿量级失配与局域吉布斯态误差近似二次压制；修正线路结构在微扰控制之外作为变分拟设仍有价值，常优于裸态与规定的一阶态。

Abstract: Preparing a quantum Gibbs state requires reproducing both its thermal distribution and the associated many-body eigenspaces. In this work, we formalize the spectral core-tail architecture (SCTA) as a framework comprising a structured thermal core, a geometrically characterized unitary tail, and an exact residual measuring the remaining core-frame Hamiltonian mismatch. We derive a local Gibbs-state error bound separating core-preparation, tail-implementation, and modeling errors. The modeling contribution is volume uniform when its Kubo-Mori response meets the shell summability condition and the relevant local data remain uniform. We then highlight three anchor Hamiltonian classes which admit exact core-tail constructions with zero residuals. For small deformations of an exact anchor, we employ a Schrieffer-Wolff reduction procedure to construct a corrected core-tail pair that formally removes the deformation order by order. Under uniform locality and solvability assumptions, we show that the first-order reduction yields a residual that remains bounded and is quadratic in the deformation strength. Furthermore, we numerically test the first-order reduction on deformed graph-stabilizer Hamiltonians. The numerical results show approximately quadratic suppression of both the Hamiltonian-level mismatch and the local Gibbs-state error in the perturbative regime. We also find that the constructed correction circuit structure remains useful as a variational ansatz beyond perturbative control, often improving on both the bare and prescribed first-order states.

</details>

Motivation: 热涨落如何修饰或抑制量子增强传感是量子度量学的核心问题，且与量子热力学中"信号诱导的功"和"传感器获取的信息"之间的关系密切相关，但缺乏统一的一般性理论框架。

Method: 建立热量子态的灵敏度（以不同量子Fisher信息度量量化）、温度依赖性与幺正信号诱导功之间的一般关系；分析高温极限下各单调QFI度量的渐近行为；在连续变量系统中针对二次高斯信号与高次信号分别研究无限温极限，并推导二次信号发生器下无限温QFI与零温SLD QFI、基态方差的下界。

Result: 高温下所有单调QFI度量在主阶上趋于一个普适量，可表示为某简单可观测量的方差，且该量本身定义了一个较少为人知的QFI度量；连续变量系统中，二次高斯信号的无限温极限有限且一般非零，而高次信号发散；对任意纯二次信号发生器，无限温QFI至少为零温SLD QFI的两倍，等价地至少为发生器基态方差的八倍，从而使估计量方差的Cramér–Rao下界至少降低一半。

Conclusion: 热量子态灵敏度存在高温下的普适涌现唯一性（类比经典Fisher信息在信息几何中的唯一性）；二次信号在高温下仍保持量子增强优势，Cramér–Rao下界至少减半；结果以玻色模谐振链（适用于囚禁离子平台）与量子场论模型进行了说明。

Abstract: Understanding how thermal fluctuations modify or suppress quantum-enhanced sensing is a central problem in quantum metrology. Closely related questions arise in quantum thermodynamics, particularly regarding the relation between the work induced by a signal and the information acquired by a sensor. Here, we establish general relations among the sensitivity of thermal quantum states, as quantified by different quantum Fisher information metrics, their temperature dependence, and the work induced by a unitary signal. We further show that, at high temperature, all monotone QFI metrics coincide to leading order with a universal quantity that can be expressed as the variance of a simple observable and itself defines a lesser-known QFI metric. This emergent uniqueness is reminiscent of the uniqueness of classical Fisher information in information geometry. In continuous-variable systems, the infinite-temperature limit is finite and generically non-zero for quadratic Gaussian signals, while it diverges for higher-degree signals. Remarkably, for any purely quadratic signal generator, the infinite-temperature QFI is at least twice the zero-temperature SLD QFI or, equivalently, at least eight times the ground-state variance of the generator. Consequently, the Cramér--Rao lower bound on the estimator variance is reduced by at least a factor of two. We illustrate these results using harmonic chains of bosonic modes relevant to trapped-ion platforms and quantum field-theoretic models.

</details>


### [87] [Convex-Gaussianity of fermionic Gibbs states in perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09608)
*Kaifeng Bu,Yuanjie Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究弱扰动相互作用费米子系统中的Gibbs态结构，证明了在低温阈值以下Gibbs态可分解为高斯态的凸组合，并给出该阈值的渐近紧致性，同时应用于Fermi-Hubbard模型的弱耦合与强耦合两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相互作用费米子系统中Gibbs态的量子结构，尤其是其何时可用高斯态（即无关联态）的凸组合来刻画，是量子多体物理中的核心问题。该工作旨在定量给出温度阈值，并揭示不同耦合机制下凸-高斯性的来源。

Method: 考虑稀疏Hamiltonian H=H_0+V，其中H_0为二次型、V为尺度ε的非二次扰动，通过解析手段研究Gibbs态ρ_β的凸-高斯分解；并将一般框架应用到最大度数为D的任意图上具有跃迁t和格点相互作用U的Fermi-Hubbard模型，分别考察弱耦合（小|U|）与强耦合（小|t|）区域。

Result: 证明当逆温度β≤O(log(1/ε))时，ρ_β可分解为高斯态的凸组合；并构造特定稀疏Hamiltonian表明β≤Θ(log(1/ε))是必要的，即该界渐近紧致。对弱耦合Fermi-Hubbard模型该框架直接适用；在强耦合区域进一步证明直到β≤O(|U|^{-1}log(|U|/(D|t|)))时Gibbs态仍保持凸-高斯性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了相互作用费米子Gibbs态凸-高斯性的统一温度阈值，并揭示弱耦合与强耦合两种区域中凸-高斯性的不同形成机制，同时证明了弱扰动情形下温度阈值的渐近最优性。

Abstract: We study the structure of Gibbs states in weakly perturbed interacting fermionic systems. First, for a sparse Hamiltonian $H=H_0+V$ with a quadratic term $H_0$ and a non-quadratic perturbation $V$ of scale $ε$, we show that the Gibbs state $ρ_β$ decomposes into a convex combination of Gaussian states whenever the inverse temperature satisfies $β\le O(\log(1/ε))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is asymptotically tight by establishing that $β\le Θ(\log(1/ε))$ is necessary for certain sparse Hamiltonians. This general framework applies directly to the weak-coupling (small-$\vert{}U\vert{}$) regime of the Fermi--Hubbard model with hopping $t$ and on-site interaction $U$ on any graph of maximum degree $D$. Complementarily, in the strong-coupling (small-$\vert{}t\vert{}$) regime, we show that the Gibbs state remains convex-Gaussian up to $β\le O\big(\vert{}U\vert{}^{-1}\log(\vert{}U\vert{}/(D\vert{}t\vert{}))\big)$, revealing a mechanism for convex-Gaussianity distinct from the weak-coupling setting.

</details>


### [88] [Bell tests for collider decay processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09294)
*Danilo M. Fucci,Alexandre C. Orthey,Alan J. Barr,Christopher G. Timpson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出对撞机衰变的贝尔测试，用独立采样分析轴作设置、探测器区域赋二元或零结果；证明贝尔违背需后选择（漏洞），但在测量独立性与接收事件操作等价下，利用该漏洞的局部模型必为测量上下文性的；推导高有效自旋分析力下被单态关联违背的贝尔不等式，其违背见证贝尔非定域性或测量上下文性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将对撞机衰变纳入贝尔测试框架，检验量子非局域性；同时拟明确此类测试中零结果/探测器接受度带来的后选择漏洞及其对结论的影响。

Method: 以独立采样的分析轴作为测量设置，以探测器区域分配二元或零（null）结果，构建对撞机衰变的贝尔测试；分析后选择漏洞，引入测量独立性与不同设置间接收事件的操作等价性假设，推导基于有效自旋分析力的贝尔不等式。

Result: 贝尔违背若无后选择则不可实现，故存在漏洞；在上述假设下，任何利用该漏洞的局部模型必须表现为测量上下文性；当有效自旋分析力足够高时，单态关联违背所导出的贝尔不等式。

Conclusion: 该不等式被违背即见证贝尔非定域性或测量上下文性，为对撞机衰变中的贝尔测试提供了严格判据及对后选择漏洞的界定。

Abstract: We propose Bell tests for collider decays using independently sampled analysis axes as settings and detector regions to assign binary or null outcomes. We show that a Bell violation requires postselection, opening a loophole. However, under measurement independence and operational equivalence of acceptance events across settings, any local model exploiting the loophole must be nonclassical in the sense of measurement contextuality. We derive a Bell inequality violated by singlet correlations when the effective spin-analyzing powers are sufficiently high. Its violation then witnesses Bell nonlocality or measurement contextuality.

</details>


### [89] [Multivariate quantum state preparation with optimized tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09304)
*Matthew L. Sims-Goh,Lukasz Cincio,Annina Z. Lieberherr,Mekena McGrew,Thomas R. Bromley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出SCENT方法，利用成对纠缠度量构建树张量网络（TTN）结构，克服MPS在处理多变量函数时的链式拓扑瓶颈，并结合环境张量近似电路编译，实现高效量子态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子张量列（quantics tensor train）以MPS表示连续函数的振幅编码，对单变量函数极为强大；但处理多变量函数时，线性链拓扑导致高纠缠量子比特间距离过大，性能迅速下降。因此需要一种更适合多变量函数的张量网络拓扑结构。

Method: 提出SCENT（Spectral Clustering for Entanglement miNimizing Trees）协议：利用可高效计算的成对纠缠度量，确定合适的树张量网络（TTN）结构，再用张量交叉插值（TCI）高效近似。随后将优化后的TTN应用于态制备，提出基于环境张量方法的近似电路编译方法，并直接利用TTN固有的规范自由度，在给定电路深度下获得更高保真度。

Result: SCENT显著优于MPS方法，并改进了以往TTN方法。旗舰演示中，将具有长程、非最近邻变量关联的20变量概率分布编码到200量子比特态制备电路中，仅用43284个CNOT门即达到7.44×10⁻⁹的不保真度；深度与保真度可权衡，同一分布用5584个CNOT门即可达到10⁻³不保真度。

Conclusion: SCENT通过构建纠缠最小化的树张量网络拓扑，有效解决了多变量函数振幅编码中MPS链式结构的局限，并在量子化学与金融投资组合优化等典型态制备问题上验证了该流水线的高效性与可调性。

Abstract: Quantics tensor trains are attracting intense interest for quantum-inspired computing and quantum state preparation. These methods, which approximate continuum functions by representing their amplitude encoding as a matrix product state (MPS), are exceedingly powerful for univariate functions but rapidly become challenging when handling multivariate functions, since the linear chain topology leads to a large distance between highly-entangled qubits. We overcome this limitation by introducing SCENT (Spectral Clustering for Entanglement miNimizing Trees). SCENT is a protocol that utilizes efficiently-computable pairwise entanglement metrics to determine a suitable tree tensor network (TTN) structure, which can then be efficiently approximated using tensor cross-interpolation (TCI); we find that it substantially outperforms MPS methods and improves upon previous TTN methods. We then apply these optimized TTNs to state preparation, introducing an approximate circuit compilation method based on environment-tensor methods without significantly conceding overall accuracy. Importantly, the inherent gauge freedom of TTNs can be directly exploited in this method, resulting in higher fidelity at a given circuit depth. We demonstrate this quantum state-preparation pipeline on archetypal state-preparation problems in quantum chemistry and financial portfolio optimization. In our flagship demonstration, we encode a 20-variable probability distribution with long-ranged, non-nearest neighbor inter-variable correlations in a 200-qubit state-preparation circuit with infidelity $7.44\times 10^{-9}$ using only 43284 CNOTs; depth and fidelity can be traded, allowing the same distribution to be prepared to infidelity $10^{-3}$ with as few as 5584 CNOTs.

</details>


### [90] [Cascade-induced high-performance nonreciprocal quantum batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09690)
*Niaz Ali Khan,Dahai He*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出此前基于Metelmann-Clerk形式报道的非互易量子电池"四倍能量积累增强"源于存在根本性不一致的Lindblad主方程，改用级联开放量子系统形式后，获得了与耗散无关的十六倍稳态非互易能量优势，并揭示了依赖耗散的电池-充电器效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易量子电池通过储库工程实现可控能量转移，是量子储能的新范式。此前基于Metelmann-Clerk形式的研究报告了相对互易体系四倍的非互易能量积累增强，但该结果所依赖的Lindblad主方程存在根本性不一致，可能系统性地低估了非互易的真实优势。因此需要建立一个数学上自洽、物理上可靠的框架来重新评估非互易量子电池的性能上限。

Method: 采用严格建立的级联开放量子系统（cascaded open quantum systems）形式，替代此前存在不一致的Lindblad主方程描述，以保证数学一致性与物理保真度；在此基础上分析非互易量子电池的稳态能量积累，并与互易体系进行对比；进一步研究电池-充电器效率随耗散（阻尼）配置的变化。

Result: 非互易体系相对于互易体系可获得与参数区域无关的十六倍稳态能量优势，超过此前报道的四倍基准；同时发现电池-充电器效率依赖耗散：在对称阻尼下效率为四倍，当电池耗散较小时效率超过该基准，而当电池耗散较大时效率更低。

Conclusion: 级联形式为非互易量子能量存储提供了数学严谨且实验可行的基础，有望推动高性能量子储能的发展；此前基于Metelmann-Clerk形式的结果因主方程不一致而低估了非互易优势。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal quantum batteries harness reservoir engineering for controlled energy transfer, heralding a paradigm shift for quantum energy storage. A prior study utilizing the Metelmann-Clerk formalism reported a fourfold enhancement in nonreciprocal energy accumulation over reciprocal counterparts. This fourfold enhancement, however, appears to stem from a Lindblad master equation that exhibits fundamental inconsistencies, which may systematically understate the actual nonreciprocal advantage. Our approach is rooted in the rigorously established cascaded open quantum systems formalism, which ensures both mathematical consistency and physical fidelity. Remarkably, we surpass the previously reported fourfold benchmark, attaining a regime-independent sixteenfold steady-state nonreciprocal energy advantage over reciprocal systems. We further uncover a dissipation-dependent battery-to-charger efficiency, fourfold under symmetric damping, surpassing this benchmark when the battery is less dissipative, and lower otherwise. This work establishes the cascaded formalism as a mathematically rigorous and experimentally viable foundation for high-performance quantum energy storage.

</details>


### [91] [Execution-transcript privacy for fault-tolerant surface-code memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09334)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究容错量子计算机遥测日志是否会泄露逻辑输入，提出正确性-隐私对偶定理：在固定轮次计划下，逻辑量子比特到转录记录的量子信道与忽略输入的信道呈 e^{-Θ(d)} 接近；但各向异性存在，振幅阻尼下计算基标签受 Z-距离 d_Z 支配而非码距 d_min。156量子比特处理器实验测得 d_Z=1 存储记录以 TV≥0.927 识别输入，并验证无参数幂律（0.85±0.03 对预测 0.86），随机化编码可将统计量恢复至噪声底且不增加双量子比特门开销。结论：容错不提供转录隐私，只将隐私重新定位到逻辑态而非电路身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算的安全性通常关注逻辑量子比特本身，但实际设备会产生包含校正子、解码动作、重置与时序的遥测日志。问题在于：这些旁路记录是否可能反推出逻辑输入，从而破坏隐私。作者动机是判断"容错"是否自动带来转录隐私，并厘清哪些记录会泄露、泄露程度由哪些参数决定。

Method: 针对固定 T=Θ(d) 轮次计划下的旋转表面码存储，在三条假设（sector-scalar 诚实骨干、转录局部性、Kotecky-Preiss 小性）下，用 diamond 范数刻画逻辑量子比特到转录的信道并给出 e^{-Θ(d)} 接近忽略输入信道的界。分析各向异性：各逻辑轴按其陪集的自身距离付费，振幅阻尼下计算基标签由 Z-距离 d_Z 决定。构造两个量子距离为 1 的码（相位翻转码与重复码）给出具体泄漏。给出匹配逆定理识别真正暴露输入的证据（含 lattice-surgery 奇偶读出）。在 156 量子比特超导处理器上检验充分证书并进行直接测量，采用随机化、标签均衡的采集；固定码变阻尼暴露以复现无参数幂律；测试随机化编码。

Result: 充分证书在实际 156 量子比特处理器上差 21.5 倍，故该定理无法在此处调用。直接测量显示 d_Z=1 存储的记录在随机化标签均衡采集下以总变差 ≥0.927 识别其输入。固定码并改变阻尼暴露可复现无参数律，指数为 0.85±0.03，预测为 0.86。随机化编码可将统计量恢复到噪声底，且不带来双量子比特门开销。相位翻转码的 X-校正子转录在未观测阻尼下严格与输入无关，而重复码在一阶泄漏。

Conclusion: 容错并不自动授予转录隐私；它把隐私重新定位，且仅定位于逻辑态，而非电路的身份。各向异性使实际隐私受各陪集自身距离（尤其 d_Z）控制，而非整体码距；实验证实 d_Z=1 存储会显著泄露输入，而随机化编码可低成本消除该统计量。

Abstract: A fault-tolerant quantum computer runs behind a telemetry stream logging syndromes, decoder actions, resets and timing separately from the answer. Can it reveal the logical input? For a distance-$d$ rotated surface-code memory on a fixed schedule of $T=Θ(d)$ rounds, under three stated hypotheses (sector-scalar honest backbone, transcript locality, Kotecky-Preiss smallness), the channel from logical qubit to transcript is $e^{-Θ(d)}$-close in diamond norm to one that ignores the input. A statement of this kind follows generically from correctability-privacy duality. Anisotropy does not. Each logical axis pays the distance of its own coset, so under amplitude damping the computational-basis label is governed by the code's $Z$-distance $d_Z\ge d_{\min}$ and not by the code distance. Two codes of quantum distance $1$ make the gap concrete. A phase-flip code's $X$-syndrome transcript is exactly input-independent under unobserved damping, while a repetition code leaks at first order. A matched converse identifies the records that do expose it, among them a lattice-surgery parity readout. On a 156-qubit superconducting processor our sufficient certificate misses by $21.5\times$, so the theorem cannot be invoked there. Measured directly, a $d_Z=1$ memory's record identifies its input with total variation $\ge 0.927$ under randomised, label-balanced acquisition. Holding the code fixed and varying the damping exposure reproduces the parameter-free law, with exponent $0.85\pm0.03$ against a predicted $0.86$. Randomized encoding returns the statistic to the floor at no two-qubit-gate cost. Fault tolerance does not grant transcript privacy. It relocates it, and only to the logical state, not to the circuit's identity.

</details>


### [92] [Certified measurement and control of an entanglement-breaking index on programmable hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09350)
*Eran Kopel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用经认证的热碰撞反馈回路，在当前可编程量子硬件上实现量子信道纠缠破缺指数（n_EB）的首次可测量与可控，所有关键量均以区间算术证书形式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道的纠缠破缺指数——即信道经多少次自复合后破坏与任意参考系统间全部纠缠——据作者所知从未被实际测量。作者希望将该整数参数从纯理论概念变为可在现有硬件上测量与调控的实验对象。

Method: 方法上，构造经认证的热碰撞反馈回路：精确建模不同浴极化轮次的复合，给出轮酉门的紧凑编译（全连接七个CNOT、五个原生任意角门、重六边形格上十个门）；用逐轮去极化精确模型保证阶梯结构在真实噪声下仍以证书整数存活；并进行3300万次抽样的门控搜索以刻画热谷对比度。

Result: 测量方面：给出整数阶梯n_EB(p)的目标清单，判定点负度证书裕度为0.025至0.109；3300万次抽样将热谷对比度上界定为一堵带内部极大值的墙，认证值2.85×10^-3接近下限89。控制方面：脉冲阀可在恰好八轮的最小脉冲处切换；最优脉冲信号认证为3.43×10^-3，优于静态协议；浴布洛赫矢量的相干倾斜将信号提升至8.6×10^-3，为静态上限的三倍且零粒子数代价；谷特征在逐轮噪声强度约10^-4处消亡。

Conclusion: 纠缠破缺指数阶梯是近期可实现的实验方案，脉冲热谷方案则可精确标价。研究还通过谷特征在噪声强度~10^-4处消亡，限定了误差外推的负担。

Abstract: The entanglement-breaking index of a quantum channel, the number of self-compositions after which the channel destroys all entanglement with any reference, has to our knowledge never been measured. We show that a certified thermal collision feedback loop makes this integer both measurable and controllable on current programmable hardware, every load-bearing quantity delivered as an interval-arithmetic certificate. On the measurement side: a target list for the integer staircase n_EB(p) with decision-point negativities certified at margins 0.025 to 0.109; an exact per-round depolarising model in which the selected staircase survives realistic noise as certified integers; an exact, tight compilation of the round unitary (seven CNOTs all-to-all, five native arbitrary-angle gates, ten on heavy-hex); and a 33-million-draw gated search bounding the thermal valley's contrast by a wall with an interior maximum, a certified 2.85x10^-3 near floor 89. On the control side: the model composes rounds of differing bath polarisation exactly, so the valley becomes a pulsed valve, switching at a certified minimal pulse of exactly eight rounds; the optimal pulsed signal, a certified 3.43x10^-3, beats the static protocol, so the best valley measurement is intrinsically pulsed; and a coherent tilt of the bath Bloch vector lifts the signal to a certified 8.6x10^-3, three times the static ceiling, at zero population cost. Valley signatures certifiably die near per-round noise strength 10^-4, fixing the error-extrapolation burden. The staircase is a near-term experiment; the pulsed valley a precisely priced one.

</details>


### [93] [Symmetry structure dependent diagnostic of the Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10470)
*Tamizhselvan S,Manju C,Bijay Kumar Agarwalla,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究封闭自旋-1/2量子系统中，基于能量的迹距离（trace distance）能否像纠缠不对称性一样刻画对称性恢复及量子Mpemba效应。通过数值模拟和解析分解，作者发现迹距离可分解为对称性相干项与残余项：在电荷对称性下残余项可忽略，迹距离可复现Mpemba行为；在置换对称性下残余项显著，弛豫动力学定性不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立量子多体系统的对称性恢复是非平衡量子物理的重要问题，已有研究表明量子Mpemba效应可通过纠缠不对称性刻画（初始破缺越强，对称性恢复越快）。然而，传统基于能量的度量（如迹距离）是否也能捕捉同一现象尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清迹距离与纠缠不对称性在刻画对称性恢复动力学时的关系与差异。

Method: 在具有不同对称性的封闭自旋-1/2量子系统中，分析对称性破缺初态的动力学。结合数值模拟与对迹距离的解析分解，将迹距离拆分为对称性相干贡献与残余贡献两部分，并据此判断其追踪纠缠不对称性、复现Mpemba-like行为的条件。

Result: 对电荷对称性，残余贡献可忽略，迹距离有效由对称性扇区相干性主导，因而能复现纠缠不对称性中的Mpemba-like行为。对置换对称性，残余贡献显著，导致弛豫动力学在定性上不同于纠缠不对称性所刻画的行为。

Conclusion: 本文明确了传统基于能量的诊断量（迹距离）在何种条件下能可靠刻画对称性恢复动力学，并阐明了纠缠不对称性与迹距离编码的不同物理信息。

Abstract: Understanding symmetry restoration in isolated quantum many-body systems is an important problem in nonequilibrium many-body quantum physics. Recent studies have shown that the quantum Mpemba effect can be characterized through entanglement asymmetry, where states with stronger initial symmetry breaking restore symmetry faster. However, it remains unclear whether conventional energy-based measures, such as the trace distance, capture the same phenomenon. We investigate this question in closed spin-$1/2$ quantum systems with different symmetries by analyzing the dynamics of symmetry-breaking initial states. Combining numerical simulations with an analytical decomposition of the trace distance into symmetry-coherence and residual contributions, we identify the conditions under which trace distance tracks entanglement asymmetry and reproduces the Mpemba--like behavior observed in it. For charge symmetry, the residual contribution is negligible, making the trace distance effectively governed by symmetry-sector coherences. In contrast, for permutation symmetry, a significant residual contribution leads to qualitatively different relaxation dynamics. Our results establish when conventional energy-based diagnostics reliably capture symmetry-restoration dynamics and clarify the distinct physical information encoded by entanglement asymmetry and trace distance.

</details>


### [94] [From the Unknown to the Desired: Transforming Unknown Initial States in the Resource Theory of Work and Heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09352)
*Tanmoy Biswas,Andreas Winter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究初始量子态未知时热力学状态变换，提出适用于所有允许输入态的通用框架，在互相对易守恒荷下构造守恒荷协议，使变换误差随系统拷贝数超多项式衰减，并实现渐近最优的通用功提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往热与功的资源理论中的状态变换通常依赖对初始态的先验知识（state-dependent protocols）。本文关注初始量子态未知的情形，旨在突破状态依赖设定，探索在有限先验信息下热力学状态变换是否仍可实现。

Method: 超越状态依赖协议，构建“单一变换适用于所有允许输入态”的通用框架；针对互相对易的守恒荷，构造守恒荷的协议；以迹距离量化变换误差，并借助系统拷贝数的增加来分析误差衰减；将该框架应用于通用功提取。

Result: 所构造的守恒荷协议的变换误差（迹距离）随系统拷贝数超多项式衰减；作为应用，可在无初始态微观知识条件下渐近最优地提取功，且误差超多项式小。

Conclusion: 热力学状态变换在有限先验信息下仍可实现；该工作建立了超越“状态已知”设定的资源理论热力学通用框架。

Abstract: We study thermodynamic state transformations in the resource theory of work and heat when the initial quantum state is unknown. Moving beyond state-dependent protocols, we develop a universal framework in which a single transformation applies to all admissible input states. For mutually commuting conserved charges, we construct charge-conserving protocols whose transformation error, quantified by trace distance, decays super-polynomially with the number of system copies. As an application, we demonstrate universal work extraction: asymptotically optimal work can be extracted without microscopic knowledge of the initial state, with a super-polynomially small error. Our results show that thermodynamic state transformations remain achievable under limited prior information and establish a universal framework for resource-theoretic thermodynamics beyond the state-aware setting.

</details>


### [95] [Faster Quantum Monte Carlo Simulation by Random Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10486)
*John M. Martyn,Joshua Lin,Neill C. Warrington,Isaac L. Chuang,Andrew J. Daley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出随机编译量子蒙特卡洛（RC-QMC）框架，通过对一族近似进行平均抑制系统性误差，以几乎零额外计算成本获得相对标准QMC方法在精度上的计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）算法是模拟量子系统最强的经典方法之一，但其精度常受Trotter化等近似引入的系统性误差限制，因此需要一种能普遍抑制此类误差的方法。

Method: 借鉴量子计算中的随机编译思想，构建RC-QMC通用框架：不再依赖单一固定近似，而是对一族近似进行采样平均（采样量子门方式的类比），逐次平均以抵消系统性误差，且计算开销基本不增加。

Result: 在两个典型蒙特卡洛算法上验证了该优势：(1) 用于估计热态的路径积分量子蒙特卡洛；(2) 用于模拟开放系统动力学的量子轨迹方法。在达到目标精度时，RC-QMC相对标准QMC方法具有计算优势。

Conclusion: RC-QMC体现了量子与经典算法的交叉融合，可推广至其他QMC方法，并暗示其在经典模拟中具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) algorithms are among the most powerful classical methods for simulating quantum systems, yet their accuracy is often limited by the systematic errors in the approximations used, such as Trotterization. Here we introduce randomly compiled quantum Monte Carlo (RC-QMC) as a general framework that suppresses these systematic errors by averaging over a family of approximations rather than relying on a single fixed one. This strategy is grounded in the concept of randomized compiling from quantum computing, which suppresses errors by sampling over quantum gates, at essentially no additional computational cost. Consequently, our framework achieves a computational advantage over standard QMC methods when estimating a target state to a desired level of accuracy. We illustrate this advantage on two key Monte Carlo algorithms: (1) path integral quantum Monte Carlo for estimating thermal states, and (2) the quantum trajectories method for simulating open system dynamics. In aggregate, these results represent a cross-fertilization of quantum and classical algorithms, are readily generalizable to other QMC methods, and suggest wider applications in classical simulation.

</details>


### [96] [Materials for Quantum Information Science: Roles in the Quantum Evolution 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09371)
*Thang Pham,Vsevolod Ivanov,Dominic P. Goronzy,Abhiram Devata,Joshua Feldon,David Barton,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述指出量子信息科学已进入"量子演化2.0"阶段，瓶颈从单个量子态的相干控制转向可扩展多量子比特处理器与网络，材料科学因此成为核心。文章系统梳理了超导电路、量子缺陷、量子光子器件及二维材料/异质结等平台的退相干机制、材料认知现状与关键开放问题，揭示出跨平台共享的"结构—相干性"问题，并总结出三大需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子平台性能已不再主要受器件设计限制，而是受制于控制不佳的表面、埋入界面以及原子身份尚不完全明确的缺陷。要推进量子技术的相干性、可扩展性与集成化，必须从材料层面理解并解决这些问题，然而目前尚无任何平台能将特定原子尺度结构与测得的相干性变化定量关联。

Method: 这是一篇综述性工作：针对超导电路、量子缺陷、量子光子器件以及包括二维材料和异质结在内的新兴材料平台，逐一识别主导退相干机制、梳理当前材料理解程度、归纳最紧迫的开放材料问题，并进行跨平台比较，以提炼共性问题与未来需求。

Result: 跨平台比较揭示出一个共享的"结构—相干性"问题：涉及的材料化学在不同平台间反复出现，且通常与处于无序或埋入环境中的轻元素相关；但没有任何平台能够将某一特定原子尺度结构定量地关联到相干性的实测变化。

Conclusion: 作者提出三项关键需求：原子尺度退相干机理的理解、可预测器件性能的高通量替代性指标，以及面向量子材料专门构建的表征工具。解决这些需求将推动所有量子平台在相干性、可扩展性与集成化方面的进步。

Abstract: Quantum information science is entering a second phase, the Quantum Evolution 2.0, in which the challenge has shifted from demonstrating coherent control of individual quantum states to building scalable multi-qubit processors and networks. This transition places materials science at the center of the field. Across superconducting circuits, quantum defects, quantum photonic devices, and emerging materials platforms, including two-dimensional materials and heterostructures, performance is now limited less by device design than by poorly controlled surfaces, buried interfaces, and defects whose atomic identities remain incompletely known. This review surveys the materials challenges of these quantum platforms together with the characterization methods needed to resolve them. For each platform we identify the dominant decoherence mechanisms, the current state of materials understanding, and the most pressing open materials problems. A cross-platform comparison then reveals a shared structure-coherence problem. The implicated material chemistry recurs across platforms, involving light elements in disordered or buried environments, yet no platform can quantitatively connect a specific atomic-scale structure to a measured change in coherence. We close by identifying three needs, mechanistic understanding of decoherence at the atomistic level, high-throughput proxy metrics predictive of device performance, and characterization tools built for quantum materials, whose resolution would advance coherence, scalability, and integration across all platforms.

</details>


### [97] [Phase transitions in first-detection statistics of monitored long-range quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10510)
*Sayan Roy,Shamik Gupta,Giovanna Morigi,Gabriele Perfetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程跳跃量子行走中首次探测返回概率（FDRP），发现跳跃强度按 $d^{-\alpha}$ 衰减时，$\alpha=1$ 为递归与瞬态行为的临界点，并揭示了由投影测量诱导的红外与紫外模式干涉导致的非解析临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次探测返回概率（FDRP）决定量子行走是递归还是瞬态，是刻画量子行走的关键特征；然而长程跳跃如何影响该行为尚不清楚，作者希望明确长程跳跃指数对FDRP及其临界性质的调控作用。

Method: 考虑一条链上的量子行走，初始格点被探测器以频闪方式监测，行走者可在格点间进行长程跳跃，跳跃强度随距离 $d$ 按 $d^{-\alpha}$（$\alpha\geq 0$）衰减；通过分析FDRP随时间的演化及其幂律衰减，并考察总探测概率和衰减指数 $\beta$ 随 $\alpha$ 的变化。

Result: $\alpha=1$ 将递归（$\alpha<1$）与瞬态（$\alpha>1$）量子行走分开，对应总探测概率的连续相变；$\alpha<1$ 时强长程跳跃导致局域化，总探测概率为1；$\alpha>1$ 时行走呈瞬态，返回概率按 $t^{-\beta}$ 幂律衰减；衰减指数 $\beta$ 作为 $\alpha$ 的函数出现非解析点，这些奇异性并非仅由低能谱决定，而是由投影测量诱导的红外与紫外能量模式干涉所致，体现了非幺正动力学固有的临界行为。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃指数 $\alpha$ 唯一地调控量子行走的递归/瞬态转变及临界行为，投影测量诱导的模式干涉产生独特奇异性；该动力学可通过调节 $\alpha$ 在原子与分子系统中进行实验探测。

Abstract: In a quantum walk, the first-detection return probability (FDRP) characterizes salient features, determining whether the quantum walk is transient or recurrent. We study the FDRP of quantum walks on a chain where the initial site is stroboscopically monitored by a detector and the walker performs long-range hopping between sites. We assume that the hopping strength decays with the distance $d$ as $d^{-α}$ and $α\geq 0$ and show that the power-law exponent $α$ critically determines the behavior of the FDRP. The value $α=1$ separates recurrent ($α<1$) from transient ($α>1$) quantum walks through a continuous phase transition in the total detection probability. For $α<1$, strong long-range hopping induces localization, resulting in unit total detection probability. Instead, for $α>1$ the long-range walk is transient and the return probability decays algebraically as a function of time as $t^{-β}$. The associated decay exponent $β$ features nonanalytic points as a function of $α$. Such singularities are not exclusively determined by the low-energy spectrum, but are caused by the interference between infrared and ultraviolet energy modes induced by projective measurements, signalling the emergence of critical behavior intrinsic to the non-unitary dynamics. These dynamics are solely controlled by tuning the long-range exponent $α$ and can thus be experimentally probed in atomic and molecular systems.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Multiplicativity of the Holevo Barycenter of Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09373)
*Sayantan Chakraborty,Stefano Mancini,Leonardo Rossetti,Andreas Winter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定了Holevo重心在信道张量积下的乘性，构造出自张量积后重心不乘性的信道，并证明其熵在张量积下既不普遍次可加也不普遍超可加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holevo重心是达到Holevo容量的系综的平均输出态。两信道的乘性问题即其重心在并行组合下是否张量化。该问题与Holevo容量的可加性问题密切相关：可加性蕴含重心张量化，但张量化本身不足以推出可加性。尽管已有非可加Holevo容量的构造，其对应重心仍张量化，故乘性是否普遍成立悬而未决。

Method: 通过显式构造量子信道，考察其与自身张量积情形下的Holevo重心，并与各自重心张量积进行比较；同时分析Holevo重心熵在张量积下的（次/超）可加性行为。

Result: 构造出信道使Holevo重心在信道自张量积下不满足乘性；并证明Holevo重心熵在张量积下既不普遍次可加也不普遍超可加。

Conclusion: 乘性并非普遍成立：Holevo重心在张量积下可以失效，从而否定乘性普遍成立的猜想，并进一步刻画了该重心熵在张量积下的非单调可加行为。

Abstract: The Holevo barycenter of a quantum channel is the unique output state obtained as the average output of any ensemble achieving the Holevo capacity. Given two quantum channels, the multiplicativity problem asks whether this barycenter tensorizes under parallel composition, namely whether the barycenter of the product channel coincides with the tensor product of the individual barycenters. This question is closely related to the additivity problem for the Holevo capacity: additivity implies tensorization of the Holevo barycenter, while tensorization alone is not sufficient for additivity. Although a construction is known that demonstrates the existence of channels with non-additive Holevo capacity, their corresponding Holevo barycenters still tensorize, leaving open whether multiplicativity might ultimately hold universally. Here, we answer this question in the negative by exhibiting channels for which the Holevo barycenter is not multiplicative under the tensor product of the channel with itself. Moreover, we show that the entropy of the Holevo barycenter is neither universally subadditive nor universally superadditive under tensor product.

</details>


### [99] [Balancing Expressivity and Overfitting in Quantum Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09407)
*Saasha Joshi,Udson C. Mendes,Luke C. G. Govia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子核在GPR主动学习中的正则化敏感性问题，发现即使小规模下过拟合也会导致性能崩溃，需谨慎选择正则化以平衡表达力与过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过查询训练点训练代理模型来建模昂贵的黑箱函数，其性能高度依赖代理模型选择；当代理为GPR时，性能主要由底层核的表达力决定，因此需探究量子核对此框架的影响。

Method: 采用量子核替代GPR中的核函数，改变主动学习的计算动力学，重点研究其对超参数调优正则化的敏感性；并通过限制性量子核实验避免指数集中，分析近term量子器件中噪声对核训练的作用。

Result: 经验表明即使小规模下过拟合也会使GPR性能崩溃；由于量子保真核的平滑性，正则化必须谨慎选择以平衡表达力与过拟合；定性结果可迁移至限制性量子核的实践应用，并揭示近term器件中有价值的噪声类型。

Conclusion: 量子核用于GPR主动学习时需精细调控正则化，限制性量子核设计可规避指数集中，且特定噪声对核训练具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Active learning is a paradigm of machine learning that can be utilized to model expensive black-box functions by training a surrogate model from actively queried training points. The performance of this framework depends heavily on the choice of the surrogate model. When the surrogate is Gaussian Process Regression (GPR), its performance is largely determined by the expressivity of the underlying kernel. In this work, we investigate the peculiarities of using a quantum kernel to change the computational dynamics of active learning with GPR, focusing on the sensitivity to regularization by hyperparameter tuning. While generic, unstructured kernels suffer from exponential concentration at large scale, we empirically demonstrate that even at small scale overfitting can collapse GPR performance. Kernel regularization can be used to counteract this effect, but due to the smoothness of the quantum fidelity kernel, regularization must be carefully chosen to balance expressivity and overfitting. Our qualitative results transfer to the practical application of restricted quantum kernels designed to avoid exponential concentration and also present the kinds of noise that may be valuable to kernel training in near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [100] [Induced random mixed states are symmetric Dirichlet mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09421)
*Brian T. Kirby,Alexander C. B. Greenwood,Sanjaya Lohani,Joseph M. Lukens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了部分迹诱导的随机混合态与Mai-Alquier分布（以对称Dirichlet加权的Haar纯态混合）之间的精确分布等价，并据此给出纯度矩、Hilbert-Schmidt距离与平均行列式的精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分迹诱导的随机混合态在量子信息中广泛出现，但其期望值计算通常缺乏统一且可构造的框架。作者希望借助与已知分布族的等价性，把诱导系综的期望值问题转化为更易处理的Dirichlet矩与低阶Haar平均。

Method: 作者证明D维随机混合态（对K维环境取部分迹）与Mai-Alquier分布（K个独立Haar随机纯态、由对称Dirichlet权重混合）在分布上精确等价；随后利用该等价将诱导系综的期望值化为单系统态空间上的Dirichlet矩与低阶Haar平均的计算。

Result: 作为应用，作者精确恢复了四阶以内的纯度矩，推导了环境维数可能不同的两个独立诱导系综之间的平均Hilbert-Schmidt距离，并给出了精确的平均行列式。

Conclusion: 该等价为诱导随机混合态及纯度矩、重叠、行列式等量的评估提供了统一且可构造的视角。

Abstract: We establish an exact equivalence in distribution between $D$-dimensional random mixed states induced by partial traces over $K$-dimensional environments and the Mai-Alquier distribution, a mixture of $K$ independent Haar-random pure states weighted by a symmetric Dirichlet distribution. This identification recasts a class of expectation values for induced ensembles into calculations involving Dirichlet moments and low-order Haar averages on a single-system state space. As applications, we recover exact purity moments up to fourth order, derive the mean Hilbert--Schmidt distance between independent induced ensembles with possibly different environment dimensions, and obtain exact average determinants. These results provide a unified and constructive perspective on induced random mixed states and on the evaluation of quantities such as purity moments, overlaps, and determinants.

</details>


### [101] [Josephson energy of superconducting junctions: amorphous versus crystalline tunnel barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09426)
*Wanting Zhang,Aldilene Saraiva-Souza,Félix Beaudoin,Xianghua Kong,Hong Guo,Yu Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过 NEGF-DFT 第一性原理器件建模，计算 Al/AlO_x/Al 约瑟夫森结的约瑟夫森能 E_J，比较晶态与非晶态 Al2O3 势垒，揭示非晶氧化物的化学计量不均匀性导致 E_J 巨大涨落的原子尺度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E_J 是决定 transmon 超导量子比特性质的关键参数，在 Al/AlO_x/Al 结中由电子隧穿超薄氧化势垒决定，因而指数依赖于势垒的原子结构。然而，非晶氧化势垒的微观结构无序如何定量映射到 E_J 这一宏观电路能量尺度尚不清楚，缺乏从原子尺度到超导电路参数的定量联系。

Method: 采用基于 NEGF-DFT 量子输运方法的第一性原理器件建模，对比一个晶态 Al2O3 势垒结与十个相同厚度的熔融淬火非晶 Al2O3 势垒结；由费米能级透射率和 Ambegaokar-Baratoff 关系提取 E_J，并进行散射态分析和有限尺寸结的自平均估算。

Result: 非晶集合的平均 E_J/h 为 2.78 GHz，标准差达 4.67 GHz，而晶态参考结仅为 0.73 GHz；单个非晶结的 E_J 值跨越近两个数量级。散射态分析表明输运为量子隧穿，涨落源于非晶氧化物的化学计量不均匀性——富 Al、低势垒区域可连通成类渗流隧穿通道，强烈增强电导。一个实际 200×200 nm² 结可对超过 2×10^4 个此类微观区域自平均。

Conclusion: 研究建立了从氧化物微观结构到超导电路能量尺度 E_J 的定量原子尺度路径，表明非晶势垒的化学计量不均匀性与渗流式隧穿通道是 E_J 涨落的主导来源，并给出器件尺寸自平均的定量估计。

Abstract: The Josephson energy $E_J$ is a key parameter governing the properties of transmon superconducting qubits. In Al/AlO$_x$/Al junctions, $E_J$ is set by electron tunneling through an ultrathin oxide barrier and therefore depends exponentially on the atomic structure of the barrier. We compute $E_J$ by first-principles device modeling based on the NEGF-DFT quantum-transport method, comparing a junction with a crystalline Al$_2$O$_3$ barrier against ten junctions with melt--quenched amorphous Al$_2$O$_3$ barriers of the same thickness. From the Fermi-level transmission and the Ambegaokar--Baratoff relation, we obtain a mean $E_J/h$ of $2.78$ GHz for the amorphous ensemble, with a standard deviation of $4.67$ GHz, compared with $0.73$ GHz for the crystalline reference; individual amorphous values span nearly two orders of magnitude. Scattering-state analysis shows that transport is quantum tunneling and that the variability originates from stoichiometric inhomogeneity of the amorphous oxide: Al-rich, low-barrier regions can connect into percolation-like tunneling pathways that strongly enhance the conductance. A realistic $200\times200$ nm$^2$ junction self-averages over more than $2\times10^4$ such microscopic regions. These results establish a quantitative atomistic route from oxide microstructure to the superconducting-circuit energy scale $E_J$.

</details>


### [102] [Why three? A two-level system with four mutually unbiased questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10078)
*Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 两个尖锐是否问题的互不偏袒等价于其对合反对易；由此无偏问题集构成Clifford代数，最大规模为奇数，故典型四个反对易问题隐含第五个。实数、复数、四元数量子理论分别允许2、3、5个问题而跳过4。本文研究无此算符积限制的四维Bloch球模型——hyperbit，及其在双量子比特中的实现与缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准量子理论中，互不偏袒问题集受Clifford代数的奇最大规模约束，四个反对易问题必然伴随第五个，使四问题二级系统被排除。作者试图探究：若放弃这一算符积限制，是否存在一个四问题二级系统模型，筛选出Jordan状态空间的单系统公设是否允许该模型，从而厘清该模型的本质缺陷及其与标准量子理论的关系。

Method: 以对合反对易刻画互不偏袒问题；将无偏问题集视为Clifford代数；构造对应四维Bloch球（hyperbit）的放松模型；比较其与Jordan状态空间单系统公设的兼容性，并以能量可观测性作为筛选判据；进一步在双量子比特系统中具体实现该模型，分析其Kirkwood-Dirac虚部、复合系统纠缠与相互作用性质、24-cell状态集在去极化下的Spekkens预备语境性，以及费米子表述中四个Majorana算符与宇称的关系。

Result: 四问题二级系统存在，即四维Bloch球（hyperbit）；标准单系统公设（除能量可观测性外）允许该模型，唯独能量可观测性将其排除。该模型在双量子比特中可实现：其Kirkwood-Dirac虚部为隐藏的𝔰𝔬(4)生成元；其复合系统允许多少纠缠但无相互作用；其24-cell状态集在去极化强度2/3内保持Spekkens预备语境性。在费米子表述中，四个问题对应两模的Majorana算符，第五个为宇称，故四问题系统正是被宇称超选择移除的扇区。

Conclusion: 四问题二级系统（hyperbit）虽满足大多数单系统公设，但由于违反能量可观测性而被排除；它本质上是宇称超选择所移除的扇区，表现为隐藏𝔰𝔬(4)生成元、无相互作用的多纠缠复合系统，并保留一定去极化范围内的Spekkens预备语境性。

Abstract: Two sharp yes/no questions are mutually unbiased if and only if their involutions anticommute. A set of unbiased questions is therefore a Clifford algebra, whose maximal size is odd, meaning four anticommuting questions typically imply a fifth: the two-level systems of real, complex and quaternionic quantum theory allow two, three and five questions, while four is skipped.
