Without this operator product restriction, a two-level system with four unbiased questions exists as the four-dimensional Bloch ball, the hyperbit. The single-system postulates that pick out the Jordan state spaces allow such a model, and only energy observability rules it out. We show what it lacks, and realise it inside two qubits: its Kirkwood-Dirac imaginary parts are hidden $\mathfrak{so}(4)$ generators, its composites allow entanglement but no interaction, and its 24-cell of states stays Spekkens preparation contextual down to depolarising strength $2/3$. In fermionic terms, the four questions are the Majorana operators of two modes while the fifth is parity: the four-question system is therefore the sector removed by parity superselection.

</details>


### [103] [A Block Tensor Train Burer-Monteiro Framework for Low-Rank Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09457)
*Shakir Showkat Sofi,Charlotte Vermeylen,Fatemeh Mohammadi,Lieven De Lathauwer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于块张量列(Block-TT)分解的混合态量子态层析框架，将密度矩阵参数化为Block-TT与其共轭转置的收缩，保证厄米性与半正定性，并把优化变量从指数级降至线性级；发展单点/两点DMRG算法支持自适应秩提升，在有限测量下实现高精度重建并显著降低内存与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析是评估量子器件性能的关键技术，但标准估计方法因密度矩阵随量子比特数指数增长而计算代价过高，难以扩展到大规模系统，因此需要能够在压缩参数化下高效估计量子态的方法。

Method: 采用块张量列(Block-TT)分解：将密度矩阵表示为Block-TT与其厄米转置的收缩，形成Burer-Monteiro因式分解的张量列类比，从而在构造上保证厄米性与半正定性，并把优化变量由指数级压缩为线性级。基于该表示，开发单点与两点密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)算法，直接在压缩参数空间上优化，支持自适应秩细化，并利用高效张量网络收缩计算期望值。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够在有限测量数据下准确重建量子态，与常规低秩层析方法相比，在内存需求和计算成本上均有显著降低，适用于纯态、近纯态及可由张量网络精确近似的基态等一大类低秩量子态。

Conclusion: Block-TT因式分解结合DMRG为混合态量子态层析提供了一个可扩展、保结构的高效框架，在压缩参数化下兼顾精度与效率，为大规模量子系统的态估计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography is a fundamental technique for estimating the state of a quantum system from measured data and plays a crucial role in evaluating the performance of quantum devices. However, standard estimation methods become computationally prohibitive as the system size increases due to the exponential growth of the density matrix, describing a quantum state, with the number of qubits. We propose a low-rank tensor-network framework for mixed-state quantum state tomography based on a block tensor train (Block-TT) factorization. Specifically, the density matrix is represented as the contraction of a Block-TT with its Hermitian transpose, yielding a TT analogue of the Burer-Monteiro factorization. This parameterization guarantees Hermiticity and positive semidefiniteness by construction while compressing the number of optimization variables from exponential to linear in the number of qubits. Building on this representation, we develop single-site and two-site density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithms for estimating quantum states from compressed measurements. The resulting methods operate directly on the compressed parameterization, support adaptive rank refinement, and exploit efficient tensor-network contractions for expectation-value evaluation. The framework is applicable to a broad class of low-rank quantum states, including pure states, nearly pure states, and ground states that admit accurate tensor-network approximations. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate state reconstruction from limited measurements together with substantial reductions in memory requirements and computational cost compared with conventional low-rank tomography methods.

</details>


### [104] [Noise Limits on Fault-Tolerant Fermionic Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09467)
*Owen Allison,Luke Coffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对费米子量子计算，利用matchgate加非高斯资源门这一通用门集，求得局域退极化噪声下的容错上限约为62%，且该上限与电路深度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定量子电路能承受的最大噪声量是容错量子计算发展的关键任务。已有工作用通用Clifford+T门集将局域退极化噪声上限约束在约45%（以门集失去通用性为阈值），而费米子量子计算中对应的噪声上限尚待确定。

Method: 采用与Clifford+T类似的方法，针对matchgate加非高斯资源门的通用门集，利用Uhlmann-Wootters concurrence计算包含资源门的4模费米子Choi态在局域退极化噪声下的性质，判断合成信道何时成为凸高斯信道，从而得到噪声阈值。

Result: 得到费米子量子计算的噪声上限为p=(8-2√6)/5，约为62%，且该上限与电路深度无关。由于噪声模型不同，该结果与Clifford+T的约45%不能直接比较，但约62%是费米子体系已知的最佳界限。

Conclusion: 借助非高斯资源门的通用性判据，费米子量子计算在局域退极化噪声下可容忍约62%的噪声，且与电路深度无关；该界限为费米子容错量子计算提供了目前最优的噪声上限参考。

Abstract: Determining the highest amount of noise that quantum circuits can handle is an interesting and crucial task in the development of fault-tolerant quantum computation. Previous work has constrained this upper limit for local depolarizing noise to $\approx 45~\%$ for circuits constructed using the universal Clifford with T gate set by finding the noise threshold where the gate set loses universality. In this work, we use a similar method with the matchgate with non-Gaussian resource universal gate set to find a limit of $p=(8 - 2 \sqrt{6})/5$ or $\approx 62~\%$ for fermionic quantum computing that is independent of circuit depth. To do this, we use Uhlmann-Wootters concurrences for a 4-mode fermionic Choi state representing a resourceful gate combined with local depolarizing noise to determine when the combined channel is convex Gaussian. These bounds are not directly comparable due to differences in noise model making the bound of $\approx 62~\%$ the best known for fermions.

</details>


### [105] [Certifying bipartite entanglement on a superconducting processor from a corrected QAOA cost layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09495)
*Carlos-Miguel Lorenzo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在BSC的9比特超导处理器Red（5比特子组件）上完成预注册单设备研究：以双比特tomographic negativity在depth-1认证QAOA代价层的真正纠缠，并直接证明CZ脉冲幅度对纠缠的因果调控；depth-3处证书崩塌且无优化信号，不主张任何优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA是典型的近期启发式算法，但在真实超导硬件上其行为由器件物理而非算法本身决定；硬件演示很少证明实现了预期耦合、制备了真正的纠缠，或揭示其控制因素。因此作者希望以预注册的严格协议在真实器件上验证纠缠的真实性及其物理控制机制。

Method: 在巴塞罗那超级计算中心的9比特超导处理器Red的5比特组件上开展预注册单设备研究（6次校准）。用双比特tomographic negativity认证depth-1校正后QAOA代价层在"好耦合器"上的二体纠缠；设置耦合关闭的孪生对照与退化耦合器对照。随后在固定Bell态制备（非QAOA层）上做预注册操纵，扫描CZ脉冲幅度以检验因果性。辅助手段包括：条件相位|zeta|的峰值响应分析、J=0对照、条纹可见度、平均门保真度诊断、逐电路statevector护栏（排除并修复对角编译错误）、随机测量纠缠见证。最后检验depth-3的证书与16实例集成优化信号。

Result: depth-1在好耦合器上认证到非零二体纠缠：negativity 0.077，BCa 95% CI [0.065, 0.091]，每日均排除零；耦合关闭孪生保持可分，退化耦合器为零。预注册操纵显示降低CZ脉冲幅度沿正斜率因果性降低认证negativity（R^2=0.83–0.96），各级区间均排除零并通过Holm与Benjamini-Hochberg校正。否定性在条件相位|zeta|=pi处达峰，标称幅度存在过冲，属峰值响应的局部线性化而非全局单调律；J=0孪生为独立关闭态对照，条纹可见度恒定，表明效应是相干的。平均门保真度随驱动非单调，仅作诊断。随机测量见证与纠缠一致但统计功效不足。depth-3证书崩塌，预注册16实例depth-3集成无优化信号（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.15）。

Conclusion: 在真实超导硬件上，QAOA代价层的纠缠可被严格认证，且其强弱由CZ脉冲幅度等器件物理因果性控制，而该控制在标称点附近处于峰值响应的过冲区。纠缠证书仅在浅深度成立，depth-3处失去可证纠缠且未观察到优化信号，实例本身经典可解，故作者不主张任何量子优势。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimisation Algorithm is the canonical near-term heuristic, yet on real superconducting hardware its behaviour is set by device physics, not by the algorithm. Hardware demonstrations rarely prove the intended coupling is realised, that genuine entanglement is prepared, or what controls it. We report a pre-registered single-device study on the nine-qubit superconducting processor Red at the Barcelona Supercomputing Center, using its five-qubit component. Across six calibrations we certify bipartite entanglement of a corrected QAOA cost layer on the good coupler at depth one by two-qubit tomographic negativity: 0.077, BCa 95% CI [0.065, 0.091], excluding zero every day, while the coupling-off twin stays separable and the degraded coupler equals zero. We then measure the device physics directly: in a pre-registered manipulation on a fixed Bell preparation (not the QAOA layer), reducing the CZ pulse amplitude causally reduces the certified negativity along a positive, well-fit slope (R^2 = 0.83-0.96), every rung-level interval excluding zero and surviving Holm and Benjamini-Hochberg correction. This is a local linearisation of a peaked response, not a global monotone law: the negativity peaks at conditional phase |zeta| = pi, which the nominal amplitude overshoots. The J=0 twin is a separate off-state control and the fringe visibility is constant, so the effect is coherent. The average gate fidelity is non-monotone in the drive and is reported only as a diagnostic. A per-circuit statevector guardrail excludes a diagonal compilation fault we disclose and repair. A randomized-measurement witness is consistent with entanglement but underpowered. The certificate collapses by depth three, and a pre-registered sixteen-instance depth-three ensemble shows no optimisation signal (one-sided Wilcoxon p = 0.15). We claim no advantage; the instances are classically trivial.

</details>


### [106] [From Certifying Rank $k$ Projectors To Non-Positivity, Entanglement, And Non-Hermitian Witnesses Through Traces Of Matrix Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09557)
*H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究用矩阵幂的迹来判定厄米矩阵全体特征值是否属于指定集合的充要条件，并将这些刻画自动转化为纠缠见证、负特征值见证及非厄米性见证，结合数值模拟与解析分析评估其性能、强弱与资源需求，并讨论数值稳定性、测量不确定性与舍入误差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度矩阵纯度判据 Tr(ρ²)=1 只是特例，作者希望回答更一般的问题：能否、以及如何仅通过幂的迹来保证厄米矩阵的所有特征值落在某一给定集合中，并把这些刻画用作可操作的量子信息与矩阵性质见证。

Method: 对"迹幂条件"给出必要且充分的刻画；将这些刻画直接构造为见证量，用以证明量子态纠缠、厄米算子至少有一个负特征值、线性算子非厄米；并通过数值模拟与解析工作分析其效力、性能、强弱与资源需求，同时考察数值稳定性、测量不确定性及舍入误差的影响。

Result: 建立了基于幂迹保证所有特征值属于指定集合的充要条件；这些条件可自动作为纠缠、负特征值与非厄米性的见证；通过数值与解析手段给出这些见证的有效性、性能、优缺点及资源需求的评估，并分析了数值稳定性、测量不确定性和舍入误差的作用。

Conclusion: 幂迹特征值判定具有一般性的充要刻画，并能统一生成多类见证；其在实际测量与数值环境下可行但有资源与稳定性约束，需兼顾测量不确定性与舍入误差。

Abstract: We all know that a density matrix $ρ$ is pure if and only if $\mathrm{Tr} \,ρ^2 = 1$. But is this the only way to prove the purity of $ρ$ using the trace of its powers? Here I systematically study the necessary and sufficient conditions of the more general question of guaranteeing that all eigenvalues of a Hermitian matrix belong to a specified set through its trace of powers. More importantly, these characterization results are automatically witnesses certifying a quantum state as entangled, a Hermitian operator has least one negative eigenvalue and a linear operator as non-Hermitian. I demonstrate the effectiveness of these witnesses, analyze their performance, and study their strength, weakness together with resource requirement through numerical simulation as well as analytical work. I also discuss briefly the effects of numerical stability, uncertainty in measurement and rounding errors on these problems.

</details>


### [107] [ECDSA.Fail: Open Autoresearch for Optimizing Elliptic-Curve Point Addition in Shor's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09582)
*Jieyi Long,Theodore Pender,Zhao Huang,Manuel B. Santos,Samrendra Kumar Singh,Bartosz Naskręcki,Bit Wonka,Joe Doyle,Pierre-Luc Dallaire-Demers,Francesco Giannicola,Ruben M. L. Paschoarelli,Oli Freuler,Jackie Chia-Hsun Lee,Vasily Gnuchev,Gopi Kannappan,John Boyer,Xavier Butler,Akash Balasubramani,Jordan Newman,Bereket Dereje,Alexander Hertlein,Robert Kodra,Lucas Levy,Shaan Patel,JT Rose,Matt Zweil,Okechukwu Wisdom,Tarek El-Eter,Edison Lee,Michael Dong,Alan Li,Anto Joseph,Gajesh Naik,Gautham Anant,Soubhik Deb,Justin Drake*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"开放自动研究"(Open Autoresearch)范式：人类与AI智能体向公共排行榜发布经评估器验证的改进。在ECDSA.Fail平台上实例化，优化可逆secp256k1点加电路(Shor算法瓶颈)，以时空启发式指标S=Q×T(峰值逻辑量子比特数×平均执行Toffoli数)为目标，参与者将S降低86.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆secp256k1点加电路是Shor算法破解椭圆曲线密码学的瓶颈。希望通过开放、可机器校验的目标，让人类与AI智能体协作共同推进该基准的优化，验证"开放自动研究"范式的可行性。

Method: 构建公共排行榜基准ECDSA.Fail，以S=Q×T为评分函数(时空权衡启发)。此外构建一个兼容窗口化加法的变体，实现窗口化Shor所需的单调用接口，在10万个随机输入上测试经验成功率。

Result: 截至2026年7月26日，最佳电路使用1,151个量子比特、1,299,453个平均执行Toffoli门，Q×T≈14.96亿，比Google公布的阈值低50%以上(口径不同)。窗口化兼容变体使用1,162量子比特、1,684,161 Toffoli门，经验成功率p̂=0.99809，Q×T/p̂≈19.61亿。截稿后S进一步降至12.59亿，另有低宽度电路达到813量子比特。

Conclusion: 公共记录显示AI智能体可与人类判断互补，为在可高效评估、可机器校验的目标上开展开放自动研究提供了证据。但由于接口、计账口径与验证范围不同，尚不能断言正式优势。

Abstract: We propose Open Autoresearch, a paradigm in which humans and AI agents publish evaluator-verified improvements to a public leaderboard. We instantiate it in ECDSA.Fail, optimizing reversible secp256k1 point-addition circuits, a bottleneck in Shor's algorithm for elliptic-curve cryptography. The benchmark minimizes the spacetime-inspired score $S=Q\times T$, where $Q$ is peak logical qubit width and $T$ is average executed Toffoli count. Participants reduced $S$ by 86.1%. At the data cutoff (26 July 2026), the best-scoring circuit uses 1,151 qubits and 1,299,453 average executed Toffoli gates, giving $Q\times T\approx1.496$ billion. This is more than 50% below Google's published point-addition score thresholds (arXiv:2603.28846), under different accounting conventions. Because the benchmark supplies one addend classically, we construct a coherent windowed-addition-compatible variant implementing the single-call interface required by windowed Shor. It uses 1,162 qubits and 1,684,161 average executed Toffoli gates. On 100,000 random inputs, its empirical success probability is $\hat{p}=0.99809$, giving $Q\times T/\hat{p}\approx1.961$ billion under an independently rerunnable per-call sensitivity model, not a full-Shor success estimate. Its qubit and Toffoli counts lie below Google's published thresholds and Schrottenloher's reported operating points (arXiv:2606.02235), although differing interfaces, accounting conventions, and validation scope preclude formal dominance. After the cutoff, the score was further reduced to 1.259 billion, while a separate low-width circuit reached 813 qubits. The public record shows AI agents complementing human judgment, providing evidence for open autoresearch on efficiently evaluable, machine-checkable objectives.

</details>


### [108] [Information-geometric bounds on nonequilibrium relaxation in quantum Markov dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09599)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子马尔可夫动力学中短时非平衡弛豫的信息几何结构，推导了短时弛豫曲率非平衡修正的上界，将Auconi经典不等式推广至量子情形，并在多个模型中数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Auconi关于经典非平衡弛豫的信息几何不等式推广到量子马尔可夫动力学，刻画短时非平衡弛豫曲率的非平衡修正及其界限。

Method: 以von Neumann相对熵为量子散度，通过Kubo-Mori映射参数化微扰，将相对熵的二阶展开转化为BKM内积；对具满秩稳态的量子马尔可夫半群，将切空间生成元分解为BKM对称与反对称部分，导出领头非平衡曲率修正为耗散与输运生成元对易子的期望，并用BKM对称耗散活动度与输运活动度之积予以界定。

Result: 得到领头非平衡曲率修正的精确表达式及其上界（耗散活动度与输运活动度之积）；在非对易量子比特模型和驱动耗散qutrit上验证该界，并在二维Fokker-Planck稳态上数值确认整条推导链；高温过阻尼极限下量子公式退化为位置空间Fokker-Planck表达式，并复现Auconi熵产生界及正确的温度因子。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子马尔可夫动力学短时非平衡弛豫的信息几何框架，给出可计算的曲率修正上界，并证明其在经典极限下与Auconi结果一致，为量子非平衡热力学提供了几何化工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the quantum information-geometric structure underlying short-time nonequilibrium relaxation in quantum Markov dynamics, and derive bounds on the nonequilibrium correction to the short-time relaxation curvature. These bounds generalize the information-geometric structure underlying Auconi's classical nonequilibrium relaxation inequality to quantum Markov dynamics. We consider the von Neumann relative entropy as quantum divergence, and parametrize perturbations through the Kubo-Mori map, which converts the second-order expansion of the relative entropy into the Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori (BKM) inner product at all temperatures. For a quantum Markov semigroup with full-rank stationary state, the induced tangent-space generator admits a canonical decomposition into a BKM-symmetric and a BKM-antisymmetric part, and the leading nonequilibrium curvature correction takes the exact form of the expectation of the commutator between the dissipative and the transport part of the generator. This correction is bounded by the product of a BKM-symmetric dissipative activity and a transport-sector activity. The bound is verified on a noncommuting qubit model and a driven-dissipative qutrit, and the full chain is confirmed numerically on a two-dimensional Fokker-Planck steady state. In the high-temperature and overdamped limit the quantum formula reduces to the position-space Fokker-Planck expression and reproduces Auconi's entropy-production bound with the correct temperature factor.

</details>


### [109] [A Sum-of-Squares Hierarchy with Quadratic Convergence for Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09629)
*Hoang Ta,Hoang Anh Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子信道单次使用下经典信息传输最优成功概率的 NP-hard 问题，构造了适用于任意消息数的 Hermitian 平方和（SOS）层级，并证明其收敛速度为二次（关于层级），误差上界与相对随机猜测的优势成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次量子信道传输经典消息的最优成功概率计算是 NP-hard 问题（即使仅两条消息）。已有的基于对称扩张的半定规划层级虽能给出收敛上界，但其误差估计仅以层级数的平方根倒数衰减（O(1/√k)），收敛较慢，亟需更高效的逼近方法。

Method: 结合态分辨（state-discrimination）对偶性与球面乘积上的正多项式核，构造可行的多项式对偶证书，从而建立一个适用于任意消息数的 Hermitian 平方和（SOS）层级。

Result: 证明了该 Hermitian SOS 层级关于层级数具有二次收敛速率（误差 ∝ 1/k^2 量级），误差上界正比于相对随机猜测的优势；对于二消息情形，所得上界给出了迹范数收缩系数（trace-norm contraction coefficient）的乘性上逼近。

Conclusion: Hermitian SOS 层级显著改进了现有对称扩张层级的收敛速率（从 O(1/√k) 提升至二次收敛），为量子信道容量与态分辨问题的可计算上界提供了更紧、更高效的逼近框架。

Abstract: Computing the optimal success probability for transmitting classical messages through a single use of a quantum channel is NP-hard, even for two messages. An existing semidefinite programming hierarchy based on symmetric extensions provides convergent upper bounds with an a priori error estimate that decays as the inverse square root of the extension level. In this work, we construct a Hermitian sum-of-squares hierarchy for an arbitrary number of messages and prove quadratic convergence in its level. The error bound is proportional to the advantage over random guessing. Our approach combines state-discrimination duality with positive polynomial kernels on products of spheres to construct feasible polynomial dual certificates. For binary messages, the resulting bounds give a multiplicative approximation from above of the trace-norm contraction coefficient.

</details>


### [110] [Perfect State Transfer from a Localised Two-Excitation State to a Dicke State via Static Spin-Network Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09654)
*Soumyojyoti Dutta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一类与时间无关、保持激发数的自旋哈密顿量，可在任意系统尺寸 N≥4 下实现从局域双激发态到对称双激发 Dicke 态的完美态传输，并给出纯符号化的存在性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在具有物理约束（保持激发数的自旋网络形式）下的完美态传输问题，区别于一般无约束实对称矩阵可实现的情形，探讨受限条件下双激发态的完美传输是否可行。

Method: 利用作用在初始未占据自旋上的 S_{N-2} 置换对称性，将动力学约化到四维不变子空间；要求 (|ψ_0⟩+|D_N^{(2)}⟩)/2 为零本征矢，闭式确定在位能并保留三个耦合参数；将逆谱问题化为两个多项式方程，消元后得到六次互反多项式，经 z=x+x^{-1} 化为三次方程；对谱族 (-n,-1,1)（n 为奇整数）通过首项系数分解与 z=-2 边界求值证明存在 z<-2 的实根，并用子结式论证给出原系统的实提升。

Result: 对每个 N≥4，均可选择足够大的奇整数 n，使存在满足条件的实根并提升到原系统，从而在物理自旋网络形式下实现完美态传输；证明完全符号化，不依赖数值优化，直接传播仅用于独立验证。

Conclusion: 该结果是完美态传输的一个受约束类比：在要求哈密顿量来自保持激发的自旋网络形式这一物理限制下，仍可对任意 N≥4 实现双激发完美态传输。

Abstract: I construct a family of time-independent, excitation-preserving spin Hamiltonians that realises perfect state transfer from a localised two-excitation state to the symmetric two-excitation Dicke state, for every system size $N \ge 4$. The Hamiltonian has the physical form $H = \sum_{i<j} J_{ij} (σ_i^+ σ_j^- + σ_j^+ σ_i^-) + \sum_i ε_i n_i$ with real couplings, and satisfies $e^{-iHt} |110\cdots0\rangle = e^{iφ} |D_N^{(2)}\rangle$ at a finite time. The construction exploits an $S_{N-2}$ permutation symmetry acting on the initially unoccupied spins, which reduces the dynamics to a four-dimensional invariant subspace. Requiring $(|ψ_0\rangle + |D_N^{(2)}\rangle)/2$ to be a zero eigenvector determines the on-site energies in closed form and leaves three coupling parameters free. The remaining inverse spectral problem reduces to two polynomial equations in two dimensionless coupling ratios; eliminating one ratio yields a degree-six reciprocal polynomial, which the substitution $z = x + x^{-1}$ converts to a cubic. For the spectral family $(-n,-1,1)$, with $n$ an odd integer, an explicit factorisation of the leading coefficient together with a boundary evaluation at $z=-2$ shows that for every $N \ge 4$ an odd $n$ can be chosen large enough that a real root with $z < -2$ exists; a subresultant argument supplies a real lift of that root to the original system. The existence proof is entirely symbolic and does not rely on numerical optimisation; direct propagation is used only as independent validation. The result is a constrained analogue of perfect state transfer: unlike the general real-state problem, where an unconstrained real symmetric matrix suffices, here the Hamiltonian is required to arise from an excitation-preserving spin-network form.

</details>


### [111] [Realization Theory for Quantum Filtering: Classifying Continuously Monitored Bosonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09663)
*Jacob Emerson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将连续监测量子系统的精确有限维滤波问题转化为实现问题，引入线性化可观测量Hankel算子$K_O$，对连续正交监测下的多项式玻色子系统给出了有限可实现的精确边界，并进一步将框架扩展到最优有限维逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少数连续监测量子系统存在精确有限维描述，但缺乏区分"可实现"与"不可实现"系统的一般判据，本文旨在建立这一判据并刻画精确量子滤波的边界。

Method: 将问题形式化为实现问题，引入线性化可观测量Hankel算子$K_O$（将过去测量记录的变化映射为未来条件可观测量的变化），通过其奇异值分析实现性与逼近误差。

Result: 对连续正交监测下的多项式玻色子系统，得到有限与无限可实现的精确边界：高斯动力学与条件动量矩类存在精确有限维滤波器，而这些类之外的非线性动力学一般产生无穷多独立响应方向，不存在稳健的有限维$C^1$可观测量级或状态级滤波器。$K_O$的奇异值给出超出有限类的最优秩-$d$局域响应误差。Kerr与Duffing振子的数值实验及针对Kerr动力学的Poisson–Charlier构造验证了该基准。

Conclusion: 这些结果界定了精确量子滤波的边界，并量化了其外的有限维逼近。

Abstract: Exact finite-dimensional descriptions exist for a few important classes of continuously monitored quantum systems, but there is no general criterion separating systems that admit such filters from those that do not. We formulate this question as a realization problem and introduce the linearized observable Hankel operator $K_O$, which maps changes in the past measurement record to changes in a future conditional observable. For polynomial bosonic systems under continuous quadrature monitoring, this gives a sharp boundary between finite and infinite realizability. Gaussian dynamics and the Conditional Momentum Moment class admit exact finite-dimensional filters, while nonlinear dynamics outside these classes generically produce infinitely many independent response directions and admit no robust finite-dimensional $C^1$ observable- or state-level filter. The singular values of $K_O$ then extend the same realization framework to approximation, giving the optimal rank-$d$ local-response error beyond the finite classes. Numerical experiments with the Kerr and Duffing oscillators, together with a dynamics-adapted Poisson--Charlier construction for Kerr dynamics, show how this observable-level benchmark interacts with concrete reduced representations. Together, these results identify the boundary of exact quantum filtering and quantify finite-dimensional approximation beyond it.

</details>


### [112] [Enhancing Pauli Correlation Encoding for quantum optimization via systematic expressivity analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09718)
*Riku Usuki,Don Arai,Ken N. Okada,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Quantum approaches for combinatorial optimization problems have attracted considerable attention in recent years. Among these approaches, Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) has emerged as a promising framework for quantum devices with limited qubit resources because it embeds optimization variables in expectation values of Pauli strings. However, the mechanisms underlying its performance and the reasons for its saturation remain unclear. In this work, we investigate these questions through a systematic analysis of expressivity and trainability. First, we compare PCE with classical surrogate models based on tensor networks whose structures progressively approach the topology of the PCE circuit. The results show that PCE attains comparable solution quality with substantially fewer trainable parameters, indicating strong parameter efficiency. Second, to determine whether the performance saturation of conventional PCE is caused by insufficient expressivity or by optimization difficulty, we perform a diagnostic expressivity test in which the circuit is trained toward reference configurations for Max-Cut. The results show that even shallow PCE circuits can represent strong solutions, indicating that the main bottleneck is not the representational power of the ansatz, but the trainability under the relaxed objective function. Motivated by this finding, we propose a multistage continuation framework that gradually transforms a smooth relaxed objective into a sharper objective that more closely approximates the target discrete problem. Numerical experiments on G-set instances with 800 vertices show that the proposed method consistently outperforms conventional PCE and is competitive with representative graph neural network (GNN) methods. These results clarify the main factors behind PCE performance and provide a practical strategy for improving PCE on quantum devices with limited qubit resources.

</details>


### [113] [Generalized Fidelity and the Data Processing Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09753)
*Reza Rajaei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明广义Bures–Wasserstein距离（由广义保真度诱导）在维数大于2时不满足数据处理不等式，并给出维数2时该不等式成立的两个充分条件及广义保真度退化为Uhlmann保真度的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究广义Bures–Wasserstein距离是否满足数据处理不等式（DPI），并厘清广义保真度与Uhlmann保真度之间的关系。

Method: 对维数大于2的情形构造反例以否定DPI；对二维情形利用广义保真度实部的显式公式推导DPI成立的两个充分条件；并分析广义保真度退化为Uhlmann保真度的条件。

Result: 维数大于2时广义Bures–Wasserstein距离不满足DPI；二维情形下获得保证DPI成立的两个充分条件；给出了广义保真度退化为Uhlmann保真度的充要条件。

Conclusion: 广义Bures–Wasserstein距离的DPI在一般维数下不成立，但在二维特定条件下可成立，且广义保真度与Uhlmann保真度的等价性有明确判据。

Abstract: In this note, we show that the generalized Bures--Wasserstein distance induced by the generalized fidelity does not satisfy the data processing inequality in dimensions greater than two. In dimension two, although we do not settle the DPI in full generality, we derive two sufficient conditions guaranteeing it by using an explicit formula for the real part of the generalized fidelity. Furthermore, we find a necessary and sufficient condition under which the generalized fidelity reduces to the Uhlmann fidelity.

</details>


### [114] [Split-Step Dirac Cellular Automata for Continuous-Time Dirac Dynamics on Finite Spatial Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09779)
*Wei-Ting Wang,Pei-Ming Ho,Ching-Ray Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出分裂步Dirac元胞自动机（SDCA），通过动量表象下的Trotter化分数步方案，在固定空间离散化下实现连续时间Dirac演化，打破标准DCA的相位匹配相消，恢复Zitterbewegung等干涉动力学，并在IBM量子处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Dirac元胞自动机（DCA）中空间与时间分辨率刚性耦合，在有限网格上引入人为的相位匹配对称性，从而抑制Zitterbewegung等干涉现象，限制了Dirac动力学的准确模拟。

Method: 提出分裂步Dirac元胞自动机（SDCA）：在动量表象中采用Trotter化的分数步（fractional-step）方案，使时间步与固定空间离散化解耦，从而打破标准DCA中的相位匹配相消；通过解析与数值方法进行基准测试，并在IBM量子处理器上实现，同时考察硬件拓扑权衡以及QFT的动态电路实现。

Result: SDCA恢复了连续时间极限下的干涉动力学；尽管更细时间分辨率增加了电路深度，NISQ实现仍能重现连续时间模型的特征速度振荡与纠缠熵动力学；并揭示了门错误、测量与feed-forward延迟之间的竞争效应。

Conclusion: SDCA为在保持固定空间量子寄存器的情况下提升时间分辨率提供了实用框架，使相对论量子动力学在近期量子器件上的探索成为可能。

Abstract: Dirac Cellular Automata (DCA) provide a framework for simulating Dirac dynamics, yet the rigid coupling between spatial and temporal resolutions can introduce artificial phase-matching symmetries on finite grids that suppress interference phenomena such as Zitterbewegung. In this work, we propose a Split-Step Dirac Cellular Automaton (SDCA) that enables continuous-time Dirac evolution at fixed spatial discretization. By employing a Trotterized fractional-step scheme in the momentum representation, SDCA breaks the phase-matching cancellation present in the standard DCA and recovers the interference dynamics of the continuous-time limit. We benchmark the SDCA through analytical and numerical studies and demonstrate its implementation on IBM Quantum processors. Despite the increased circuit depth required for finer temporal resolution, the NISQ implementation reproduces the characteristic velocity oscillations and entanglement-entropy dynamics of the continuous-time model. We further investigate hardware-topology trade-offs and dynamic circuit implementations of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), highlighting the competing effects of gate errors, measurement, and feed-forward latency. These results demonstrate that SDCA provides a practical framework for improving temporal resolution while maintaining a fixed spatial quantum register, enabling the exploration of relativistic quantum dynamics on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [115] [Small-Bias Quantum Approximate Counting via the Multiplicative Adversary Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09804)
*Albert Lin,Han-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用乘性对手方法（multiplicative adversary method）研究量子近似计数双权重判定问题，证明查询下界 Ω(max{ζ√((N−M)(M+Δ))/Δ, √(ζN/Δ)})，与多项式方法刻画一致；核心贡献是通过逐查询追踪进展的乘性对手推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子近似计数的双权重判定：给定 x∈{0,1}^N 的 oracle，需以成功概率 1/2+ζ 区分 |x|=M 与 |x|=M+Δ。已知该参数依赖可由 Podder、Yao、Ye 对双层对称函数的多项式方法刻画得到，但缺少一种能逐查询追踪进展的对手方法推导；本文旨在给出这样的乘性对手证明。

Method: 采用乘性对手方法。第一项：必要时取补，设 M+Δ≤N−M；使用 Ambainis、Spalek、de Wolf 特征空间方法中的 Hamming 层子空间，复合相邻层酉映射以关联两个非相邻承诺层；固定被查询坐标后分析块对角化为四维子空间，精确计算单查询进展比得到第一下界，并推出相干输入叠加下 ‖(I−Π̂_bad)|Ψ^T⟩‖²=O(T²Δ²/((N−M)(M+Δ)))。第二项：用三特征值乘性对手直接证明 n 比特 unique OR 以概率 1/2+ζ 需 Ω(√(ζn)) 查询，再把 unique OR 归约到双权重计数问题。

Result: 得到查询下界 Ω(max{ζ√((N−M)(M+Δ))/Δ, √(ζN/Δ)})，参数依赖与多项式方法刻画一致；并给出相干输入叠加的坏子空间投影范数上界 O(T²Δ²/((N−M)(M+Δ)))。

Conclusion: 乘性对手方法可独立、逐查询地重现双层对称函数多项式刻画给出的下界，为量子近似计数双权重判定提供了新的对手式证明路径。

Abstract: We study the two-weight decision version of quantum approximate counting: given oracle access to $x\in\{0,1\}^N$, distinguish $|x|=M$ from $|x|=M+Δ$ with success probability $1/2+ζ$. Using the multiplicative adversary method, we prove $Ω\left(\max\left\{ζ\sqrt{(N-M)(M+Δ)}/Δ,\sqrt{ζN/Δ}\right\}\right)$. The same parameter dependence follows from the polynomial-method characterization of the two-layer symmetric function by Podder, Yao, and Ye. Our contribution is a multiplicative-adversary derivation that tracks the progress produced by individual oracle queries. For the first term, after complementing the input if necessary, we assume $M+Δ\le N-M$. We use the Hamming-layer subspaces from the eigenspace method of Ambainis, Spalek, and de Wolf and compose their adjacent-layer unitary maps to relate the two nonadjacent promise layers. After fixing the queried coordinate, the analysis block-diagonalizes into four-dimensional subspaces. An exact calculation of the one-query progress ratio gives the first lower bound. The same estimate also implies $\left\|(I-\widehatΠ_{\mathrm{bad}})\lvertΨ^T\rangle\right\|^2=O\left(T^2Δ^2/((N-M)(M+Δ))\right)$ for the coherent input superposition used in the adversary argument. For the second term, we prove directly using a three-eigenvalue multiplicative adversary that unique OR on $n$ bits with success probability $1/2+ζ$ requires $Ω(\sqrt{ζn})$ queries, and then reduce unique OR to the two-weight counting problem.

</details>


### [116] [Electrically tunable, two-photon interference from remote silicon-vacancy centers in industrial silicon carbide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09805)
*Fedor Dzmitryevich Hrunski,Daniel Scheller,Maximilian Hollendonner,Kim Ullerich,Shravan Kumar Parthasarathy,Chiun Fu,Andre Pointner,Wolfgang Knolle,Florian Kaiser,Durga Bhaktavatsala Rao Dasari,Roland Nagy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于工业级SiC p-i-n二极管中的硅空位色心，实现长时间稳定、高干涉可见度的双光子干涉，为分布式量子网络提供可规模化、光谱稳定的构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子网络依赖空间分离的独立量子节点，其发射光子需高可见度干涉以建立端到端纠缠。关键挑战在于：高可见度必须在长时间尺度上稳定维持，以降低纠错开销并提升网络速率，而这在可规模化器件中尚未实现。

Method: 将V_Si色心集成于不同工业级SiC p-i-n二极管中，通过电压偏置进行控制。测量19个随机选取色心的光谱重叠与谱线窄化（低于60 MHz，接近寿命极限），评估其长期稳定性；并在两个相距2米、位于不同低温恒温器中的色心之间开展双光子干涉测量。

Result: 实现19个随机选取V_Si色心的光谱重叠及接近寿命极限的谱线窄化（通常低于60 MHz）；性能长期稳定，仅需每8.4小时重新调节偏置。跨两个低温恒温器（相距2米）、持续26天的测量中，双光子干涉原始可见度达82%，与当前最先进水平相当。

Conclusion: 工业级SiC器件中的V_Si色心可作为可规模化、光谱稳定的构建模块，满足分布式量子网络对高可见度与长时间稳定性的要求。

Abstract: Distributed quantum networks rely on spatially separated, independently operated quantum systems as network nodes, whose emitted photons must be interfered with high visibility to establish end-to-end entanglement. Crucially, for network-relevant applications, high visibilities must be achieved over prolonged timescales to reduce overheads for error correction, and to increase network rates. Here, we demonstrate experimentally that silicon vacancy $\mathrm{V_{Si}}$ color centers in silicon carbide (SiC) achieve these requirements, notably in a mass-deployable fashion. We integrate $\mathrm{V_{Si}}$ centers in different industrial-grade SiC p-i-n diodes, which are controlled via voltage biassing. This way, we demonstrate both, spectral overlapping of 19 randomly selected $\mathrm{V_{Si}}$ centers in different diodes, as well as spectral narrowing close to the lifetime limit, i.e., typically below 60 MHz. Notably, these performance parameters are long-term stable, e.g., readjusting the p-i-n diode bias is required only every 8.4 hours, which reduces significantly the overall experimental overhead. We then use these assets to demonstrate high-quality two-photon interference between $\mathrm{V_{Si}}$ centers located in two different cryostat setups, which are spatially separated by two meters. Notably, we perform a 26-days long measurement campaign, demonstrating two-photon interference with state-of-the-art raw interference visibilities of 82%, which aligns with the current state-of-the-art. These results establish $\mathrm{V_{Si}}$ centers in industry-grade SiC devices as a scalable, spectrally stable building block for distributed quantum networks.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Observable Subspaces: Fast Simulation of Observable Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09806)
*Hannes Leipold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过将量子动力学在可观测量的李轨道上分解，当生成元的动态李代数（DLA）规模为多项式时，可实现多项式时间的经典模拟；提出动态可观测量子空间（DOS）构造方法，支持数百量子比特的闭动力学模拟，并揭示受限DOS电路因避免贫瘠高原而可优于全表达电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学模拟通常面临指数级计算复杂度，而现有经典模拟方法难以扩展到大尺度系统。作者希望利用生成元动态李代数的多项式结构，找到一条对特定可观测量期望值进行高效经典模拟的路径，并探索受限电路在变分量子算法中的潜在优势。

Method: 将量子动力学沿一组可观测量的李轨道（Lie orbits）分解；在生成元动态李代数（DLA）为多项式规模的前提下，构造动态可观测量子空间（DOS），以捕获计算特定可观测量（如Pauli串、diffusor mixer及一般局域生成元）期望值所需的全部相关动力学。采用高效稀疏矩阵表示，将非线性与表示解耦，并允许进行剪枝的基构造，从而模拟含数百量子比特的闭动力学。

Result: 可实现数百量子比特系统的闭动力学多项式时间经典模拟；受限DOS电路虽不能表达对应哈密顿量的整个空间，但因不存在贫瘠高原（Barren Plateau）而可显著优于全表达电路；此类电路仍可通过推断体现某种量子优势，并可作为更具表达力电路的暖启动，以及为量子振幅放大或量子相位估计寻找高质量试探态。

Conclusion: 在量子动力学模拟中，受限电路可在多项式时间内被模拟，同时为下游任务（如量子相位估计）生成高质量引导态，其能量显著低于全表达力电路。该框架为经典可模拟性与量子优势的结合提供了新途径。

Abstract: By decomposing quantum dynamics across the Lie orbits of a list of observables, we find polynomial bounded classical simulations for the dynamics of the quantum system given a polynomial sized dynamic Lie algebra (DLA) for the generators of the quantum system. To do so, we describe how to construct the Dynamic Observable Subspace (DOS) that captures all the relevant dynamics for calculating expectation values for a specific observable for Pauli strings, diffusor mixers, and general local generators. Efficient sparse matrix representation, decoupling nonlinearity from such representation and basis construction with permissible pruning allows us to simulate the closed dynamics of such systems with hundreds of qubits. Moreover, we find that while restricted DOS circuits may not express the space of an associated Hamiltonian, they can dramatically outperform a fully expressive circuit due to the absence of the Barren Plateau. While classically simulatable, such circuits can still exhibit a form of quantum advantage through inference, act as warm starting for more expressive circuits, and find high quality trial states for Quantum Amplitude Amplification or Quantum Phase Estimation. In the simulation of quantum dynamics, we find that our restricted circuit can be simulated in polynomial time while producing high quality guiding state for downstream tasks like Quantum Phase Estimation with significantly lower energy than circuits with full expressivity.

</details>


### [118] [Robust Device-Independent Certification of Boolean-Phase Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09836)
*Yunguang Han,Xingyuan Bu,Aleksandra Gočanin,Jiabing Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为布尔相位门（计算基对角门）提出鲁棒的自检验方案：通过布尔导数将相位信息转化为经典符号，用CHSH块构建Bell测试，实现Choi态与测量观测量的联合自检验，并给出任意局域维度下、对所有布尔函数一致的保真度仿射下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关的量子门认证要求输入与输出测试针对同一参考量子比特，仅对输出Choi态做自检验无法保证这一一致性；因此需要能同时自检验输出Choi态与所测观测量的鲁棒认证方案。

Method: 针对由布尔函数定义的一类布尔相位门，利用布尔导数把目标依赖的相位信息转换为可由局域测量结果评估的经典符号；以CHSH块构建Bell测试，每方仅需两个二值测量且不需要纠缠测量。结合恒等测试与门输出测试构成独立源网络，使参考设备使用相同物理可观测量；对CCZ进行六方联合分析以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在最大违背处，测试可自检验归一化Choi态及所测可观测量；偏离最大值时给出提取态平方保真度的显式仿射下界，该下界对所有布尔函数一致且在任意局域维度下成立。网络设置下得到有效n量子比特信道的闭式Choi保真度界；对CCZ在不改变Bell表达式与测量设置的情况下得到更强的鲁棒性界。

Conclusion: 该构造表明，门的代数结构可将目标依赖信息从量子测量设计转移到局域结果的经典后处理，从而实现一致且鲁棒的设备无关量子门认证。

Abstract: Device-independent certification of a quantum gate requires the input and output tests to identify the same reference qubits. Self-testing the output Choi state alone does not guarantee this consistency. We develop a robust certification scheme for Boolean-phase gates, a broad family of computational-basis diagonal gates specified by Boolean functions. Boolean derivatives convert the target-dependent phase information into classical signs that can be evaluated from local measurement outcomes. This leads to Bell tests built from CHSH blocks using two binary measurements per party and no entangling measurements. At maximal violation, the tests self-test the normalized Choi state and the measured observables. Away from the maximum, they give an explicit affine lower bound on the extracted-state squared fidelity that is uniform over all Boolean functions and valid in arbitrary local dimensions. We then combine an identity test and a gate-output test in an independent-source network in which the reference devices use the same physical observables, obtaining a closed-form Choi-fidelity bound for an effective \(n\)-qubit channel. For CCZ, a joint six-party analysis gives a stronger robustness bound without changing the Bell expression or measurement settings. The construction shows how the algebraic structure of a gate can shift target-dependent information from quantum measurement design to classical processing of local outcomes.

</details>


### [119] [Minimax games for quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09839)
*Kun Fang,Michael X. Cao,Hao-Chung Cheng,Li Gao,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个博弈论框架研究量子信道区分，其中测试者与干扰者分别控制输入，结合三种输入结构与四种信息模式，构建十二种博弈模型，给出有限块长假设检验的精确刻画与渐近Stein指数，并发展了一种将可达性升级为强逆结论的通用论证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子信道区分研究主要分别考虑最优情形（最佳测试者输入）或最差情形（最差干扰者输入），缺乏同时刻画测试者与干扰者相互竞争的博弈视角，因此需要建立一个统一框架来系统研究双方各自控制输入时的信道区分性能。

Method: 构建博弈论框架，令测试者与干扰者分别控制不同输入；依据测试者与干扰者是否使用纠缠输入或跨信道使用的IID输入划分三种输入结构，依据干扰者策略可见性及其对真实假设的知识划分四种信息模式，组合得到十二种博弈模型。用九种极小极大假设检验散度对全部十二种模型进行精确有限块长刻画，并推导其渐近Stein指数；进一步发展了一种将可达性结果升级为强逆结果的通用论证。

Result: 对所有十二种模型给出有限块长假设检验的精确刻画及渐近Stein指数。发现对纠缠干扰者而言，干扰者策略可见性与是否知晓真实假设均不影响渐近Stein指数，而信息模式对IID干扰者仍然重要。以一般信道与替换信道的区分为例，证明所有渐近Stein指数均等于同一个可加的单字母量。此外，所提论证为若干博弈模型建立了强逆性质，解决了Berta等人（Commun. Math. Phys. 385, 55 (2021)）提出的复合假设检验开放问题，并加强了Lami（arXiv:2510.06340）的若干近期结果。

Conclusion: 该博弈论框架与相关技术为涉及竞争角色的量子信息任务研究提供了统一工具，明确了不同输入结构与信息模式对信道区分性能的影响，并推广了强逆结论及复合假设检验的相关结果。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination is a primitive task for identifying, verifying, and benchmarking quantum dynamics. Previous studies have primarily considered either the best-case tester-input setting or the worst-case jammer-input setting. Here, we introduce a game-theoretic framework in which both the tester and jammer control separate inputs. Combining three input structures, characterized by whether the tester and jammer use entangled inputs or IID inputs across channel uses, with four information patterns, determined by the visibility of the jammer's strategy and its knowledge of the true hypothesis, yields twelve game models. We provide exact finite blocklength hypothesis testing characterizations of all twelve models in terms of nine minimax hypothesis testing divergences and derive their asymptotic Stein exponents. Notably, for entangled jammers, neither the visibility of the jammer's strategy nor its knowledge of the true hypothesis affects the asymptotic Stein exponent, whereas the information pattern remains consequential for IID jammers. As an example, we study the discrimination of a general channel from a replacer channel and show that all asymptotic Stein exponents coincide with the same additive, single letter quantity. We further develop a general argument that upgrades achievability results to strong converse results, thereby establishing strong converse properties for several game models, resolving an open problem in composite hypothesis testing posed by Berta et al. [Commun. Math. Phys. 385, 55 (2021)], and strengthening several recent results of Lami [arXiv:2510.06340]. The framework and techniques developed here may support future studies of quantum information tasks involving competing roles.

</details>


### [120] [Simple QKD keyrate computations from tangent-line bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09847)
*Jun Hui Goh,Ernest Y. -Z. Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用现成求解器（通过构造恰当切线对目标函数下界项进行线性化）计算基础QKD协议（如qubit BB84）的有限码长密钥率，无需此前复杂的凸优化框架；并将方法扩展到六态协议，给出单轮Rényi熵的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年基于熵积累框架的QKD有限码长密钥率归结为凸优化问题，虽已有多种求解器，但为兼容广泛协议而依赖繁复框架与复杂算法。作者希望说明：对BB84等基础协议可借助商用/现成求解器更简便地计算密钥率，并推动六态协议在熵积累下的研究。

Method: 对基础QKD协议，通过对目标函数中的下界项求取合适的切线（tangent lines）实现线性下界，从而将优化问题化为可用off-the-shelf求解器处理的形式；进一步针对六态协议推导单轮Rényi熵的闭式表达式。

Result: 计算出相应的有限码长密钥率，并与先前工作进行对比；对六态协议得到了单轮Rényi熵的闭式表达。

Conclusion: 对BB84等基本协议，有限码长密钥率可用现成求解器通过切线下界简便计算；六态协议的闭式Rényi熵结果有助于后续基于熵积累的六态协议研究。

Abstract: Recent work in quantum key distribution (QKD) has yielded finite-size keyrates based on convex optimisation problems, using the framework of entropy accumulation. Multiple solvers have been developed to address these optimisations in recent years, but have been based on a somewhat elaborate framework using sophisticated algorithms, in order to accommodate a broad range of QKD protocols. In this work, we note that for basic QKD protocols such as qubit BB84, the keyrates can be easily computed using off-the-shelf solvers instead, by obtaining suitable tangent lines to lower bound terms in the objective function. We compute the resulting finite-size keyrates and compare them to previous work. Moreover, we extend this approach to the six-state protocol, by deriving closed-form expressions for the single-round Rényi entropies in the protocol. This should facilitate subsequent study of the six-state protocol via entropy accumulation.

</details>


### [121] [When Does a Quantum Speedup Survive End-to-End?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09850)
*Pablo Herrero Gómez,Antonio Jimeno Morenilla,David Muñoz Hernández,Higinio Mora Mora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从"接口相对性"角度重新审视量子加速的基本原语，提出转录层可容许关系A_M≼_int A_Q，并以其为工具对团复形TDA中的归一化Betti数估计进行可操作性审计，区分三类接口体制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始量子加速并非绝对量，而是依赖于输入访问方式（量子接口的实现包）与输出契约（状态/样本的消费方式）。现有TDA量子算法往往以抽象谱或块编码接口给出加速声明，却未厘清这些接口所许可的经典转录、设置开销与精度开销，导致加速主张缺乏可操作的审计标准。

Method: 引入相对于量子接口声明实现包的转录层可容许关系A_M≼_int A_Q，刻画同一实现包所许可的自适应经典访问转录，并将设置、转录生成与精度开销全部计入。以此为审计框架，对团复形TDA的归一化Betti估计分离出三种声明接口体制：可逆索引单纯形接口、基于成员关系的制备、抽象谱/块编码接口，并结合接口闭包与转录归约分析端到端代价。

Result: 可逆索引单纯形接口通过单计算分支上求值可逆例程，可认证与经典相匹配的单纯形采样及局部Laplacian行访问；基于成员关系的制备引入开销为C(n,k+1)/|S_k|的拒绝路径；抽象谱或块编码接口则需额外实现包、转录归约或共享表示。在索引证书与接口闭包下，端到端代价由所导入估计器对间隙γ的谱依赖决定；已具体实现的有界树宽族在ℚ上以秩计算即可获得精确poly(n)经典Betti计算。低秩分离进一步支持访问与输出契约所起的作用。

Conclusion: 量子原语加速是接口相对的，必须连同实现包、转录与精度开销一并审计；本文的可容许关系为区分"真加速"与"接口假设下的加速"提供了操作性判据，并表明在索引证书与接口闭包下，Betti估计的代价本质上受制于估计器的谱间隙依赖，而部分结构化族（如有界树宽）本就存在精确经典多项式算法。

Abstract: Primitive quantum speedups are interface-relative: they depend on the input access used to run the primitive and on the output contract used to consume its state or samples. This paper introduces a transcript-level admissibility relation \(A_M\preceq_{\mathrm{int}}A_Q\), defined relative to the declared implementation package of the quantum interface. It identifies which adaptive classical access transcripts that same package licenses, with all setup, transcript-generation, and precision overheads charged.
  The main application is an operational audit for normalized-Betti estimation in clique-complex TDA, separating three declared-interface regimes. Reversible indexed simplex interfaces certify matched classical simplex sampling and local Laplacian row access by evaluating their reversible routines on single computational branches. Membership-based preparations induce a rejection route of overhead \(\binom{n}{k+1}/|S_k|\). Abstract spectral or block-encoding interfaces require an accompanying implementation package, transcript reduction, or shared representation. Under the indexed certificate and interface closure, the end-to-end cost is fixed by the imported estimator's spectral dependence on the gap \(γ\); the concretely realized bounded-treewidth family already admits exact \(\mathrm{poly}(n)\) classical Betti computation by rank over \(\mathbb{Q}\). A low-rank separation supports the role of access and output contracts.

</details>


### [122] [From Block-encoding to Generalized Quantum Signal Processing: Principles, Algorithms and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09977)
*Tal Gurfinkel,Kaushika De Silva,Anuradha Mahasinghe,Jens Renders,Jack Blyth,James Greenwell,Archie Butterworth,Yusen Wu,Lyle Noakes,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将块编码、量子化、QSP、QSVT与GQSP统一为量子算法设计的框架，提出选择合适方法的系统决策流程，并给出从目标矩阵函数到可执行量子电路的端到端流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代量子算法日益以相干过程的形式实现算子与奇异值的多项式变换，亟需一个统一语言来连接各类问题并指导算法设计，明确在何种算子结构与目标变换下应选择何种技术。

Method: 将块编码、量子化、QSP、QSVT和GQSP作为五项紧密关联的关键工具，从第一性原理构建统一框架；提出系统化决策工作流，将设计组织为端到端流水线：确定目标矩阵函数、构造块编码、确定谱域、设计多项式或Laurent多项式近似、合成相位因子、转化为可执行量子电路。

Result: 通过代表性应用展示该框架，说明不同算法可由统一的算子变换序列构造，并阐明何时直接使用GQSP、经由量子化、Laurent展开或QSVT最为合适。

Conclusion: 该统一框架提供了基于多项式变换推理、设计与实现量子算法的实用方法论，具有接近最优的查询复杂度。

Abstract: Modern quantum algorithms are increasingly formulated as coherent procedures for implementing polynomial transformations of operators and singular values. This perspective provides a powerful and unifying language for quantum algorithm design, connecting a wide range of distinct problems through five closely related key tools: block-encoding, qubitization, QSP, QSVT and GQSP. Block-encoding embeds non-unitary matrices into larger unitaries; qubitization converts block-encodings into structured operators; QSP, QSVT and GQSP enable polynomial transformations with near-optimal query complexity. Together, these techniques form a general toolkit for transforming matrix functions into implementable quantum circuits. This paper develops these techniques from first principles as a unified framework for constructing quantum algorithms. We apply this framework to representative applications to highlight design principles and demonstrate how distinct algorithms can be constructed from a unified sequence of operator transformations.
  A central contribution is a systematic decision workflow for selecting the appropriate approach according to the operator structure and the desired transformation polynomial. This perspective clarifies when direct GQSP or through qubitization, or Laurent expansion, or QSVT is most appropriate. We organize algorithmic design into an end-to-end pipeline: identifying the target matrix function, constructing an appropriate block-encoding, determining the relevant spectral domain, designing a polynomial or Laurent-polynomial approximation, synthesizing the phase factors, and translating the transformation into an executable quantum circuit. By applying this unified framework to example applications, we showcase a practical methodology for reasoning, designing, and implementing quantum algorithms based on polynomial transformations.

</details>


### [123] [Fidelity-Aware Scheduling of Quantum Circuits on Multi-QPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09980)
*Innocenzo Fulginiti,Antonio Tudisco,Salvatore Zammuto,Patrick Hopf,Deborah Volpe,Helmut Seidl,Giovanna Turvani,Robert Wille,Christian B. Mendl,Martin Schulz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于图神经网络的保真度感知调度框架，在编译前预测电路在各QPU上的期望保真度，实现多QPU系统的高效任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPCQC平台包含多个在规模、拓扑、原生门和噪声特性上各异的QPU。噪声设备中错误随编译电路迅速累积，最大化执行保真度对可靠结果至关重要。保真度取决于针对特定目标设备的编译，同一高层电路在不同QPU上会产生不同可执行文件与不同期望保真度。穷举式保真度分配需将每个电路在每个设备上编译，计算开销巨大。

Method: 构建低开销的保真度感知调度框架：使用图神经网络（GNN）在编译之前估计每个电路在每个可用QPU上的期望保真度；随后由可调调度器利用这些估计值，在执行的保真度与并行度之间进行权衡控制。

Result: 该框架能够近似穷举式基于保真度的分配结果，同时相比将每个电路在所有设备上编译的暴力方法节省了计算资源。

Conclusion: 基于GNN的编译前保真度预测可有效支撑多QPU调度，在接近最优分配质量的同时显著降低编译开销，为HPCQC平台的可靠执行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High Performance Computing-Quantum Computing (HPCQC) platforms expose multiple Quantum Processing Units (QPUs) that may differ in size, topology, native gates, and noise characteristics. For current noisy devices, errors compound along the compiled circuits quickly, and minimizing them, that is, maximizing the circuits' execution fidelity, is essential for reliable results. Fidelity depends on the compilation to a specific target device: the same high-level circuit may produce different executables and, therefore, different expected fidelities across QPUs. We present a low-overhead fidelity-aware scheduling framework for multi-QPU systems based on a Graph Neural Network (GNN) that estimates, before compilation, the expected fidelity of each circuit on each available QPU. Then, a tunable scheduler uses these estimates to control the trade-off between execution fidelity and parallelism. Results show that this framework allows for approximating an exhaustive fidelity-based assignment, saving computational resources compared to a brute-force approach that compiles each circuit on every device.

</details>


### [124] [Direct Cultivation of Entangled $|CS\rangle$ Magic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09993)
*Gunsik Min,Jun Heo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出纠缠态 |CS⟩=CS|++⟩ 的直接培养架构，用双 Clifford 对合投影出四个可用分支并以六比特 [6,2,4] 记录保护分支标签，结合 CAT₇、Steane 纠错与 CZZ 校验构成完整工厂，实现显式认证至三阶故障的输出通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往 magic-state cultivation 主要针对单比特非 Clifford 资源，缺乏对纠缠非 Clifford 态的直接培养方案；本文旨在直接培养纠缠态 |CS⟩，避免先培养单比特资源再纠缠的间接路线。

Method: 利用两个对易 Clifford 对合将态投影到四个由 Pauli-frame 更新相联系的分支；采用最小六比特 [6,2,4] 记录保护分支标签以抵御将有效记录映射为另一有效记录的读出错误；工厂由 CAT₇ gadget、Steane 纠错、CZZ 受控校验及即时 Steane-to-surface 扩展组成。解码器使用 263 个操作探测器比特，另留 168 比特作后续验证，流程为精确低阶解析、预计算高阶目录与四陪集 BP+OSD。

Result: 在规定的 active-location stochastic-Pauli 模型下，精确枚举发现二阶故障以内不存在被接受的闭合边界逻辑失效机制，而三阶故障机制显式存在；有限 p 模拟量化了接受率、残余 syndrome 与解码负载，并对三阶故障定向采样估计主导逻辑错误通道。优化直接 d=5→13 扩展使接受输出操作数降低约 35–37%。相同二值逻辑错误率下比较两条路线：在较低噪声的两个基准点，即使三-T 路线已有现成输出 patch，直接 CS 的操作数仍更便宜，排序在 8×10⁻⁴ 与 9×10⁻⁴ 之间反转。

Conclusion: 结果表明纠缠非 Clifford 态可直接培养，并具备受保护的分支记录与显式认证的三阶故障输出通道。

Abstract: Magic-state cultivation has so far focused mainly on single-qubit non-Clifford resources. We develop a direct cultivation architecture for the entangled state $|CS\rangle=CS|++\rangle$. Two commuting Clifford involutions project onto four usable branches related by Pauli-frame updates. A minimal six-bit $[6,2,4]$ record protects the branch label against readout errors that map one valid record to another. Verified CAT$_7$ gadgets, Steane error detection, CZZ-based controlled checks, and immediate Steane-to-surface expansion form the complete factory. The decoder uses 263 operational detector bits, while 168 additional bits are withheld for later validation. Decoding proceeds through exact low-order resolution, a precomputed higher-order catalogue, and four-coset BP+OSD. Under the stated active-location stochastic-Pauli model, exact enumeration finds no accepted closed-boundary logical-failure mechanism through fault order two, while explicit order-three failure mechanisms exist. Finite-$p$ simulations quantify acceptance, residual syndromes, and decoder workload, and targeted sampling of order-three faults estimates the leading logical-error channels. Optimizing the direct $d=5\rightarrow13$ expansion reduces accepted-output operation count by approximately 35--37\%. We compare the two routes at the same binary logical-error rate. Direct CS remains cheaper in operation count at the two lower-noise benchmark points even when the three-$T$ route is given pre-existing output patches. The ordering reverses between $8\times10^{-4}$ and $9\times10^{-4}$. These results show that an entangled non-Clifford state can be cultivated directly with a protected branch record and an explicitly certified fault-order-three output channel.

</details>


### [125] [Coded Clifford Measurements for Multiqubit Magic-State Cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09994)
*Gunsik Min,Jun Heo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示魔术态培养中的经典记录层本质上是一个二进制线性码，用Clifford可观测量测量对应生成矩阵，可用Plotkin界限制其距离，证明物理可实现的Clifford族已饱和该界，从而在记录长度上无额外代价并降低编译开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔术态培养通过测量逻辑Clifford对称性并丢弃不一致结果来抑制错误，但纠缠资源会接受多个分支，保留分支携带多比特经典记录，其损坏会导致逻辑帧错误；作者希望理解并优化这一记录层的结构，以同时减少逻辑冗余与编译开销。

Method: 将记录层刻画为二进制线性码；对任意第三级Clifford层级酉U∈C3，用相互对易的Hermitian Clifford可观测量C(v)=UX(v)U†分辨分支UZ(a)|+⟩^{⊗k}，把测量计划视为生成矩阵，对有效记录进行后选择得到P_wv=O(q^d)；用Plotkin界限制任意二进制记录（线性或非线性）；并在原生CZZ辅助的Steane实现中进行编译与精确态矢量模拟。

Result: 物理可实现的Clifford族已饱和Plotkin界：距离四时，|CS⟩仅需六个逻辑测量，|CCZ⟩需七个，而独立重复分别需要八个和十二个；限制为可容错测量的宇称不增加记录长度。在原生CZZ辅助Steane实现中，[6,2,4] CS方案是唯一最小成本的距离四解，使培养核心活跃位置减少26.6%；精确态矢量模拟（不做最终理想码空间投影）确认接受率更高，残余Steane边界权重约为一半。

Conclusion: 编码方法能同时降低逻辑冗余和编译开销；将记录层视为二进制线性码为魔术态培养提供了统一而紧致的优化框架。

Abstract: Magic-state cultivation suppresses errors by measuring logical Clifford symmetries and discarding inconsistent outcomes. For an entangled resource several branches are accepted, so the retained branch carries a multibit classical record whose corruption produces a logical-frame error. We show that this record layer is exactly a binary linear code. For any third-level Clifford-hierarchy unitary $U \in \mathcal{C}_3$, the branches $UZ(a)|+\rangle^{\otimes k}$ are resolved by commuting Hermitian Clifford observables $C(v) = UX(v)U^{\dagger}$, so a measurement schedule is a generator matrix and postselection on valid records gives $P_{\mathrm{wv}} = O(q^{d})$ at record distance $d$. A Plotkin bound then limits every binary record, linear or not. The physically realizable Clifford family already saturates it: at distance four, six logical measurements suffice for $|CS\rangle$ and seven for $|CCZ\rangle$, against eight and twelve for independent repetition. Restricting to fault-tolerantly measurable parities therefore costs nothing in record length. The saving survives compilation: in a native-CZZ-assisted Steane realization the $[6,2,4]$ CS schedule is the unique minimum-cost distance-four solution, reducing the cultivation core by 26.6\% in active locations, and exact state-vector simulation without a final ideal code-space projection confirms higher acceptance and roughly half the residual Steane-boundary weight. Coding thus reduces logical redundancy and compiled overhead together.

</details>


### [126] [A Separation between Full-Rank PVM and Assumption-free Self-Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10013)
*Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一个非局域博弈，在纯满Schmidt秩投影策略中自检验最大纠缠量子比特策略，但通过一个非投影测量存在不等价最优解，解决了Baptista等人的猜想，并证明该自检验不鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baptista等人猜想能否同时对策略施加满秩与投影性约束来实现自检验，本文旨在构造满足该约束同时存在非投影最优解的反例，以解决这一猜想。

Method: 将CHSH博弈与辅助博弈G组合；G在满秩、投影假设下迫使确定性回答，而在无此假设时允许trine POVM最优解。通过C⊕M2(C)的迹态对G的最优关联进行分类，分析状态支撑上的投影性与投影扩张。

Result: 该博弈在纯满Schmidt秩投影策略中自检验最大纠缠量子比特策略，但在含一个非投影测量的策略下达不等价最优；状态支撑上的投影性消去矩阵直和项，非零非中止概率的投影扩张具有非迹局部态且其零左理想非双边；局部维数为六的族表明满秩PVM自检验不鲁棒。

Conclusion: 满秩与投影性可同时施加而不排除非投影最优策略，从而解决该猜想；同时该满秩PVM自检验缺乏鲁棒性。

Abstract: We construct a nonlocal game that self-tests a maximally entangled qubit strategy among pure full-Schmidt-rank projective strategies, but admits an inequivalent optimum using one nonprojective measurement. This resolves a conjecture of Baptista et al. on imposing full rank and projectivity simultaneously. The construction combines CHSH with an auxiliary game $G$ that forces deterministic answers under these assumptions and admits a trine POVM optimum without them. We classify the optimal correlations of $G$ as a line segment parametrized by the tracial states of $\mathbb{C}\oplus M_2(\mathbb{C})$. Projectivity on the state support removes the matrix summand, whereas projective dilations with nonzero nonabort probability have a nontracial local state whose zero left ideal is not two-sided. Finally, a family in local dimension six shows that the full-rank PVM self-test is not robust.

</details>


### [127] [One Relative Phase Orders Operational Thresholds of Noisy Bell Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10034)
*Xuan Du Trinh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对于其失效态构成在局域酉变换下封闭的凸集的量子操控能力（如可导引性与贝尔非局域性），贝尔混合态噪声的X部分给出了该能力的确定性阈值上界，且该上界由固定X部分的15个泡利期望值中的7个所能支撑的最紧界决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与两比特纠缠和CHSH非局域集不同，可导引集和贝尔非局域集尚无已知的一般闭式判据。因此需要寻找一种不依赖闭式判据的方法来界定这些能力的阈值。

Method: 针对任意失效态构成局域酉封闭凸集的量子能力，考虑含任意噪声的贝尔混合态，利用噪声的X部分构造阈值上界；证明该界为固定X部分的15个泡利期望值中7个所支撑的最紧界；并分析X型噪声下噪声与贝尔相干项之间的相对相位通过干涉对阈值的影响，推导单调性规律。

Result: 噪声X部分给出确定性阈值的上界，且该界为最紧界；若一个态的X部分具备该能力，则态本身也具备该能力，从而可通过部分层析成像认证能力；当噪声为X型时，混合态中噪声与贝尔相干项间的相对相位无法通过局域酉变换消除，并通过干涉控制阈值。

Conclusion: 证明了阈值关于噪声与贝尔相干项之间相对相位的单调不减规律，无论该能力是否存在闭式判据。

Abstract: Unlike the two-qubit entangled and CHSH-nonlocal sets, the steerable and Bell-nonlocal ones have no known general closed-form criterion. For any such operational ability whose failing states form a convex set closed under local unitaries, and for a Bell mixture with arbitrary noise, the $X$ part of the noise gives an upper bound on the definitive threshold, the Bell weight above which the mixture attains the ability. That bound is the tightest that the seven of the fifteen Pauli expectation values fixing the $X$ part can support. The same theorem shows that a state has the ability whenever its $X$ part does, so partial tomography of a state can certify its ability. When noise is of $X$ form, the mixture carries a relative phase between the noise and Bell coherences that cannot be gauged away by local unitaries, and it controls the threshold through interference. We prove a monotonicity law: the threshold is nondecreasing in this phase, whether or not a closed-form criterion for the ability exists.

</details>


### [128] [Semi-device-independent quantum randomness certification in semiconductor spin-noise measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10037)
*Hamid Tebyanian,Marius Cizauskas,Manfred Bayer,Marc Assmann,Alex Greilich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现基于半导体自旋噪声的半器件无关量子随机性认证，无需自旋系统微观模型，认证熵率达3.2–3.4 Gbit/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂固态系统是规模化、高带宽量子随机数生成的理想平台，但其涨落的量子来源难以认证，因为底层微观动力学难以建模与验证。

Method: 将光学源视为不可信，用实验测定的平均光子数上界、声明的模拟量程与逐样本裁剪上限约束；接收端建模为已校准、含噪、粗粒化的零差测量；采用半正定规划并严格控制Fock空间截断，认证对持有任意量子边信息的窃听者仍私密的随机性。

Result: 离线分析得到来自单电荷(In,Ga)As量子点系综的认证熵率3.2–3.4 Gbit/s，以及n-GaAs在自旋噪声匹配探测模式下的33 Mbit/s，超过此前自旋噪声发生器认证熵率两个数量级以上，且容忍固态发射体本身可分辨的每符号能量贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为固态自旋噪声源提供了无需微观模型的可认证量子随机性方案，显著提升认证熵率，并放宽了近真空输入的要求。

Abstract: Complex solid-state systems are promising platforms for scalable, high-bandwidth quantum random-number generation, yet certifying the quantum origin of their fluctuations remains difficult because the underlying microscopic dynamics are hard to model and validate. Here we demonstrate semi-device-independent quantum randomness certification from semiconductor spin noise, to our knowledge the first such certificate on any spin-noise source, without relying on a microscopic model of the spin system. The untrusted optical source is constrained by an experimentally tested mean-photon-number bound together with a declared analogue-range and per-sample clipping ceiling, while the trusted receiver is described as a calibrated, noisy, coarse-grained homodyne measurement. Using a semidefinite programme with rigorously controlled Fock-space truncation, we certify randomness that remains private against an adversary holding arbitrary quantum side information. Offline analysis yields certified entropy rates of $3.2$--$3.4$\,Gbit/s from a singly charged (In,Ga)As quantum-dot ensemble and $33$\,Mbit/s from $n$-GaAs in a spin-noise-matched detection mode. This exceeds the certified entropy rate of earlier spin-noise generators by more than two orders of magnitude, and the certificate tolerates a resolved per-symbol energy contribution from the solid-state emitter itself rather than requiring a near-vacuum input.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum computational resources and validation protocols for a three-mode non-Gaussian trilinear Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10043)
*Niccolò Laurora,Matteo Bina,Giulia Ferrini,Alessandro Ferraro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论分析三模非高斯三线性哈密顿量所生成的计算资源（多体纠缠与Wigner负性），提出基于位移宇称Bell测试的非局域态认证，并设计高效实验验证协议减少测量开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯相互作用是实现连续变量量子计算普适性的关键，但其实验表征与正确实现的验证仍具挑战性。

Method: 针对近期在超导微波平台实现的三模非高斯三线性哈密顿量，系统分析其生成的多体纠缠与Wigner负性；利用位移宇称Bell测试证明非局域态生成，量化Wigner对数负性并与已知非高斯资源态对比；提出测量高效验证协议，结合零方差观测量（nullifier与stabilizer）测量与有限相位空间测量。

Result: 通过位移宇称Bell测试实现了多体纠缠的操作性认证，量化了Wigner对数负性并完成基准比较；所提协议无需完整重构Wigner函数即可验证哈密顿量实现，显著降低实验开销。

Conclusion: 该工作为三模非高斯三线性哈密顿量的资源刻画提供了理论框架与可行实验验证方案，为连续变量量子计算中非高斯操作的认证奠定基础。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian interactions are a key ingredient for achieving universality in continuous-variable quantum computation, yet their experimental characterization and the validation of their correct implementation remain challenging tasks. In this work, we focus on a three-mode non-Gaussian trilinear Hamiltonian that has recently been realized in superconducting microwave platforms, and present a comprehensive theoretical analysis of the computational resources it generates, together with experimentally accessible protocols to validate their presence. We systematically investigate its ability to generate two key resources for quantum computation: multipartite entanglement and Wigner negativity. In particular, using displaced-parity Bell tests, we demonstrate the generation of nonlocal states and thereby provide an operational certification of multipartite entanglement in the non-Gaussian states produced by the dynamics. We further quantify the Wigner logarithmic negativity and benchmark it against that of established non-Gaussian resource states. Building on this resource-based characterization, we introduce a measurement-efficient protocol for the experimental validation of the Hamiltonian implementation without requiring full reconstruction of the Wigner function. The protocol combines the measurement of zero-variance observables (nullifiers and stabilizers) with a limited number of targeted phase-space measurements, leading to a drastic reduction of the experimental overhead.

</details>


### [130] [Accelerating A*-Based Algorithms for Decoding Quantum Low-Density Parity-Check Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10056)
*Lamia Yous,Francisco Garcia Herrero,Mark F. Flanagan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段量子LDPC译码框架：先用BP译码器处理校验子并生成硬判决与软信息，在BP未收敛时通过门控机制滤除置信度振荡的量子比特，再将精炼结果输入Tesseract进行残余纠错。在保持独立Tesseract逻辑错误率的同时，显著降低译码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tesseract译码器基于A*搜索，能保证找到最可能错误模式，但其图规模极大且搜索本质串行，导致计算开销高、运行时间长，难以实用。需要一种方法在保持其纠错性能的同时提升译码效率。

Method: 两阶段混合译码框架。第一阶段用置信传播（BP）译码器处理校验子，输出硬判决（二值错误向量）和软信息（每量子比特置信度）。当BP不收敛时，引入门控机制检查BP输出，识别并过滤置信度振荡的量子比特；将精炼后的输出作为Tesseract的输入，由Tesseract识别并纠正残余错误。该框架利用BP的高速性，将困难实例交给Tesseract处理。

Result: 在所有测试的物理错误率下，Tesseract扩展节点数至少减少5倍；对[[126,12,d<11]] T1码，在p=0.05时减少近15倍；对[[72,12,6]]自行车双变量（BB）码，在p=0.05时约减少8.8倍。整体译码复杂度大幅降低，同时保持独立Tesseract的逻辑错误率（LER）。

Conclusion: BP与Tesseract结合的两阶段框架可有效降低量子LDPC译码的整体复杂度并保持译码性能，门控机制对非收敛BP输出的精炼是关键，为量子纠错的高效实用译码提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes represent a promising approach for error correction in quantum computing. The recently proposed Tesseract decoder uses the A* search algorithm that guarantees finding the most likely error pattern. However, practical implementations of Tesseract often involve an extremely large graph, and the inherently sequential nature of the search results in high computational overhead and long runtime. To improve decoding efficiency, we propose a two-stage decoding framework. First, a belief propagation (BP) decoder efficiently processes the syndrome. This step generates hard decisions (a binary error vector) and soft information (per-qubit confidence levels). In non-convergent BP cases, a gating mechanism examines the BP decoder's output to identify and filter out qubits with oscillating confidence values. In these cases, the refined output serves as input to Tesseract, which then attempts to identify and correct any residual errors. This hybrid approach leverages the high speed of BP decoding and delegates the more challenging decoding instances to Tesseract. Numerical results demonstrate a substantial reduction in overall decoding complexity while maintaining the logical error rate (LER) of the stand-alone Tesseract. Across all tested physical error rates, the proposed method achieves at least 5x reduction in the number of expanded nodes in the Tesseract, with a peak reduction of nearly 15x at a physical error rate of p = 0.05 for the [[126, 12, d < 11]] T1 code, and approximately 8.8x at p = 0.05 for the [[72, 12, 6]] bicycle bivariate (BB) code.

</details>


### [131] [Haar-Bayesian Pure-State Prediction under Relative-Entropy Loss: Arbitrary-Effect Reduction and Global Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10072)
*Masahito Hayashi,Ayanava Dasgupta,Naqueeb Ahmad Warsi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究在n个已知副本上任意集体测量后，对未知有限维纯量子态一个未测量副本的Haar-Bayes预测，以量子相对熵度量损失；证明最高权协变POVM全局Bayes最优且全局minimax，风险为h_d((n+1)/(n+d))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对未知纯量子态的一个未测量副本，在任意集体测量下如何进行最优预测并在所有POVM上优化测量，同时给出全局最优/最小最大风险。

Method: 采用Haar先验的Bayes预测框架，以量子相对熵为损失；对固定测量，预测态为后验均值，优化损失为熵；对对称子空间上所有POVM优化。利用后验预测态μ_E=(I+nρ_E)/(n+d)及纯谱majorization给出逐结果熵下界。

Result: 相干秩一效果达到下界，其Haar轨道给出最高权协变POVM，因而全局Bayes最优且（由协变性）全局minimax；精确风险h_d((n+1)/(n+d))。同一约化还表明该POVM最大化潜在纯态与后验预测态的平均后验纯度/联合重叠，最优值为((n+1)^2+d-1)/(n+d)^2。

Conclusion: 最高权协变POVM是在所有集体测量中的全局Bayes最优与全局minimax测量；其风险与平均后验纯度有闭式表达，并给出了熵下界的紧性来源。

Abstract: We study Haar-Bayesian prediction of one unmeasured copy of an unknown finite-dimensional pure quantum state after an arbitrary collective measurement on $n$ observed copies. Performance is evaluated by quantum relative entropy. For a fixed measurement, the Bayes predictive state is the posterior mean and the optimized conditional loss is its entropy. We then optimize the measurement over all POVMs on the symmetric subspace. For every nonzero positive effect $E$, the corresponding posterior predictive state is $μ_E=(I+nρ_E)/(n+d)$, where $ρ_E$ is the normalized one-particle marginal of $E$. Since a pure spectrum majorizes every density-operator spectrum, this identity gives an outcome-wise entropy lower bound. Coherent rank-one effects attain the bound, and their Haar orbit yields the highest-weight covariant POVM. Hence this POVM is globally Bayes optimal over all collective measurements and, by covariance, globally minimax. Its exact risk is $h_d((n+1)/(n+d))$, where $h_d(r)=-r\log r-(1-r)\log((1-r)/(d-1))$. The same arbitrary-effect reduction shows that the highest-weight POVM also maximizes the joint overlap between the latent pure state and its posterior predictive state, equivalently the mean posterior purity, with optimum $((n+1)^2+d-1)/(n+d)^2$.

</details>


### [132] [Loss-correcting fault-tolerant quantum computing architecture for neutral atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10079)
*Sanaa Sharma,Yutaka Hirano,Akihisa Goban,Hayata Yamasaki,Shinichi Sunami,Prakash Murali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对中性原子阵列中量子比特丢失这一主要错误源，建立了丢失感知的FTQC模型，并提出了容忍丢失的横向门架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列是大规模容错量子计算（FTQC）的领先量子比特技术，但其主要错误源是量子比特丢失（loss），且该错误随每次操作和移动而累积。标准纠错针对随机泡利错误，无法纠正丢失，因此大多数FTQC性能分析不直接适用。同时，编译与路由决策通常针对泡利错误代价模型优化，往往对丢失不敏感，可能增加丢失风险。

Method: 作者全面建模量子比特丢失对中性原子FTQC的影响，并开发了容忍丢失的横向门架构。将丢失视为跨整个程序的动态预算而非预设误差参数，通过布局、编译和译码的协同设计来控制它。目标是采用无独立存储区和纠缠区的物理实现，消除主导丢失的重复SLM-AOD交接和长距离穿梭。开发了编译优化，在遵守RF音调预算与AOD带宽约束下最大化门并行性。并将这些与丢失感知的延迟擦除译码器以及端到端丢失感知的魔态培养协议相结合。

Result: 相比分区基线，每个综合征提取轮次的累积丢失改善最高达2.15倍；相比当前架构，逻辑错误率降低超过两个数量级。该框架还为连续重载速率、AOD数量及穿梭轨迹选择等具体设备指标提供了指导。

Conclusion: 该研究为中性原子硬件规模化过程中的系统架构师提供了重要见解，表明通过丢失感知的协同设计可显著降低丢失与逻辑错误率。

Abstract: Neutral-atom arrays are a leading qubit technology for large-scale, fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). A dominant error source on this platform is qubit loss, which accrues with every operation and movement. The presence of loss undermines the promises of existing architectural work. Standard error correction targets stochastic Pauli errors and cannot correct loss, so most FTQC performance analyses are not directly compatible with it. Moreover, compilation and routing decisions, which strongly affect overall loss, are typically optimized against Pauli-error cost models and often remain loss-agnostic, potentially increasing exposure to the loss channel.
  In this work, we comprehensively model the effect of qubit loss on neutral-atom FTQC and develop a loss-tolerant transversal-gate architecture. We treat loss not as a predetermined error parameter but as a dynamic budget spent across a whole program, allowing us to control it by co-designing layout, compilation, and decoding. We target a physical implementation with no separate storage and entangling zones, eliminating the repeated SLM-AOD handoffs and long-distance shuttling that dominate loss in other layouts. We develop compiler optimizations that maximize gate parallelism while respecting the RF tone budget and AOD bandwidth constraints. Our work couples these with a loss-aware, delayed-erasure decoder and an end-to-end loss-aware magic state cultivation protocol. Overall, we improve accumulated loss per syndrome-extraction round by up to 2.15X against a zoned baseline and reduce logical error rates by over two orders of magnitude versus current architectures. Our framework also informs concrete device targets such as continuous reloading rates, AOD counts, and shuttling trajectory choices. We expect these insights to matter for system architects as neutral-atom hardware scales.

</details>


### [133] [In situ characterization of a photon-subtraction device via heralding counts and homodyne detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10100)
*Priyanka Sharma,Matteo G. A. Paris,Stefano Olivares*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可在实验中原位实施的方案，仅利用光子减法装置运行时的测量数据，同时估计分束器透射率与预告探测器量子效率，并基于经典多参数估计理论推导Fisher信息矩阵，比较联合与序贯估计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子减法是产生非高斯光态的重要技术，其器件性能主要由分束器透射率和预告探测器量子效率决定；准确获知这两个参数对评估所生成非经典态的质量至关重要，因此需要一种实用的原位参数估计方法。

Method: 利用on/off预告探测器的click统计量，以及对分束器透射模式（输入为位移压缩态）进行零拍测量所得数据；在经典多参数估计理论框架下推导Fisher信息矩阵，研究联合估计与序贯估计两种策略，并评估统计模型的sloppiness及其对测量正交分量、探测光子数、压缩比例、分束器透射率和探测器效率的依赖。

Result: 合适的零拍正交分量选择可显著降低参数简并度，从而实现高效的联合估计；在广泛的实验相关参数范围内，联合估计策略始终给出比序贯估计更低（更优）的估计下界。

Conclusion: 所提原位方案实验可行，仅依赖器件运行数据即可同时估计透射率与探测效率；通过优化零拍正交分量可缓解参数简并，且联合估计在精度上优于序贯估计。

Abstract: Photon subtraction is one of the most important techniques for generating non-Gaussian optical states and constitutes a key resource for quantum information processing and quantum metrology. The practical performance of a photon-subtraction device is primarily determined by the transmissivity of the beam splitter and the quantum efficiency of the heralding detector. Accurate knowledge of these parameters is therefore essential for assessing the quality of the generated non-classical states. In this work, we propose an experimentally feasible in situ scheme for the simultaneous estimation of these two parameters using only the measurement data produced during the operation of the device. Our protocol combines the click statistics of an on/off heralding detector with homodyne measurements performed on the transmitted mode of the beam splitter when fed by a displaced squeezed state. Within the framework of classical multi-parameter estimation theory, we derive the corresponding Fisher information matrix and investigate both joint and sequential estimation strategies. For simultaneous measurement of parameters, we evaluate the sloppiness of the underlying statistical model and analyze its dependence on the measured quadrature, probe photon number, squeezing fraction, beam splitter transmissivity, and detector efficiency. Our analysis proves that an appropriate choice of the homodyne quadrature substantially reduces parameter degeneracy and enables efficient simultaneous estimation. Furthermore, we show that the joint estimation strategy consistently provides a lower estimation bound than the sequential estimation approach over a broad range of experimentally relevant parameters.

</details>


### [134] [Multiparameter quantum bounds for entanglement-assisted aperture synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10111)
*Kalaga Madhav,Pieter Kok,Nic Scott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将M站量子干涉成像建模为多参数估计问题，证明多模QFI可约化为单光子QFI，给出纠缠恢复QFI的比例因子与可达性，并从数值上揭示扩展源模型的测量不兼容性，提出全局模排序接收机、噪声资源阈值与凸优化分配方案，以CHARA阵列为例给出设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠辅助光学干涉理论仅处理单一两站基线、单一可见度，无法刻画M站阵列对扩展场景的多参数联合估计，也不清楚纠缠在光子数超选择规则下的实际增益与资源分配方式。

Method: 针对M站阵列成像扩展场景建立多参数估计框架；证明归约引理（多模QFI=平均光子数×单离域光子QFI）；分析局域光子数超选择规则与共享纠缠的作用，导出f(r)=r/(1+r)并证明可达性；计算QFI矩阵与平均Uhlmann曲率，比较Holevo界与SLD界；设计全局模排序（量子傅里叶变换）集体接收机；推导噪声资源阈值；将有限纠缠预算的最优分配表述为凸规划并求解闭式解。

Result: 点源测量兼容，所有考察的扩展源模型均不兼容，Holevo界超出SLD界最高约74%；全局模排序接收机的加权方差在SLD界约7%以内，比显式成对接收机低约一个数量级；给出近参数下约20 km的盈亏平衡基线；最优分配呈平方根律比例解，倾向低可见度基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子孔径合成重塑为集体多参数估计问题，为M站纠缠辅助干涉成像提供可达性界、接收机方案与资源分配准则，并在CHARA阵列上给出现实设计目标。

Abstract: Entanglement-assisted optical interferometry promises diffraction-limited imaging over baselines where light cannot be physically combined, but existing theory treats only a single visibility on one two-station baseline. This work formulates the multiparameter estimation problem for an M-station array imaging an extended scene. A reduction lemma shows that the multimode quantum Fisher information (QFI) equals the mean photon number times the QFI of a single delocalized photon, collapsing the problem to a finite-dimensional one. The local photon-number superselection rule (SSR) erases all phase information from the bare state, while shared entanglement restores a fraction f(r)=r/(1+r) of the QFI on a baseline supplied with r pairs -- a factor shown to be achievable, since the reduced two-mode state of any pair is exactly the two-station weak-thermal state. The QFI matrix and its mean Uhlmann curvature reveal numerically that a point source is measurement-compatible, whereas for every extended-source model examined it is incompatible, the Holevo bound exceeding the symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) bound by up to ~74%. An explicit collective receiver -- a global mode-sorting (quantum Fourier transform) measurement -- then attains a weighted variance within ~7% of the SLD bound and roughly an order of magnitude below an explicit pairwise receiver, and a noisy-resource advantage threshold gives the break-even baseline (~20 km for near-term parameters) beyond which repeater-distributed entanglement beats direct transmission. Finally, the optimal allocation of a finite entanglement budget is a convex program with a closed-form proportional (square-root-law) solution favoring low-visibility baselines. These results recast quantum aperture synthesis as collective multiparameter estimation and give concrete design targets, illustrated on the CHARA array.

</details>


### [135] [The Sample Complexity of Quantum Entanglement Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10141)
*Nathan Roll*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究"决定哪些量子比特应共享纠缠需要多少历史请求"这一问题，证明所需数据量取决于查询带来的分配选择，且更大内存不一定需要更多数据。作者刻画了独立对易X/Z型Pauli查询下可达到的预测对比度区域，构造保持经典比特的编码，并给出路径与分组结构下的极小极大超额误差标度律，同时在量子设备与零售交易数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究量子纠缠分配决策所需的样本复杂度：直觉上内存越大应需要越多历史请求，但论文质疑并考察这一直觉，追问所需请求数与分配选择（查询结构）之间的关系，并将该学习问题推广到结构化交易共址（co-location）场景。

Method: 方法上：内存存储一个经典比特，通过固定探测器回答请求并保持每个测量扇区内的相干性；针对独立的对易X型与Z型Pauli查询，刻画完整的可达到预测对比度区域，并构造在每个非零顶点保持该比特的编码；在尖锐报告下推导d量子比特路径和每组至多k量子比特分组的极小极大超额误差标度；分析连通双团（biclique）区域在深度、区域数和每区域连接数有界时的样本需求；处理制备噪声带来的校准要求并推导需额外新鲜探测器调用的精确权衡；将学习律迁移到结构化交易共址问题。

Result: 主要结果：尖锐报告下，d量子比特路径与每组至多k量子比特的分组在m次请求后的极小极大超额误差正比于 k^{-1}·min{1, √(d log(k+1)/m)}，对2≤k<d一致成立；连通双团区域在深度、区域数与每区域连接数有界时可在不增加样本需求的情况下增长；制备噪声引入独立的校准要求，并给出需额外新鲜探测器调用的精确权衡。实验方面：在原生15量子比特设备上比较了不同编码，完整链路方案在设备上表现最优；在公开购买篮数据上，频率分组在最大容量零售设置中优于购物篮搜索。

Conclusion: 结论：决定量子比特纠缠分配所需的历史请求数并非随内存单调增长，而是由查询产生的分配选择决定；更大内存可能不需要更多数据。论文给出了可达到预测对比度区域的完整刻画、保持比特的编码构造及精确的误差标度律，并通过量子设备实验与零售交易数据验证了理论预测，表明该学习律可迁移至结构化交易共址等实际场景。

Abstract: How many past requests are needed to decide which qubits should share entanglement? We show that the answer depends on the allocation choices created by the queries: a larger memory can require no more data. The memory stores a classical bit and answers requests through a fixed detector that preserves coherence within each measured sector. For independent commuting $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli queries, we characterize the full attainable prediction-contrast region and construct encodings that preserve the bit at every nonzero vertex. With sharp reports, a $d$-qubit path and groups of at most $k$ qubits have minimax excess error after $m$ requests proportional to $k^{-1}\min\{1,\sqrt{d\log(k+1)/m}\}$, uniformly for $2\leq k<d$. Connected biclique regions can grow without increasing sample demand when depth, region count and connections per region stay bounded. Preparation noise introduces a separate calibration requirement. We derive an exact tradeoff with extra fresh detector calls and transfer the learning law to structured transaction co-location. Population-risk experiments test the statistical predictions. We also compare encodings on a native 15-qubit device and learned partitions on public purchase baskets. The full chain wins on the device; frequency grouping outperforms basket search in the largest-capacity retail setting.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum Barankin bounds beyond local unbiasedness: Analytic results for Gaussian states via a right-division framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10157)
*Maximilian Reichert,Javier Navarro,Gerardo Adesso,Manuel Gessner,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于非对称右除超算符的量子Barankin界，给出高斯态Barankin矩阵的解析表达式，可高效计算参数估计精度下界，并揭示少次测量区间的阈值效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Cramér-Rao界是量子参数估计的标准精度极限，但它依赖局部无偏性假设，在少次测量（few-shot）区间存在阈值效应时无法刻画真实可达精度，因此需要一种能施加全局无偏性或任意偏置约束的更一般下界。

Method: 提出量子Barankin界，对满足在任意参数点上任意偏置约束的估计量给出均方误差下界，可施加全参数空间的无偏性约束；与近期基于对称除超算符的量子Barankin界不同，本文采用非对称右除超算符构造，从而得到高斯态Barankin矩阵的解析表达式，实现高效计算。

Result: 推导出高斯态Barankin矩阵的解析形式，使该界可高效计算；并在若干呈现少次测量阈值效应的实例中应用，验证了该界能刻画被标准量子Cramér-Rao界方法忽略的阈值效应。

Conclusion: 该非对称右除超算符形式的量子Barankin界为全局无偏及带偏置约束的量子参数估计提供了有效的精度下界，在高斯态下可解析计算，并能捕捉少次测量区的阈值效应，是对量子Cramér-Rao界的有力补充。

Abstract: We propose a quantum version of the Barankin bound as an alternative to the quantum Cramér-Rao bound for quantum parameter estimation. The quantum Barankin bound provides a lower bound on the mean squared error of estimators satisfying arbitrarily chosen bias constraints at arbitrarily chosen parameter points. In particular, unbiasedness over the entire parameter space can be imposed, yielding precision limits for globally unbiased quantum parameter estimation. In contrast to the recently derived quantum Barankin bound, which is based on a symmetric division superoperator, our bound is based on a nonsymmetric right-division superoperator. This formulation enables us to find analytic expressions for the Barankin matrix for Gaussian states, which allows for an efficient calculation of the bound. We demonstrate the usefulness of our results by applying them to various examples that exhibit the threshold effect in the few-shot regime, which is invisible to the standard quantum Cramér-Rao bound approach.

</details>


### [137] [Unbounded degree overhead for Alice-conditioned quantum Bell certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10162)
*Fumin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph
