TL;DR: 本文研究Alice条件化NPA层级在Bell场景下的认证代价问题。作者证明在最简单的Bell场景中，不存在任何有限层级的Alice条件化NPA层级能够包含所有标准二级Bell证书；而标准二级证书本身却是精确的。由此界定了精确nice-SOS输入在编译博弈可靠性证明中的度数上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 要求每个平方和项只涉及Alice的一个测量问题，可能会带来无界的认证代价。作者希望探究：普通SOS度数与被单问题证书结构所"绑定"的资源之间是否存在本质区别，以及有限的Alice条件化层级能否刻画全部标准二级Bell证书。

Method: 作者在无限二面体群上构造了一族截断正泛函，证明它们在任意给定有限层级上都超过tilted-CHSH量子界，而一个标准二级证书却是精确的。通过Fejér加权迹，将正性归约为从滑动平均Gram矩阵中进行秩一减法。并在端点邻域内，利用最优策略核与精确Bernstein矩阵正性，在α∈[13/10,3/2]区间上给出尖锐的一级代价。

Result: 在最简单的Bell场景中，没有有限层级的Alice条件化NPA层级包含所有标准二级Bell证书。所需条件化层级在端点tilt附近至少以(2-α)^{-1/2}增长。不存在任何有限条件化层级能够针对量子侧信息认证整个最优CHSH随机性权衡，而标准二级却可以。在远离端点处，证明在α∈[13/10,3/2]上存在尖锐的一级代价。

Conclusion: 结果将普通SOS度数与被单问题证书结构所绑定的资源分离开来，表明条件化层级在结构上弱于标准SOS层级，并对精确nice-SOS输入在编译博弈可靠性证明中的度数给出了上界。

Abstract: Requiring each sum-of-squares term to involve only one of Alice's measurement questions can impose an unbounded certification cost. In the simplest Bell scenario, we prove that no finite level of the Alice-conditioned NPA hierarchy contains all standard level-two Bell certificates. An explicit family of truncated positive functionals on the infinite dihedral group exceeds the tilted-CHSH quantum bound at every prescribed finite level, while a standard degree-two certificate is exact. A Fejer-weighted trace reduces positivity to a rank-one subtraction from a moving-average Gram matrix. The required conditioned level grows at least as $(2-α)^{-1/2}$ near the endpoint tilt. This bounds the degree of exact nice-SOS inputs to compiled-game soundness proofs. Consequently, no finite conditioned level certifies the entire optimal CHSH randomness tradeoff against quantum side information, although standard level two does. Away from the endpoint, we prove a sharp one-level cost throughout $α\in[13/10,3/2]$, using optimal-strategy kernels and exact Bernstein matrix positivity to certify a continuous interval. The results separate ordinary SOS degree from the resources keyed by single-question certificate structure.

</details>


### [138] [Finite-Particle Quantum Reduction of Thermodynamic Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10211)
*Borhan Ahmadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在相同驱动与相同粗粒化空间记录下，有限量子（少玻色子Bose–Hubbard链）比匹配的经典场对应体系弛豫更强，但保留的平均能量能解析更多被记录掩盖的微观结构，信息浪费更少、熵产生更小；将过程闭环为仅用平均能量与空间记录的热机循环后，匹配热资源下量子体系输出更多功、效率更高，且优势随粒子数增大趋于经典场极限而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学之所以有用，是能在微观重构不可行时用少量可测量量预测与控制复杂系统。本文追问：在有限量子系统中，这种"操作层面的降维"是否比匹配的经典系统代价更低。核心动机是检验量子性本身能否降低粗粒化观测下的热力学代价（熵产生），并转化为热机性能优势。

Method: 对比少玻色子Bose–Hubbard链与其"保粒子数"的经典场对应体系，施加相同驱动力并使用相同的粗粒化空间记录；以观测到的粒子分布与保留的平均能量为观测量，量化弛豫程度、被记录掩盖的微观结构利用情况与熵产生；再将该过程闭环为热机循环，控制量仅用测得的平均能量与空间记录（不做全态层析），在匹配热资源下比较输出功与效率，并考察粒子数增加时优势的变化趋势。

Result: 量子体系在观测粒子分布上弛豫更多；同时保留的平均能量解析出更多被粗粒记录隐藏的微观结构，导致不可用信息更少、熵产生更小。在匹配热资源的热机循环中，更小的熵产生转化为更多输出功和更高效率。该优势随粒子数增加、趋近经典场区而减弱直至消失。

Conclusion: 在有限量子、少粒子尺度上，热力学的操作化降维可比经典情形代价更低：量子体系在相同粗粒观测下保留更多可用信息、产生更少熵，从而在仅用平均能量与空间记录控制的热机中获得功与效率优势；但该量子优势不具尺度不变性，随粒子数增大退化为经典场行为。

Abstract: Thermodynamics is useful because it lets us predict and control complex systems from a few accessible quantities when microscopic reconstruction is impractical. We ask whether this operational reduction can be less costly in a finite quantum system than in a matched classical one. We compare a few-boson Bose--Hubbard chain with its number-conserving classical-field counterpart under the same driving and the same coarse spatial record. The quantum system relaxes more in the observed particle distribution, yet the retained mean energy resolves more of the microscopic structure hidden by that record, leaving less information unusable and smaller entropy generation. We then close the process into a heat-engine cycle whose controls use only the measured mean energy and spatial record, not full state tomography; at matched thermal resources, the smaller entropy generation gives more work and higher efficiency. The advantage fades as the particle number grows toward the classical-field regime.

</details>


### [139] [Information capacity of quantum statistics: Fock-state tests of a discrete binary-sequence model on cloud photonic quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10216)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Octavia T. Volpe,Dejan Stojkovic,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用商用云光量子处理器的高精度数据，对量子力学作为离散信息容量理论统计极限的假设进行检验，将双光子态寄存器信息容量下限定于10^2以上，并排除n≤100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子力学是否为更基本离散理论的统计极限：该离散理论给系统有限信息容量，偏离量子统计的程度由系统使用容量的多少控制，需要在实验上对该容量给出下界。

Method: 以Powers等人的二进制序列模型为具体检验理论，结果概率来自长度n的离散序列计数，n→∞时恢复量子力学，n度量寄存器信息容量。在可编程干涉仪上测量光子Fock态|1>、|1,1>、预报|2>及级联分束器对，并在原位确定主导系统误差；采用要求旋转可复合的复合一致参数化，使用随机效应似然分析与参数自助法校准。

Result: 复合一致参数化以偏差1.24/n恢复量子力学；随机效应似然分析排除所有n≤100，双光子态寄存器信息容量若有限则超过10^2。级联分束器直接检验复合律：数据具有分裂不变性，以8σ排除朴素计数复合，并确认干涉符号规则。模型无关地，曲线平均的量子分配律偏差大于2.3×10^-2在95% CL被排除，原发表的线性参数化被直接排除。

Conclusion: 商用云光量子处理器已成为量子基础研究的定量仪器，信息容量是可实验限定的物理量；由于编译偏移对每个电路冻结因而可校准，当前硬件有望触及10^-3量级下限（n~10^3）。

Abstract: Our central premise is that quantum mechanics may be the statistical limit of a more fundamental discrete theory: any such theory equips a physical system with a finite information capacity, and its departure from quantum statistics is controlled by how much of that capacity the system uses. We show that commercial cloud photonic quantum processors have reached the precision required to bound this capacity from below, using the binary-sequence model of Powers et al. as the concrete test theory: outcome probabilities arise from counting discrete sequences of length $n$, quantum mechanics is recovered as $n \to \infty$, and $n$ measures the information capacity of the register behind a prepared state. Photon Fock states $|1\rangle$, $|1,1\rangle$, heralded $|2\rangle$, and cascaded beam-splitter pairs are measured on programmable interferometers with dominant systematics determined in situ. The model's composition-consistent parametrization, singled out by requiring that rotations compose, recovers quantum mechanics with deviations $1.24/n$; a random-effects likelihood analysis calibrated by parametric bootstrap excludes all $n \le 100$: the information capacity of the register carrying the two-photon state, if finite, exceeds $10^2$. Cascaded beam splitters test the composition law directly: the data are split-invariant, excluding naive count composition at $8σ$ and confirming the interference-sign rule. Model-independently, curve-averaged deviations from the quantum partition law larger than $2.3\times10^{-2}$ are excluded at 95% CL, and the originally published linear parametrization is excluded outright. Because the compilation offset is frozen per circuit it is calibratable, opening the $10^{-3}$ floor ($n \sim 10^3$) to current hardware: cloud photonic processors are quantitative instruments for quantum foundations, and information capacity an experimentally boundable quantity.

</details>


### [140] [An analysis of iterative refinement for quantum linear system solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10250)
*Adrian Harkness,Mohammadhossein Mohammadisiahroudi,Brandon Augustino,Giacomo Nannicini,Tamás Terlaky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出并分析了一种迭代精化（IR）框架，用于改善量子线性系统算法（QLSA）结合量子态层析（tomography）时的精度依赖性，使整个求解与经典描述提取过程保持对逆误差容限的多对数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QLSA能以多对数复杂度制备编码解的量子态，但通过层析提取经典描述时通常引入对目标精度的多项式依赖，导致整体精度代价较高。作者希望在全流程中保持多对数级的逆误差容限依赖。

Method: 提出迭代精化（IR）方案：求解一系列相关线性系统以逐步精化解，仅需固定精度的量子子程序，从而最终获得高精度解的经典描述。在三种输入模型下进行分析：量子读/经典写RAM（QRAM）、单位算符线性组合（LCU）以及稀疏访问oracle。

Result: 在量子模拟器和真实量子硬件上进行数值实验。结果表明，迭代精化能有效提高解精度，并展现出对硬件噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IR框架可在保持固定精度量子子程序的前提下，逐步精化解并实现高精度经典解描述；在多种输入模型下理论分析可行，实验验证其效率与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present and analyze an iterative refinement (IR) framework for improving the precision dependence of algorithms that combine a quantum linear system algorithm (QLSA) with quantum state tomography. Existing QLSAs achieve polylogarithmic dependence on the inverse error tolerance to prepare a quantum state encoding the solution, but extracting a classical description of such a state via tomography typically introduces a polynomial dependence on the target precision. To retain polylogarithmic dependence in the inverse error tolerance throughout the entire process, we develop an IR scheme that solves a sequence of related linear systems to progressively refine the solution while requiring only fixed-precision quantum subroutines, finally obtaining a classical description of a high-precision solution. We analyze the proposed framework under three input models: quantum-read/classical-write RAM (QRAM), linear combinations of unitaries (LCU), and sparse-access oracles. We evaluate the proposed scheme through numerical experiments on both quantum simulators and real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that iterative refinement efficiently improves the precision of the solution and exhibits robustness to hardware noise.

</details>


### [141] [Solving wave propagation problems via geometric quantum state preparation on dispersion manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10282)
*Yakov Solomons,Lee Peleg,Netanel Barel,Jonathan Nemirovsky,Amit Ben-Kish,Yotam Shapira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子算法，通过线性系统表述求解偏微分方程，重点处理频域离散Helmholtz方程描述的波动传播问题，直接制备共振流形上的量子态以规避后选择开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统求解器虽能实现系统自由度的指数级压缩，但其运行复杂度通常由离散算子的条件数决定。利用微分算子的解析结构可替代显式矩阵求逆，但Helmholtz方程的逆算子在色散面 k^2=ω^2/c^2 上奇异，导致直接傅里叶空间态制备方法指数低效，此为本文要解决的挑战。

Method: 针对逆算子在色散面奇异的问题，直接在共振流形上制备量子态，并通过傅里叶相位编码源位置，避免了在共振流形上进行后选择所产生的指数级开销。该方法还推广至傅里叶空间解在色散流形上具有奇异支撑的双曲型微分方程，将其求解重新表述为几何量子态制备问题。

Result: 算法消除了与共振流形后选择相关的指数级开销，成功概率线性依赖于源的数量，且与计算域大小无关。

Conclusion: 该方法为求解具有色散流形奇异支撑的双曲型微分方程提供了新途径，将求解问题转化为几何量子态制备，成功概率仅随源数量线性增长并与域大小无关。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for solving partial differential equations through a linear-system formulation, focusing on wave propagation problems described by the discretized Helmholtz equation in frequency domain. Although quantum linear-system solvers offer exponential compression of the system degrees of freedom, their runtime complexity is generally governed by the condition number of the discretized operator. Exploiting the analytic structure of the differential operator can provide an alternative to explicit matrix inversion, as illustrated for the screened Poisson equation through an explicit quantum circuit. For the Helmholtz equation, however, the inverse operator becomes singular on the dispersion surface $k^2=ω^2/c^2$, rendering direct Fourier-space state-preparation methods exponentially inefficient. We address this challenge by directly preparing quantum states supported on the resonant manifold and encoding source locations through Fourier phases. The resulting algorithm eliminates the exponentially large overhead associated with post-selection on the resonant manifold, yielding a success probability that depends linearly on the number of sources and is independent of the computational domain size. More generally, our approach applies to hyperbolic differential equations whose Fourier-space solutions possess singular support on dispersion manifolds, recasting their solution as a problem of geometric quantum state preparation.

</details>


### [142] [Programmable photonic state fusion via heralded storage of asynchronously generated resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10312)
*Mustafa Gündoğan,Dennis Rätzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有源存储环的聚变协议，将独立预报的光子态依次加载到同一循环模式中，消除产生时间标签，使累积态成为可编程线性因子的乘积，从而通过多项式分解构造目标叠加态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率性光子源在不同试验中产生的基元态彼此独立，而多光子协议要求它们在共同的时序模式上干涉，这一时间模式失配问题限制了多光子态的可扩展制备。

Method: 利用有源存储环依次加载独立预报的光子态；通过监测dump模式中的真空态进行条件选择，使新产生的态转移到已存储光子所在的循环模式，从而去掉其产生时间标签；要求基元态两条备选路径经历相同的加载变换，使累积态表示为可编程线性因子乘积，再借助多项式分解构造目标叠加；并随态增长动态调节存储环耦合。

Result: 动态调节存储环耦合显著提高加载效率，将固定平衡耦合器下快于指数的惩罚改进为指数标度。将该协议用于光子钟干涉测量中的双光子路径-频率态，并估算了包含光源等待时间与往返损耗在内的探测速率。

Conclusion: 该聚变协议可有效消除概率性光源的时间模式失配，实现目标叠加态的可编程构造并改善损耗标度，为光子钟干涉等多光子应用提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Probabilistic photonic sources generate elementary states in different trials, whereas multiphoton protocols require them to interfere in common temporal modes. We propose a fusion protocol that overcomes this mismatch by successively loading independently heralded photonic states into active storage loops. Conditioning on vacuum in monitored dump modes selects events in which each newly generated state is transferred into the same circulating modes as the photons already stored, thereby removing its generation time label. Provided the two alternatives of each elementary state undergo the same loading transformation, the accumulated state is described by a product of programmable linear factors, allowing a target superposition to be constructed by polynomial factorization. Adjusting the storage loop coupling as the state grows substantially improves the loading efficiency, changing the faster-than-exponential penalty of fixed balanced couplers to exponential scaling. We apply the protocol to two-photon path--frequency states for photonic clock interferometry and estimate the detected rate including source waiting time and round-trip loss.

</details>


### [143] [Constant-depth global shadow estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10408)
*Qingyue Zhang,Zhou You,Dayue Qin,Xiaopeng Li,Jens Eisert,You Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出浅层相位影子（shallow phase shadows），基于稀疏 Clifford-IQP 集合，证明即使该集合不构成近似相对误差设计，仍可高效全局估计稳定子态保真度，并给出常数深度（含中途测量与前馈）或对数深度的实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器规模增长时，读出电路可能成为主要瓶颈。随机测量与经典影子虽强大，但全局估计通常依赖高度随机的集合，需要不断增加的电路深度，带来大量实验开销。因此需要更少随机性、任务自适应的读出策略。

Method: 引入基于稀疏 Clifford-IQP 集合的浅层相位影子协议。该协议仅需受控相位纠缠门，在 all-to-all 架构上可用中途测量与经典前馈实现常数深度，或在不使用辅助系统时实现对数深度，并在电路资源与估计精度之间提供可调权衡。

Result: 证明了对稳定子态保真度的高效全局估计，尽管该集合并不构成近似相对误差设计；同时给出常数深度与对数深度两种实现，适用于具有长程连接性的实验相关架构。

Conclusion: 可扩展的量子读出无需复现通用随机性：面向任务的随机化能够实现显著更浅的全局刻画协议。

Abstract: Reliable and scalable readout strategies are essential for quantum technologies. As quantum processors grow, extracting useful information must remain feasible without measurement circuits becoming a dominant bottleneck. Randomized measurements and classical shadows provide a powerful route, but global estimation is conventionally associated with highly random ensembles that require increasing circuit depth and hence substantial experimental overhead. In this work, we show that substantially less randomness suffices when the readout is meaningfully adapted to the quantities being estimated. We introduce shallow phase shadows, based on a sparse Clifford-IQP ensemble, and prove efficient global estimation of stabilizer-state fidelities despite the ensemble not forming an approximate relative-error design. On all-to-all architectures, the protocol admits a constant-depth implementation using mid-circuit measurements and classical feedforward, or logarithmic depth without auxiliary systems. The protocol requires only controlled-phase entangling gates and offers a tunable trade-off between circuit resources and estimation accuracy, making it particularly amenable to experimentally relevant architectures with long-range connectivity. Our results show that scalable quantum readout need not reproduce generic randomness: task-adapted randomization can enable substantially shallower global characterization protocols.

</details>


### [144] [Faster Computation with the Generalized Laplacian Quantum Walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10429)
*Jonas Duda,Thomas G. Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明由广义拉普拉斯算子驱动的连续时间量子游走，可通过增大度矩阵倍数项使完全二部图多标记顶点搜索时间趋近最优，快于标准拉普拉斯或邻接矩阵量子游走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子游走是量子计算的通用模型并支撑多种量子算法。作者希望探索由广义拉普拉斯算子（可出现在自旋链中）驱动的连续时间量子游走，是否能比由标准拉普拉斯或邻接矩阵支配的典型量子游走更快地解决计算问题。

Method: 研究连续时间量子游走，所用广义拉普拉斯算子由标准拉普拉斯加上度矩阵的实值倍数构成。通过调节该倍数的大小，分析其在完全二部图且含多个标记顶点情形下的搜索行为，并研究该倍数增大时搜索时间趋于最优的现象。

Result: 随着度矩阵倍数幅值增大，广义拉普拉斯量子游走在完全二部图上搜索多个标记顶点的时间趋近最优，优于标准拉普拉斯或邻接矩阵下的典型量子游走。

Conclusion: 广义拉普拉斯量子游走有望成为开发额外更快量子算法的有用方法。

Abstract: Quantum walks are the quantum analogues of classical random walks or Markov chains. They are universal models of quantum computing, and they underpin a variety of quantum algorithms. We prove that a continuous-time quantum walk effected by a generalized Laplacian, which can arise in spin chains, can solve a computational problem more quickly than typical quantum walks governed by the standard Laplacian or adjacency matrix. This generalized Laplacian consists of the standard Laplacian plus a real-valued multiple of the degree matrix, and we prove that as the magnitude of the multiple of the degree matrix is increased, its corresponding quantum walk can search the complete bipartite graph with multiple marked vertices in time that approaches the optimal. This raises the potential for the generalized Laplacian quantum walk to be a useful method for developing additional faster quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [145] [Coherent advantage in the computational expressivity of excitonic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10448)
*Matthew Du,Carlos Floyd,Dipti Jasrasaria,Suriyanarayanan Vaikuntanathan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究将驱动-耗散激子网络作为可训练计算平台，以格点间耦合为输入、稳态为输出，证明相干性使计算表达能力随网络规模扩展，类似于人工神经网络，而强退相干会抑制这种扩展和整体表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能耗上升促使人们寻找可训练的物理替代计算基质；分子发色团耦合调控的实验进展使相干激发动力学可控，为探索非平衡量子系统的计算能力提供了契机。

Method: 构建驱动-耗散激子网络，将格点间耦合编码为输入，将系统稳态作为输出，分析相干性与退相干（dephasing）对计算表达能力的调控。

Result: 相干性使计算表达能力随网络规模扩展，表现出与人工神经网络类似的规模标度行为；强退相干同时抑制这种规模扩展和整体表达能力。

Conclusion: 相干性是非平衡量子系统中实现高表达能力计算的一种资源。

Abstract: The rising energy consumption of AI has generated interest in physical systems as alternative substrates for trainable computation. Recent experimental advances have enabled precise control over the couplings between molecular chromophores, which give rise to coherent excitation dynamics. Here, we study driven-dissipative excitonic networks as a computational platform, where the intersite couplings define the input and the steady state defines the output. We show that coherence enables computational expressivity to scale with network size, analogous to artificial neural networks. Both this scaling and the overall expressivity are suppressed by strong dephasing. Our work establishes coherence as a resource for expressive computation in nonequilibrium quantum systems.

</details>


### [146] [Information Causality Characterizes the Set of Quantum Correlations in the Simplest Bell Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10508)
*Mariami Gachechiladze,Nikolai Miklin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义信息因果性（IC）与新的通信协议相结合，在最简二体贝尔场景（两二元测量）中直接从IC导出量子贝尔不等式，精确刻画量子关联，并证明广义IC严格强于宏观局域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息因果性作为约束非信号理论关联的物理原理，能否精确恢复量子关联边界（超越Uffink不等式）一直是悬而未决的问题。作者希望检验并强化IC在解释量子非局域性极限中的作用。

Method: 将针对关联输入的广义IC表述与一种新的通信协议相结合，推导量子贝尔不等式，并据此导出Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes判据；同时构造违反广义IC但满足宏观局域性的关联实例进行对比。

Result: 在最简二体贝尔场景中，所导出的量子贝尔不等式精确刻画量子关联；成功从IC直接导出Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes判据；证明广义IC蕴含宏观局域性，但存在宏观局域却违反广义IC的关联，表明广义IC严格更强。

Conclusion: 结合此前广义IC蕴含非平凡通信复杂度原理的结果，本文强化了信息因果性在解释量子非局域性极限中的地位，并为在更一般贝尔场景中推导量子关联集合的更紧边界提供了系统途径。

Abstract: Information causality (IC) was introduced as a physical principle constraining correlations in non-signaling theories. Whether it can recover the exact quantum correlation boundary, beyond Uffink's inequality, has remained an open question. Here, we combine its generalized formulation for correlated inputs with a new communication protocol to derive quantum Bell inequalities that exactly characterize the quantum correlations in the simplest bipartite Bell scenario, with two binary measurements. In particular, we derive the Tsirelson-Landau-Masanes criterion directly from IC. Thus, the generalized IC implies macroscopic locality, while we also present macroscopically local correlations that violate the generalized IC. This establishes that generalized IC is a strictly stronger principle in this scenario. Together with our earlier result that generalized IC implies a nontrivial communication complexity principle, these findings strengthen the role of information causality in explaining the limits of quantum nonlocality and provide a systematic route to deriving tighter bounds on the set of quantum correlations in more general Bell scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [Optimal Low-Rank Quantum State Tomography with Bounded-Sample Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10514)
*Ashwin Nayak,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了低秩量子态层析在联合测量至多 $t$ 个样本时的最优样本复杂度为 $\\Theta\\left(\\frac{dr}{\\varepsilon^2}\\max\\{1,\\frac{r}{\\sqrt t}\\}\\right)$，并证明该上界可由非自适应协议达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究联合测量（每次测量可作用于至多 $t$ 个样本）对低秩量子态层析样本复杂度的提升作用，量化联合测量相比单样本测量的最大增益，并确定达到无限制集体测量速率所需的联合测量规模。

Method: 下界：固定均匀谱、变化状态支撑，界定任意 $t$ 样本联合测量的 Fisher 信息迹，结合自适应 Fisher 链式法则与 van Trees 不等式导出迹范数下界。上界：构造并分析基于高斯联合测量的非自适应层析协议，利用显式二阶矩恒等式与支撑外的条件高斯分布进行秩相关误差分析。

Result: 样本复杂度为 $\\Theta\\left(\\frac{dr}{\\varepsilon^2}\\max\\{1,\\frac{r}{\\sqrt t}\\}\\right)$。下界允许自适应选择联合测量，上界为非自适应，二者匹配。联合测量至多将单样本测量算法的复杂度改善 $\\sqrt t$ 倍；联合测量约 $r^2$ 个样本是达到无限制集体速率的充要条件。

Conclusion: 联合测量对低秩量子态层析的加速存在 $\\sqrt t$ 倍上限，且约 $r^2$ 样本的联合测量即可实现最优集体速率，非自适应高斯联合测量协议可达到该最优复杂度。

Abstract: We determine the optimal sample complexity of low-rank quantum state tomography when each measurement may act jointly on at most $t$ samples. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, estimating an unknown state on $\mathbb{C}^d$ of rank at most $r$ to trace norm error $\varepsilon$ with constant success probability requires, and is achievable with, $$
  Θ\left(
  \frac{dr}{\varepsilon^2}
  \max\left\{1,\frac r{\sqrt t}\right\}
  \right)$$ samples. The lower bound allows the protocol to choose each joint measurement adaptively using all previous classical outcomes; the matching upper bound is nonadaptive. Thus joint measurements on at most $t$ samples improve the complexity of algorithms making single-sample measurements by at most a factor $\sqrt t$. Further, measuring order $r^2$ samples jointly is necessary and sufficient to attain the unrestricted collective rate.
  For the lower bound, we vary the support of a state with fixed uniform spectrum and bound the Fisher information trace of every joint measurement on $t$ samples. The adaptive Fisher chain rule and the van Trees inequality then give the trace norm lower bound. For the upper bound, we construct and analyze a nonadaptive tomography protocol based on a Gaussian joint measurement. An explicit second moment identity and a conditional Gaussian law outside the state's support give a rank-dependent error analysis, yielding the matching rate.

</details>


### [148] [Private communication via zero-private-capacity quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10520)
*Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明存在两个各自私密容量为零的量子信道，联合使用时可实现正的私密通信速率，解决了量子信息论中长期悬而未决的私密容量超激活问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道私密通信需兼顾可靠传输与环境保密。核心问题是：两个私密容量均为零的信道能否联合实现私密通信。该问题长期悬而未决，且若成立将表明单个信道的私密容量无法决定其安全通信价值。

Method: 构造一个四能级信道与一个半擦除概率的量子比特擦除信道，二者私密容量均为零。编码方案使接收方获得线性信息增益，而环境泄漏至多为二次增长，从而通过固定的联合测量与经典编码实现私密性。初始激活实例借助大语言模型交互发现，并在 Lean 4 中完成形式化。

Result: 两个信道的联合使用实现每乘积使用超过 0.0001903 私密比特的正私密速率，同时各自私密容量为零，构成私密容量的超激活现象。

Conclusion: 该超激活现象在独立的经典无记忆窃听信道中不可能出现，说明仅凭信道的私密容量不足以刻画其对安全通信的价值。

Abstract: Private communication over a noisy quantum channel requires reliable transmission to the receiver and secrecy from the environment. Whether two channels with zero private capacity can jointly enable private communication is a longstanding open problem in quantum information theory. Here we resolve this problem by exhibiting a four-level channel and a qubit erasure channel with half erasure probability, each with zero private capacity, whose joint use achieves more than 0.0001903 private bits per product use. The encoding gives the receiver a linear information gain with at most quadratic environmental leakage, enabling privacy through a fixed joint measurement and classical coding. This superactivation, impossible for independent classical memoryless wiretap channels, shows that a channel's private capacity alone does not determine its value for secure communication. The initial activation example was identified through interactions with large language models, and the result has been formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [149] [Noether Symmetries Generate Deterministic Energy-Harvesting Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10533)
*Ali Akil,M. Hamed Mohammady,Zihan Wang,Oscar Dahlsten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性能量收集（DEH）何时可行，指出源-收集器动力学的对称性结构提供了识别DEH可行源态的通用途径，并推广到广义概率理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEH指从涨落源中吸收能量而不吸收熵。已有例子较为零散，缺乏识别哪些源态可实现DEH的一般性原理，因此需要建立普适的对称性判据。

Method: 利用源-收集器动力学的连续对称性与守恒Noether荷，证明只要收集器边界态对称不变，任一守恒荷诱导的源侧轨道上的态都实现相同的确定性收集器跃迁；并将Noether定理推广到广义概率理论以推广主结论。

Result: 一个荷部门间存在非零不对称性的DEH可行源态可生成无穷多个同时可行的源态；用Jaynes-Cummings模型、三自旋XX链和SU(2)模型说明构造；并建立收集的不对称性上界——源不对称性平均不增，DEH恰好饱和该上界并保持不对称性。

Conclusion: 对称性结构为DEH提供了通用构造与判据，DEH与不对称性守恒紧密相关，结果在广义概率理论框架下依然成立。

Abstract: We consider the general principles for when deterministic energy harvesting (DEH) is possible. DEH means absorbing energy from a fluctuating source without entropy being absorbed. We show that the symmetry structure of the source--harvester dynamics gives a general route beyond existing examples to identify DEH capable source states. Any continuous symmetry with a conserved Noether charge induces a source-side orbit of states that all implement the same deterministic harvester transition, provided the harvester boundary states are symmetry invariant. Consequently, one DEH capable source state with nonzero asymmetry between charge sectors can generate infinitely many simultaneously DEH capable source states. A Jaynes-Cummings model, a three-spin XX chain, and an SU(2) model illustrate the construction. We further extend Noether's theorem to generalised probabilistic theories and thereby generalise our main result. We further establish an asymmetry bound for harvesting, showing that source asymmetry cannot increase on average, while DEH saturates the bound exactly preserving asymmetry.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [A counterexample to the periodicity conjecture for finite-dimensional algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09732)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个36维有限维代数反例，其所有单模都是周期为4的周期模，但该代数本身非周期，从而否定了周期猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期猜想认为：若有限维代数的所有单模都是周期模，则该代数本身也是周期的。该问题长期未被解决，本文旨在检验其正确性。

Method: 显式构造一个36维有限维代数，并分析其单模的周期性及各模的周期性质。

Result: 所构造代数的所有单模均为周期模，周期为4；但该代数存在一个2维非周期模，故代数本身非周期。

Conclusion: 该36维代数构成周期猜想的反例，表明单模的周期性不能推出代数的周期性，猜想不成立。

Abstract: The periodicity conjecture asks whether a finite-dimensional algebra is periodic whenever all its simple modules are periodic. We construct a $36$-dimensional counterexample whose simple modules have period four. The algebra has a $2$-dimensional nonperiodic module, which shows that the algebra itself is not periodic.

</details>


### [151] [Toric degeneration for hybrid qudit spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09998)
*Jing-Song Huang,Soo Teck Lee,Pavle Pandžić*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究混合qudit空间上的协变代数，给出其生成元与关系描述，并证明它们是某类显式描述的半群代数的平坦形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为混合qudit空间建立协变代数框架，揭示其与半群代数之间的形变关系，从而为量子信息中的代数结构提供统一描述。

Method: 定义与混合qudit空间相联系的协变代数，用生成元与关系（generators and relations）进行刻画，并与显式给出的半群代数做对比，构造平坦形变。

Result: 证明了所考虑的协变代数是相应半群代数的平坦形变（flat deformation）。

Conclusion: 混合qudit空间的协变代数可纳入半群代数形变理论框架，为后续研究其表示与不变量提供了代数基础。

Abstract: We consider covariant algebras attached to certain hybrid qudit spaces. We describe these algebras in terms of generators and relations, and we show they are flat deformations of certain explicitly described semigroup algebras.

</details>


### [152] [Syzygy Transformations of Cluster Characters and Geometric Twists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10090)
*Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用完全乘法公式研究簇特征的 syzygy 与 suspension；在稳定 2-Calabi–Yau Frobenius 范畴中，syzygy 与 cosyzygy 诱导局部化特征代数的互逆自同构；在 Hom-有限 2-Calabi–Yau 三角范畴中，同一唯一性原理将簇特征沿 suspension 传输，得到 Auslander–Reiten $F$-多项式恒等式，且无需变异可达性。结果推广 positroid twist 恒等式到任意对象，恢复 Grassmannian 与 unipotent-cell 公式，识别边界局部化前的配分函数代数，并在相关热带坐标映射为同胚时导出热带协变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 簇特征在此前研究中多依赖变异可达性等组合条件，限制了适用范围；本文旨在用完全乘法公式给出更一般、无需变异可达性的统一框架，把 syzygy/suspension 与簇特征、Auslander–Reiten $F$-多项式以及 positroid twist 恒等式联系起来。

Method: 核心工具是完全乘法公式。作者分别在稳定 2-Calabi–Yau Frobenius 范畴与 Hom-有限 2-Calabi–Yau 三角范畴中考察 syzygy、cosyzygy 与 suspension 对簇特征的作用：前者证明其诱导局部化特征代数的互逆自同构；后者借助同一唯一性原理将所有簇特征沿 suspension 传输，从而建立 Auslander–Reiten $F$-多项式恒等式。随后将该框架用于 positroid twist 恒等式与热带协变性的推导。

Result: 在稳定 2-Calabi–Yau Frobenius 范畴中，syzygy 与 cosyzygy 诱导局部化特征代数的互逆自同构；在 Hom-有限 2-Calabi–Yau 三角范畴中，suspension 下所有簇特征被同一唯一性原理传输，得到 Auslander–Reiten $F$-多项式恒等式，且不要求变异可达性。应用上，positroid twist 恒等式被推广到所有对象，Grassmannian 与 unipotent-cell 公式得到恢复，边界局部化前的配分函数代数被识别，并在热带坐标映射为同胚时导出热带协变性。

Conclusion: 完全乘法公式为簇特征的 syzygy/suspension 理论提供了统一且不依赖变异可达性的框架；由此得到 Auslander–Reiten $F$-多项式恒等式，并把 positroid twist 恒等式、Grassmannian 与 unipotent-cell 公式、配分函数代数以及热带协变性纳入同一体系。

Abstract: We use the full multiplication formula to study syzygy and suspension of cluster characters. In stably 2-Calabi--Yau Frobenius categories, syzygy and cosyzygy induce inverse automorphisms of localized character algebras; in Hom-finite 2-Calabi--Yau triangulated categories, the same uniqueness principle transports all cluster characters under suspension. The latter gives our Auslander--Reiten $F$-polynomial identity. No mutation reachability is required. We extend positroid twist identities to all objects, recover the Grassmannian and unipotent-cell formulas, identify partition-function algebras before boundary localization, and derive tropical covariance when the relevant tropical coordinate map is a homeomorphism.

</details>


### [153] [Derived equivalences between diagram categories of finite posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10110)
*Chiara Ascenzi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限偏序集所索引的图解范畴之间的universal derived equivalences。作者基于Ladkani的构造，提出了"admissible cut"这一完全由偏序集序结构刻画的内在判据，从而可由满足该切割的偏序集构造出与之universally derived equivalent的新偏序集。该构造在保留划分的前提下可逆，因此可从变换后的偏序集恢复原有混合序关系。作为应用，证明了高度至多为1的有限偏序集与其对偶universally derived equivalent，给出了最小元处source-to-sink变换的判据，并通过对这类局部变换的序列恢复了有限树所有定向之间的universal derived equivalence；结果还应用于有限偏序集索引的persistence modules及通过附加有限参数得到的扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ladkani的构造能够由某些偏序集生成universally derived equivalent的新偏序集，但该构造的应用条件此前缺乏完全由偏序集本身序结构刻画的内在描述。本文旨在给出这样的判据，使得构造的适用性可以仅在偏序集的序结构层面判定，并借此系统化地产生universal derived equivalences并推广到相关应用场景。

Method: 从Ladkani的构造出发，作者引入"admissible cut"概念，完全用有限偏序集的序结构来刻画何时该偏序集存在可应用该构造的分解。基于此内在判据，从给定偏序集构造出新的偏序集，并证明二者universally derived equivalent；同时说明在保留相应划分时构造可逆，原混合序关系可从变换后的偏序集恢复。进一步将此局部变换用于若干具体情形，包括高度≤1的偏序集、最小元处的source-to-sink变换、有限树定向，以及persistence modules和附加有限参数的扩张。

Result: 给出了有限偏序集存在可应用Ladkani构造的分解的内在判据——admissible cut；由此可从满足该切割的偏序集构造出与之universally derived equivalent的新偏序集，且构造在保留划分时可逆。应用方面：证明了每个高度至多为1的有限偏序集与其对偶universally derived equivalent；给出了最小元处source-to-sink变换的刻画判据；并通过对这类局部变换的序列，恢复了有限树所有定向之间的universal derived equivalence。结果亦应用于有限偏序集索引的persistence modules及附加有限参数所得的扩张。

Conclusion: admissible cut为Ladkani构造提供了完全基于序结构的内在适用条件，成为产生有限偏序集之间universal derived equivalences的系统工具，并因构造可逆而保留了序关系信息的可恢复性。该框架统一并推广了高度≤1偏序集与对偶的等价、最小元处source-to-sink变换以及有限树各定向间的等价等结果，并可延伸至persistence modules与有限参数扩张的情形。

Abstract: We study universal derived equivalences between diagram categories indexed by finite posets. Starting from a construction of Ladkani, we give an intrinsic criterion for determining when a finite poset admits a decomposition to which this construction can be applied. This leads to the notion of an admissible cut, formulated entirely in terms of the order structure of the poset. Our main result therefore provides a method for producing, from a given finite poset admitting such a cut, a new poset that is universally derived equivalent to it. The construction is reversible once the partition is retained, so that the original mixed order relations can be recovered from the transformed poset. As applications, we show that every finite poset of height at most one is universally derived equivalent to its opposite, give a criterion characterizing source-to-sink transformations at minimal elements, and recover the universal derived equivalence of all orientations of a finite tree through sequences of such local transformations. These results are also applied to persistence modules indexed by finite posets and to extensions obtained by adjoining further finite parameters.

</details>


### [154] [Hybrid Kazhdan-Lusztig basis and parabolic induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10152)
*Volodymyr Mazorchuk,Shraddha Srivastava*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过在主块上构造并研究新的分层结构，为Hecke代数的混合Kazhdan-Lusztig基提供了范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kazhdan-Lusztig理论中，混合Kazhdan-Lusztig基的范畴化是表示论与几何表示论的核心问题之一。已有工作多针对普通Kazhdan-Lusztig基的范畴化，而混合形式缺乏统一的范畴框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在BGG范畴O的主块（principal block）上构造一系列新的分层结构（stratified structures），并对其进行研究，以此实现Hecke代数混合Kazhdan-Lusztig基的范畴化。

Result: 成功构造了BGG范畴O主块上的新分层结构，并以此为工具给出Hecke代数混合Kazhdan-Lusztig基的范畴化。

Conclusion: 主块上的新分层结构为混合Kazhdan-Lusztig基提供了自然的范畴实现，深化了对Kazhdan-Lusztig理论与BGG范畴O之间联系的理解。

Abstract: We provide a categorification for the hybrid Kazhdan-Lusztig bases of the Hecke algebra by constructing and investigating a series of new stratified structures on the principal block of BGG category $\mathcal{O}$.

</details>


### [155] [Finiteness and growth of brick chain filtrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10217)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维代数上的Ringel砖链滤过，证明了砖链滤过数目的有限性及其精确上界，并构造了增长指数级的例子，回答了Pavón和Ringel的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ringel提出的砖链滤过（brick chain filtrations）具有重要表示论意义，但其计数有限性、上界以及增长速率等问题尚未解决；Pavón也提出了关于挠类中最大子模取值有限性的问题。本文旨在回答这些公开问题。

Method: 利用子模簇（submodule varieties）证明固定模属于挠类的最大子模随挠类变化只取有限多个值；由此推出砖链滤过的有限性及维度d模的2^{d^2}上界。对τ-倾斜有限代数，用单模合成重数确定的多项式系数给出界。对三箭Kronecker代数构造砖族，并用Littlewood--Richardson公式和hook-length公式计算子模计数。

Result: 证明了最大子模取值有限，砖链滤过数目有限且对维度d模有上界2^{d^2}；τ-倾斜有限代数情形由合成重数决定的多项式系数上界；三箭Kronecker代数上构造出滤过计数随合成长度平方指数增长的砖族，最终超过合成长度的阶乘。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了Pavón的问题以及Ringel的两个问题，确立了砖链滤过计数的有限性与增长规律，揭示了τ-倾斜有限与无限情形下的本质差异。

Abstract: We study Ringel's brick chain filtrations over finite-dimensional algebras: filtrations whose factors are filtered by copies of individual bricks, ordered so that morphisms from earlier bricks to later ones vanish. First, using submodule varieties, we prove that the largest submodule of a fixed module belonging to a torsion class takes only finitely many values as the class varies, answering Pavón's question. We deduce finiteness of brick chain filtrations and the bound $2^{d^2}$ for modules of dimension $d$. For $τ$-tilting finite algebras, we bound their number by the multinomial coefficient determined by simple composition multiplicities. Finally, we construct families of bricks over the three-arrow Kronecker algebra whose filtration counts grow exponentially in the square of composition length. We prove this growth using a Littlewood--Richardson formula for submodule counts and the hook-length formula. In particular, the counts eventually exceed the factorial of composition length. These results answer Ringel's two questions.

</details>


### [156] [Cluster Morita theorem for negative cluster categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10396)
*Riku Fushimi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了允许 (-d)-simple-minded system 的 Hom-finite 代数三角范畴，将其等同于 proper (-d)-self-injective 非正 dg 代数的稳定范畴，并在 d-Calabi-Yau 假设下给出无环负簇范畴的刻画定理及多种 Calabi-Yau 结构对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望统一理解具有 simple-minded system 的代数三角范畴的结构，将其实现为特定 dg 代数的稳定范畴，并在 Calabi-Yau 情形下刻画负簇范畴及建立左右 Calabi-Yau 对称性。

Method: 固定整数 d≤-2，利用 (-d)-simple-minded system、d-stable locally finite strictly positive dg model、cosingular dg quotient、Hochschild 与约化循环局部化、Brav-Dyckerhoff 求值态射等工具进行范畴与链层刻画。

Result: 证明了 Hom-finite 代数三角范畴可刻画为 proper (-d)-self-injective 非正 dg 代数的稳定范畴，并存在相应 dg model；在 d-Calabi-Yau 假设下给出无环负簇范畴的刻画；链层上识别了右 (d+1)-Calabi-Yau 结构与归一化右 d-Calabi-Yau 结构；当 Koszul 对偶 proper 时，Brav-Dyckerhoff 求值态射为混合复形的拟同构，得到左右 Calabi-Yau 对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 simple-minded system 与 dg 代数稳定范畴之间的对应，推广了负簇范畴的刻画，并揭示了 Calabi-Yau 结构在链层与 Koszul 对偶下的对称性。

Abstract: Fix an integer $d\leq-2$. We characterize Hom-finite algebraic triangulated categories admitting a $(-d)$-simple-minded system as stable categories $\underline{\mathrm{CM}}(B)$ of proper $(-d)$-self-injective non-positive dg algebras; equivalently, each admits a $d$-stable locally finite strictly positive dg model whose cosingular dg quotient recovers the chosen enhancement. Under a $d$-Calabi--Yau hypothesis, we give a characterization theorem for acyclic negative cluster categories in terms of the finite graded extension algebra of a simple-minded system. At chain level, Hochschild and reduced cyclic localization identify right $(d+1)$-Calabi--Yau structures on the finite-dimensional part with normalized right $d$-Calabi--Yau structures on the cosingular quotient. Finally, when the Koszul dual is proper, the Brav--Dyckerhoff evaluation morphism is a quasi-isomorphism of mixed complexes, yielding left--right Calabi--Yau symmetry.

</details>


### [157] [Distinguished standard modules for $\mathrm{GL}_{2m}(\mathbb{C})/\mathrm{GL}_m(\mathbb{H})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10483)
*Alan Xuelun Hou,Tudor Popescu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了 $\GL_{2m}(\mathbb{C})$ 的标准模被 $\GL_m(\mathbb{H})$ 区分的充要条件：存在无不动点的对合 $p\in S_{2m}$ 使得 $\delta_{p(i)}=\delta_i^*$ 对每个 $i$ 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般线性群在四元数群作用下的区分性（distinguishedness），即刻画标准模中具有非零 $\GL_m(\mathbb{H})$-不变线性泛函的成员，这是相对迹公式与周期积分领域的重要问题。

Method: 令 $S=\delta_1\times\cdots\times\delta_{2m}$ 为 $\GL_{2m}(\mathbb{C})$ 的标准模，定义 $\delta_i^*(z)=\delta_i(\bar z)^{-1}$。首先验证 Suzuki–Tamori 重数估计定理的假设；接着利用 Matringe–Offen–Yang 的轨道计算得到必要条件与维数上界；最后通过局部交错周期（local intertwining periods）证明充分性。

Result: 标准模 $S$ 被 $\GL_m(\mathbb{H})$ 区分，当且仅当存在无不动点的对合 $p\in S_{2m}$ 使得 $\delta_{p(i)}=\delta_i^*$ 对所有 $i$ 成立。同时给出了匹配的维数上界（即重数至多为 1 的相关估计）。

Conclusion: 完全刻画了 $\GL_{2m}(\mathbb{C})$ 标准模被 $\GL_m(\mathbb{H})$ 区分的条件，即参数集在 $\delta\mapsto\delta^*$ 下需被一个无不动点对合配对。

Abstract: We characterize the standard modules of $\GL_{2m}(\C)$ that are distinguished by $\GL_m(\HH)$. Let $δ_1,\dots, δ_{2m}$ be characters of $\mathbb{C}^\times$. Assume that $S = δ_1 \times \cdots \times δ_{2m}$ is a standard module of $\GL_{2m}(\mathbb{C})$. For each $i$, define ${δ_i^*} (z) = δ_i(\overline{z})^{-1}$ for $ z \in \mathbb{C}^\times$. In particular, we conclude that a standard module for $\GL_{2m}(\mathbb{C})$ is distinguished by $\GL_{m}(\mathbb{H})$ if and only if there exists an involution $p\in S_{2m}$ without fixed points such that $δ_{p(i)}=δ_i^*$ for every $i$. We first verify the hypotheses of the multiplicity estimate theorem of Suzuki and Tamori in \cite{ST}. The orbit calculation of Matringe, Offen, and Yang in \cite{MOYglobal} then gives the necessary condition and a dimension bound. Then local intertwining periods prove sufficiency.

</details>

### [158] [Weyl defects in skein theory and quantum cluster charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09308)
*Jennifer Brown,Juan Ramón Gómez García,David Jordan,Matthias Vancraeynest*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文证明抛物缺陷的修正可逆性：Borel 缺陷与其对偶相消，差一个可逆 Weyl 缺陷及开子范畴限制；对任意约化群成立，并给出 SL3 的扩展计算，同时引入余维一、二缺陷的缺陷 skein 理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 arXiv:2102.12283 与 arXiv:2505.14836 引入的抛物缺陷，其核心问题是这类缺陷在何种意义下可逆。解决该问题有助于理解缺陷范畴的代数结构，并为量子簇簇代数中反复出现的标准图提供构造途径。

Method: 在缺陷 3-流形与缺陷曲面的粘合相容框架下，建立同时含余维一和余维二缺陷的缺陷 skein 理论；证明 Borel 缺陷与其对偶的相消仅在插入可逆 Weyl 缺陷并限制到开子范畴后成立，并对 G=SL3 做扩展计算以作说明。

Result: 对任意约化群 G，Borel 缺陷与其对偶在开子范畴内相差一个可逆 Weyl 缺陷而相消；该修正可逆性成立。SL3 情形给出扩展计算验证。所建立的缺陷 skein 理论与缺陷 3-流形和缺陷曲面的粘合相容。

Conclusion: 结果为曲面相关量子簇簇代数中的某些标准图提供了潜在的缺陷 skein 理论构造，并将抛物缺陷的可逆性推广到任意约化群。

Abstract: We prove a modified invertibility property for the parabolic defects introduced in arXiv:2102.12283, arXiv:2505.14836. Namely, we show that the Borel defect cancels its dual, up to insertion of an invertible Weyl defect and up to restriction to an open-subcategory. The main result holds for an arbitrary reductive group $G$ -- for illustration we give extended computations for $G=\mathrm{SL}_3$. Along the way, we introduce a defect skein theory with defects in both codimension one and two, which is compatible with gluing of defect 3-manifolds and defect surfaces. Our results provide a potential defect skein theoretic construction of certain standard charts which appears frequently in quantum cluster varieties associated to surfaces.

</details>


### [159] [Classification of finite-dimensional Nichols algebras of rank two in twisted Yetter--Drinfeld categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09812)
*Bowen Li*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文对满足支撑生成群G且B(V⊕W)有限维的辫子不可分解对(V,W)进行分类，共八种情形、五种支撑quandle，Cartan图均为A2、B2或G2型，并给出显式维数；在Γ2情形下发现普通情形所无的G2型新族，并在16阶非交换群上构造实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Yetter–Drinfeld模上简单对象的辫子不可分解对及其Nichols代数有限维性，是分类有限维点化Hopf代数（尤其小Gelfand–Kirillov维数）的核心问题；普通（非twisted，Φ=1）情形已有分类，而twisted情形Φ∈Z^3(G,C^×)是否带来新现象尚不清楚。

Method: 在归一化3-上循环Φ下，考察G^G_YD^Φ中有限维简单对象V,W，利用支撑与支撑quandle刻画对(V,W)，以上循环型辫子结构分析其辫子不可分解性，并通过Cartan图（广义Cartan矩阵/Weq等价）判定Nichols代数B(V⊕W)的有限维性，进而逐一分类。

Result: 互换意义下将支撑生成G且B(V⊕W)有限维的辫子不可分解对分为八种情形，对应五种支撑quandle；每种情形Cartan图为A2、B2或G2型标准型，并显式确定dim B(V⊕W)。在Γ2情形出现twisted特有的G2型族，普通Γ2分类中不存在；并给出16阶非交换群上的显式例子。

Conclusion: twisted Yetter–Drinfeld范畴中的有限维Nichols代数分类较普通情形更丰富：虽支撑quandle与Cartan图类型仍受限（A2/B2/G2），但Γ2上新增G2型族，说明3-上循环扭曲可产生本质新例子，值得进一步研究更一般Φ与群的推广。

Abstract: Let \(G\) be a finite non-abelian group, let \(Φ\in Z^3(G,\mathbb C^\times)\) be normalized, and let \(V,W\) be finite-dimensional simple objects of \({}_G^G\mathcal{YD}^Φ\). We classify, up to interchange, the braided-indecomposable pairs \((V,W)\) whose supports generate \(G\) and for which \(\mathcal B(V\oplus W)\) is finite-dimensional. The classification consists of eight cases with five possible support quandles. In every case the Cartan graph is standard of type \(A_2\), \(B_2\), or \(G_2\), and the dimension is determined explicitly. A new phenomenon occurs for \(Γ_2\): the twisted setting admits a family of type \(G_2\) absent from the ordinary \(Γ_2\) classification and we construct an explicit example over a non-abelian group of order \(16\).

</details>


### [160] [Local Ocneanu rigidity for separable algebra objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10440)
*Tinhinane Amina Azzouz,Mainak Ghosh,Sebastien Palcoux*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在代数闭域上的Hom-有限monoidal范畴中建立可分代数对象的局部Ocneanu刚性，得到轨道开性、仿射Bezout有效有限性、Frobenius子代数的交换关系与自对偶幂等刻画，并给出Etingof-Walton有限性定理的有效版本及算子代数中间子代数计数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ocneanu刚性刻画了特定代数结构在形变下的刚性，但经典结论依赖abelian环境范畴与整体性假设。本文希望去掉abelian性，建立"局部"刚性，并将有限性结果有效化，同时统一处理Hopf代数余理想子代数与算子代数（C*-代数、von Neumann代数、子因子）中的中间子代数计数问题。

Method: 在Hom-有限monoidal范畴C中研究可分代数对象：用可分性态射收缩前两个Hochschild上同调群，无需abelian环境范畴；用代数群轨道开性刻画可分代数结构与来自可分源的同态；借助仿射Bezout估计给出有效有限性；对Frobenius子代数，用交换关系将源与嵌入数据替换为单个自对偶幂等，并证明可分内共轭类在交换轨迹中开；用连通性把轨道界提升为子代数个数界；在酉情形下结合有限指数与有限中心假设处理E-兼容中间子代数。

Result: 可分内共轭类在交换轨迹中开；非零环境代数得到界2^(dim_k End_C(X))，连通性给出子代数个数的相同界；得到Etingof-Walton有限性定理的有效形式：复数域上有限维半单Hopf代数H至多2^(dim_C H)个左余理想子代数；酉情形下界定了C*-代数与von Neumann代数包含的E-兼容中间子代数（酉共轭意义下）；对不可约子因子将Bakshi-Das-Liu-Ren的9^[M:N]界改进为2^[M:N]。

Conclusion: 可分性通过Hochschild上同调收缩提供局部Ocneanu刚性，配合仿射Bezout与轨道开性可推出有效的有限性定理；自对偶幂等表述简化了Frobenius子代数数据，连通性将上界从轨道数传递到子代数数；该框架统一覆盖Hopf代数余理想子代数与算子代数中间子代数，给出指数级（而非9^[M:N]）的显式上界。

Abstract: We establish local Ocneanu rigidity for separable algebra objects in Hom-finite monoidal categories C over an algebraically closed field k. A separability morphism contracts the first two Hochschild cohomology groups without requiring an abelian ambient category. Separable algebra structures and homomorphisms from separable sources have open algebraic-group orbits, and affine Bezout estimates give effective finiteness results. For Frobenius subalgebras, exchange relations replace the source and embedding data by a single self-dual idempotent. We prove that their separable inner-conjugacy classes are open in the exchange locus, yielding a bound of 2^(dim_k End_C(X)) for nonzero ambient algebras; connectedness gives the same bound on the actual number of subalgebras. As an application, we obtain an effective form of the Etingof-Walton finiteness theorem: every finite-dimensional semisimple Hopf algebra H over the complex numbers has at most 2^(dim_C H) left coideal subalgebras. In the unitary setting, we bound E-compatible intermediates of C*-algebra and von Neumann algebra inclusions up to unitary conjugacy, under finite-index and finite-center hypotheses. For irreducible subfactors, these improve the 9^[M:N] bound of Bakshi-Das-Liu-Ren to 2^[M:N].

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [The hyperbolic class and vanishing laws in a TMF-valued four-manifold invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09819)
*Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 在关于谱Looijenga构造的显式假设下，计算了Gukov-Krushkal-Meier-Pei零截面不变量在周期拓扑模形式中的双曲平面值，得到Hopf元素η，并由此给出光滑闭单连通spin四流形上的完整取值公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的相关工作在额外的配边对偶假设下才能得到双曲值；本文旨在去掉该假设，直接从Looijenga限制映射推导双曲平面值。

Method: 在谱Looijenga构造的显式假设下，直接利用Looijenga限制映射计算零截面不变量在周期拓扑模形式中的双曲平面值，并结合定格的湮灭与消没结果。

Result: 双曲平面值为Hopf元素η，且附加一个双曲平面的作用等价于乘以该元素；结合定格的湮灭与消没结果，得到光滑闭单连通spin四流形上的完整取值公式。

Conclusion: 非零signature迫使该不变量为零，因此K3曲面的两种定向取值均为零。

Abstract: Under explicit hypotheses on the spectral Looijenga construction, we compute the hyperbolic-plane value of the zero-section invariant of Gukov, Krushkal, Meier, and Pei in periodic topological modular forms. The value is the Hopf element eta, and adjoining a hyperbolic plane acts by multiplication by this element. Earlier work obtains the hyperbolic value under an additional cobordism-duality assumption; here we derive it directly from the Looijenga restriction maps without that assumption. Together with annihilation and vanishing results for definite lattices, this gives a complete value formula on smooth closed simply connected spin four-manifolds. In particular, nonzero signature forces the invariant to vanish, so both orientations of a K3 surface have value zero.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [On scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of the root--Kerr object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09191)
*Samim Akhtar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究 root-Kerr 天体的标量与电磁微扰，推导无质量带电标量场的共流 Heun（CHE）径向方程及 Nekrasov-Shatashvili（NS）字典，导出了 Newman-Penrose Maxwell 标量的电磁微扰方程；并构建了盘/边缘边界数据问题以连接精确解与物理散射矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: root-Kerr 天体是 Kerr-Newman 黑洞在质量趋于零时的极限，其外部的无源电磁场与旋转盘源（含本质性分布边缘贡献）需要系统研究其标量与电磁微扰，以理解该时空的传播特性及散射性质。

Method: 对无质量带电标量，将最小耦合 Klein-Gordon 方程分离为带标量-背景电荷直接耦合的 CHE 径向方程，并给出 NS 字典，证明盘为普通径向导数点；对电磁微扰，直接推导辐射 Newman-Penrose Maxwell 标量方程，并建立盘/边缘边界数据问题，识别将 CHE/NS 连接系数转换为物理散射矩阵所需的响应数据。

Result: 标量与电磁微扰均具有相同的 CHE 结构；Maxwell 微扰与背景势无体耦合，因此仅靠外部方程无法确定电荷相关光子散射数据；通过全自旋螺旋度翻转康普顿信息可约束辐射子空间上的响应。

Conclusion: 该分析将精确外部传播与振幅匹配，同微观响应所需的额外源动力学区分开来，突出了从外部方程到物理散射矩阵之间需要额外源动力学数据。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of the root--Kerr object. Its source-free exterior carries the electromagnetic field obtained in the vanishing-mass limit of the Kerr--Newman black hole, while its physical source is a rotating disk with an essential distributional rim contribution. For a massless charged scalar, the minimally coupled Klein--Gordon equation separates into a confluent-Heun (CHE) radial equation with a direct coupling between the scalar and background charges. We give the associated Nekrasov--Shatashvili (NS) dictionary and show that the disk is an ordinary radial point. For electromagnetic pertubations, we derive the equations for the radiative Newman--Penrose Maxwell scalars directly. They have the same confluent-Heun structure, but a Maxwell perturbation has no bulk coupling to the background potential. Charge-dependent photon scattering data therefore cannot be fixed by the exterior equation alone. We formulate the disk/rim boundary-data problem, identify the response data required to convert exact CHE/NS connection coefficients into a physical scattering matrix, and show how all-spin helicity-flip Compton information constrains the response on the radiative subspace. The analysis separates exact exterior propagation and amplitude matching from the additional source dynamics required for a microscopic response.

</details>


### [163] [One Gravity, Many G's: Generalized Horizon Entropy and Gravitational Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09192)
*Yen Chin Ong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出"视界引力响应系数"概念，用熵对各视界面积的偏导数统一刻画引力耦合，解释黑洞-宇宙学耦合的质量增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义熵框架下的引力理论会导出多个、甚至可变的引力耦合，导致"哪个量才是物理上测量的牛顿常数"存在歧义，本文受此模糊性驱动展开研究。

Method: 将熵视为多视界状态空间上的函数 S=S(A_1,A_2,…,A_n)，以熵对单个视界面积的导数定义"视界引力响应系数"（即引力响应/磁化率概念）。

Result: 不同视界可以拥有彼此不同的引力响应，而无需引入多个基本引力耦合，类似于宏观介质中方向依赖的响应系数。

Conclusion: 该框架自然地表明不同视界熵（如宇宙学视界与黑洞视界）可相互影响，从而可能解释文献中已讨论的黑洞-宇宙学耦合"黑洞质量增长"现象。

Abstract: Effective and thermodynamic derivations of gravity theory in the context of generalized entropy can give rise to multiple, and potentially varying, gravitational couplings, raising the question of which quantity should be identified with the physically measured Newton constant. Motivated by this ambiguity, we formulate the notion of gravitational susceptibility of the horizon. In spacetimes containing multiple horizons, if the entropy is a function on a multi-horizon state space, $S=S(A_1,A_2,\cdots,A_n)$, then the derivatives of the entropy with respect to individual horizon areas define the horizon gravitational response coefficients. Different horizons may possess distinct gravitational responses without requiring multiple fundamental gravitational couplings, analogous to direction-dependent response coefficients in macroscopic media. This framework naturally suggests the possibility that horizon entropies (such as the cosmological apparent horizon and a black hole horizon) can influence one another, which could explain the black hole-cosmology coupled "black hole mass growth" previously discussed in the literature.

</details>


### [164] [Evolving wormhole cosmology: modified Friedmann dynamics and observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09193)
*Aritra Sanyal,Kazuharu Bamba,Prabir Rudra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Morris-Thorne度规出发，在平坦FLRW背景中构建含时变形状函数的演化虫洞宇宙学模型，通过体积平均得到带红移依赖修正项的改进Friedmann方程，并用多组观测数据检验其宇宙学表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虫洞解通常处于静态或嵌入特定背景，缺乏与标准宇宙学观测直接对接的框架；作者希望将Morris-Thorne虫洞推广到膨胀宇宙背景，考察其能否作为可检验的宇宙学模型并与暗能量现象学区分。

Method: 采用可分离的、随时间变化的形状函数构建Morris-Thorne虫洞在平坦FLRW背景中的演化模型，推导修正的能量-动量张量；引入体积平均程序得到含红移相关虫洞修正项的改进Friedmann方程；将形状函数指数与喉部演化指数合并为宏观指数β，并检验虫洞状态方程与该几何平均的一致性，验证全部可穿越性条件；最后用宇宙钟（cosmic chronometer）、DESI DR2 BAO及标准BAO数据共七种数据集组合进行拟合。

Result: 七种数据集组合相互一致；联合拟合给出的当前膨胀率介于本地测量值与CMB推断值之间，减速-加速转变与独立测定结果相符，有效暗能量状态方程接近但可区别于纯宇宙学常数；演化中的喉部半径为区分虫洞时空与唯象暗能量参数化提供了新的几何特异性观测量。

Conclusion: 该演化虫洞模型在几何上自洽且满足所有可穿越性条件，其宇宙学预测与多源观测数据相容；喉部半径的时变行为可作为判别虫洞时空与现象学暗能量模型的潜在观测量。

Abstract: We construct an evolving wormhole cosmological model from a Morris--Thorne metric with a separable, time-dependent shape function in a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaitre--Robertson--Walker background, deriving the corrected wormhole energy-momentum tensor and a volume-averaging procedure that yields a modified Friedmann equation with a redshift-dependent wormhole correction term. We show that the shape-function index and throat-evolution exponent combine into a single macroscopic exponent $β$, and we demonstrate that the resulting wormhole equation of state is consistent with this geometric averaging. All traversability conditions are verified. Confronting the model with cosmic chronometer, DESI Data Release~2 BAO, and standard BAO data across seven dataset combinations, our findings show that all combinations are mutually consistent, with the joint fit giving a present-day expansion rate intermediate between local and CMB-inferred values, a deceleration-to-acceleration transition consistent with independent determinations, and an effective dark-energy equation of state close to, but distinguishable from, a pure cosmological constant. The evolving throat radius offers a novel, geometry-specific observable for discriminating wormhole spacetimes from phenomenological dark-energy parametrisations.

</details>


### [165] [Entropy Obstruction to Closed Semiclassical Bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09196)
*Naman Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了半经典时空（紧致Cauchy切片）中的有限-$G\hslash$奇点定理：在离散极大聚焦等条件下，若一侧区域条件性超熵且另一侧鲁棒量子囚禁，则该区域的未来Cauchy发展包含不完备零测地生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将熵奇点定理（超熵+内向光片）与量子囚禁机制结合，构造适用于半经典引力的有限-$G\hslash$奇点定理，用以排除受控半经典反弹。

Method: 设紧致Cauchy切片被紧致曲面分为$B$、$C$两区；假设$B$条件性超熵（$H_{\max,\mathrm{gen}}^\varepsilon(BC|C)>0$）、从分割面朝向$B$的未来内向零边界为离散极大光片、$C$鲁棒量子囚禁；再假设离散极大聚焦与闭合光片的正则半经典端点，据此推导零测地不完备性。

Result: 在所述假设下，$B$的未来Cauchy发展包含不完备零测地生成元，即出现奇点；该结论与熵奇点定理平行，但由鲁棒量子囚禁提供额外的有限-$G\hslash$局部阻碍。

Conclusion: 在闭合Friedmann宇宙中，当收缩半球位于此类光片上且所含独立信息超过其边界可支撑量时，该定理排除受控半经典反弹。

Abstract: We prove a finite-$G\hslash$ singularity theorem for semiclassical spacetimes with compact Cauchy slices. Let a compact Cauchy slice be divided by a compact surface into regions $B$ and $C$. Suppose that $B$ is conditionally hyperentropic, $H_{\max,\mathrm{gen}}^\varepsilon(BC|C)>0$, that the future-inward null boundary from the dividing surface toward $B$ is a discrete max lightsheet, and that $C$ is robustly quantum trapped. Assuming discrete max-focusing and a regular semiclassical endpoint for a closing lightsheet, the future Cauchy development of $B$ contains an incomplete null generator. This parallels entropy singularity theorems in which hyperentropy is combined with the existence of an inward lightsheet, while robust quantum trapping supplies the additional finite-$G\hslash$ local obstruction needed here. In a closed Friedmann universe, the theorem excludes a controlled semiclassical bounce when a contracting hemisphere lies on such a lightsheet and contains more independent information than its boundary can support.

</details>


### [166] [Constraints on Scalar--Tensor--Vector Gravity Theory Parameters Inferred from Quasiperiodic Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09197)
*Marco Muccino,Kuantay Boshkayev,Binay Prakash Akhouri,Izzet Sakallı,Yassine Sekhmani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究STVG静态带电解中中性试验粒子的圆轨道测地线，并用12个吸积致密天体的双千赫兹准周期振荡（QPO）约束其参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用双千赫兹QPO观测为标量-张量-矢量引力（STVG）的静态带电解参数提供约束，并考察其对致密天体质量推断的影响。

Method: 从有效势出发推导比能、比角动量、开普勒角速度及径/垂向回旋频率的闭合表达式；给出视界、光子球、阴影半径、ISCO的闭式解（在相应极限下退化为RN与Schwarzschild值）；数值积分零测地线；在RP模型下对8颗中子星与4个微类星体的QPO对做Metropolis-Hastings MCMC，比较Schwarzschild、RN、零电荷STVG与完整STVG解。

Result: 增强耦合会加宽俘获截面，但光子球处偏折角的对数发散保持不变；最佳拟合主要出现在中子星样本的带电解，而黑洞样本四模型拟合相当；轨道动力学仅通过组合Meff=(1+α)M和Qeff²=(1+α)(αM²+Q²)依赖M、α、Q，解释了中子星样本的多峰后验，并给出仅凭QPO计时可测量的模型无关上限。

Conclusion: QPO计时对STVG参数的可测性受有效组合量限制；所得约束对推断质量超过TOV极限等天体物理推论具有意义，并对此进行了详细讨论。

Abstract: We study circular geodesics of neutral test particles in the static charged solution of Scalar--Tensor--Vector Gravity (STVG), and we use the twin kilohertz quasiperiodic oscillations (QPOs) of twelve accreting compact objects to bound its parameters. Starting from the effective potential we obtain closed forms for the specific energy, the specific angular momentum and the Keplerian angular velocity, and from these the radial and vertical epicyclic frequencies. The horizon, photon sphere, shadow radius and innermost stable circular orbit are given in closed form as well, and each one reduces to the RN and Schwarzschild value in the appropriate limit. Null geodesics are integrated numerically and show how the enhanced coupling widens the capture cross section while leaving the logarithmic divergence of the deflection angle at the photon sphere intact. Within the RP model we then run Metropolis--Hastings MCMC simulations on the QPO pairs of eight neutron stars and four microquasars, comparing the Schwarzschild spacetime, the RN spacetime, STVG with vanishing charge and the full STVG solution. The best fits are obtained mainly for the charged solutions in the neutron star sample, while all four models describe the black hole sample equally well. We show that the orbital dynamics depends on the mass $M$, the coupling $α$ and the charge $Q$ only through the two combinations $\Meff=(1+α)M$ and $\Qeff^{2}=(1+α)(αM^{2}+Q^{2})$, which accounts for the multimodal posteriors found in the neutron star sample and sets a model-independent limit on what QPO timing alone can measure. The astrophysical implications of these bounds, in particular for inferred masses above the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff limit, are discussed in detail.

</details>


### [167] [Initial conditions for inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09200)
*Gerasimos Kouniatalis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单场机制，通过将暴胀子分解为粗粒化模式与高占据数的相对论非零模式带，利用高斯-哈特里平均产生方差依赖的势能极小值，从而动态地为实现指数平台暴胀准备初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数平台暴胀通常需要特定的初始条件，而如何在单场框架内自然地动态准备这些初始条件仍是一个待解决的问题。

Method: 将暴胀子分解为粗粒化模式与高占据数、近似高斯的相对论非零模式带；对势能进行高斯-哈特里平均，得到依赖于方差的极小值；在平移不变的微正则测度下分析相空间体积的分布，并考察膨胀过程中模式的redshift行为。

Result: 当激发方差较大时，粗粒化场被置于平台高处；相空间体积指数集中于该极小值附近，而均匀动能分数占比为序一的情形呈指数非典型。膨胀期间σ²∝a⁻²、ρχ∝a⁻⁴，诱导极小值快速移向真空，但作用于粗粒化模式的力被指数小因子限制，从而给出速度、位移和动能分数的解析上限。导出了慢滚e折数目的充分条件，并给出超过60个e折的基准。

Conclusion: 该机制自然地为平台暴胀准备初始条件，并预言相关联的有限起始特征：大尺度功率抑制及衰减振荡，以及方差卷积双谱。

Abstract: We propose a single-field mechanism that dynamically prepares the initial conditions required for exponential plateau inflation. The inflaton is decomposed into a coarse-grained mode and a high-occupancy, approximately Gaussian band of relativistic nonzero modes. Gaussian--Hartree averaging of the potential produces an exact variance-dependent minimum, which places the coarse-grained field high on the plateau when the excitation variance is large. Under a translation-invariant microcanonical measure, the available phase-space volume is exponentially concentrated near this minimum, while an order-one homogeneous kinetic-energy fraction is exponentially atypical. During expansion, the preparation modes redshift as radiation, $σ^2\propto a^{-2}$ and $ρ_χ\propto a^{-4}$. Although the induced minimum rapidly moves toward the vacuum, the force acting on the coarse-grained mode is bounded by an exponentially small factor, yielding analytic upper limits on its velocity, displacement, and kinetic fraction. We derive a sufficient condition for the subsequent number of slow-roll e-folds and exhibit a benchmark with more than 60 e-folds. The mechanism also motivates correlated finite-onset signatures: large-scale power suppression with decaying oscillations and a variance-convolution bispectrum.

</details>


### [168] [On The Regularized McVittie Black Bounce and a Family of Traversable Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09238)
*Soumya Chakrabarti,Chiranjeeb Singha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过将McVittie度规的中心奇点与宇宙学奇点分别正则化（r→√(r²+b²)、a→√(a²+a_b²)），提出正则化McVittie度规，该几何可插值于宇宙学黑洞、black bounce与可穿越虫洞之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McVittie度规描述嵌入膨胀宇宙中的自引力致密天体，但固有中心曲率奇点（r→0）与宇宙学奇点（a(t)→0）两类奇点；受black-bounce几何构造启发，尝试同时消除这两类奇点。

Method: 对中心奇点采用r→√(r²+b²)替换，对宇宙学奇点采用非零尺度因子a→√(a²+a_b²)替换，并引入有效非理想流体（具有有限各向异性应力）作为物质源支撑该时空。

Result: 所得时空由有限各向异性应力的有效非理想流体支撑；几何在宇宙学黑洞、black bounce与可穿越虫洞之间过渡；分析了视界（apparent horizon）形成及圆轨道测地线结构。

Conclusion: 该几何可解释为嵌入正则宇宙学背景中、共形演化的Morris-Thorne虫洞，为同时正则化McVittie型时空的中心与宇宙学奇点提供了可行框架。

Abstract: The McVittie metric describes a self-gravitating compact object embedded in an expanding universe. It inherits two singularities: a curvature singularity at $r \to 0$ and a cosmological singularity at $a(t) \to 0$. Motivated by the construction of black-bounce geometries, we regularize both of these singularities and propose a regularized McVittie metric. The central singularity is regularized by replacing $r \to \sqrt{r^{2}+b^{2}}$, leading to a geometry that interpolates between a cosmological black hole, a black bounce, and a traversable wormhole. Similarly, the cosmological singularity is regularized by introducing a non-vanishing scale factor $a \rightarrow \sqrt{a^{2} + a_b^{2}}$. The resulting spacetime is supported by an effective imperfect fluid with finite anisotropic stress. We investigate the formation of the apparent horizon, analyze the circular geodesic structure, and argue that the geometry can also be interpreted as a conformally evolving Morris-Thorne wormhole embedded in a regular cosmological background.

</details>


### [169] [Reconstructing sign-switching dark energy histories: Scalar-field regularity, conditional potential comparison, and representative dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09261)
*Shahnawaz A. Adil,Özgür Akarsu,Mariam Bouhmadi-López,Beñat Ibarra-Uriondo,Nihan Katırcı,J. Alberto Vázquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究现象学相似的符号切换暗能量历史是否必然对应可比的标量场实现，重建了ECDM、SSCDM和LΛCDM三类历史的最小耦合标量场，发现其实现性、端点正则性与可逆性存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号切换（负到正密度穿越）的暗能量历史在现象学层面可能相似，但其标量场实现未必可比。作者希望澄清：给定一个现象学历史，是否存在、以及在何种正则性条件下存在对应的标量场动力学，从而避免把现象学历史直接等同于标量动力学。

Method: 固定动能符号，对三种预设历史（误差函数ECDM、紧凑光滑阶跃SSCDM、阶梯型LΛCDM）进行最小耦合标量场重建；连续负到正密度穿越挑选phantom支；分析各模型的势函数正则性（C^1、非C^2、非Lipschitz力、分布型动能应力）；并进行条件性合成比较（sigmoid–Gaussian族、广义axion-like族）及闭合检验。

Result: ECDM在每个有限红移上给出光滑on-shell势，其状态方程在密度零点仅为比值奇点；精确SSCDM轨迹正则但端点势仅为C^1非C^2，V−V_e∝|φ−φ_e|^{4/3}，非Lipschitz力允许延迟离开冻结平台，故势加平台数据不能唯一生成该历史；精确Ladder需要分布型动能应力，在单场作用量中无普通经典实现。合成比较中ECDM最优为sigmoid–Gaussian族，SSCDM最优为广义axion-like族，但拟合axion指数n<1/2意味着端点力发散。代表性正向解显示负到正标量密度穿越，势零点先于密度零点。闭合检验中，未重调的ECDM最优模板近似追踪目标，max|ΔΩ̃_φ|≃0.16；紧凑SSCDM结构性闭合失败。

Conclusion: 在将现象学暗能量历史解释为标量动力学之前，必须评估场映射的存在性、可逆性与端点正则性；局部Lipschitz力无法从精确冻结初值复现SSCDM的有限时长冻结平台。幻影作用量仅作为均匀有效代理使用。

Abstract: Phenomenologically similar sign-switching dark-energy histories need not have comparable scalar-field realizations. We reconstruct minimally coupled scalars with fixed kinetic sign for three prescribed histories: the error-function model (ECDM), the compact smooth-step model (SSCDM), and the ladder-like model (L$Λ$CDM). Continuous negative-to-positive density crossings select the phantom branch. ECDM yields a smooth on-shell potential at every finite redshift; its equation-of-state pole at the density zero is only a ratio singularity. Exact SSCDM has a regular trajectory but a $C^1$, non-$C^2$ endpoint potential, $V-V_e\propto|φ-φ_e|^{4/3}$. Its non-Lipschitz force permits delayed departures from frozen plateaus, so the reconstructed potential and plateau data do not uniquely generate the prescribed history. The exact Ladder requires distributional kinetic stress and has no ordinary classical realization in the adopted one-field action. In conditional synthetic comparisons, the sigmoid--Gaussian family ranks highest for ECDM and the generalized axion-like family for SSCDM, although the fitted axion exponents $n<1/2$ imply a divergent endpoint force. Representative regular forward solutions exhibit negative-to-positive scalar-density crossings, with the potential zero preceding the density zero. In a closure test, unretuned evolution of the top-ranked ECDM template tracks the target approximately, $\max|Δ\widetildeΩ_φ|\simeq0.16$. Closure fails structurally for compact SSCDM: no potential with a locally Lipschitz force can reproduce its exact finite-duration frozen plateaus from exactly frozen initial data. Field-map existence, invertibility, and endpoint regularity must be assessed before interpreting a phenomenological history as scalar dynamics. The phantom action is used only as a homogeneous effective proxy.

</details>


### [170] [Gravitational quantum friction and the information-loss problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09283)
*Francesco Del Porro,Francesco Di Filippo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在半经典引力框架内，量子物质壳层的坍缩会通过引力耦合与真空负频模发生不可避免的相互作用，产生"引力量子摩擦"，从而阻碍陷阱区形成并将物质信息编码到霍金辐射中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨黑洞信息丢失问题，并尝试在不引入超出半经典引力新物理的前提下，理解坍缩过程中信息如何被处理，以及视界与奇点形成是否可能被阻止。

Method: 将坍缩壳层建模为量子物质的相干态，在半经典引力理论下分析物质与几何的普适引力耦合，研究其与$|in\rangle$真空态中负频模之间的相互作用。

Result: 发现该相互作用在陷阱视界形成后立即不可避免地发生，是半经典引力坍缩的普适特征；能量从坍缩物质转移到出射霍金辐射，表现为"引力量子摩擦"，既阻碍陷阱区域形成，又将坍缩物质携带的信息印刻到发射的量子上。

Conclusion: 该机制对黑洞信息丢失问题具有启示意义，并暗示坍缩可能存在不同于传统视界与奇点形成的终态，即有可能避免视界与奇点的产生。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a shell, modeled as a coherent state of quantum matter. Without adding any physics beyond semiclassical gravity, we show that the universal gravitational coupling between matter and geometry induces an interaction between the shell and the negative-frequency modes present in the $|in \rangle$ vacuum state. We find that this interaction occurs unavoidably immediately after a trapping horizon forms, making it a universal feature of semiclassical gravitational collapse. The resulting transfer of energy from the collapsing matter to outgoing Hawking radiation acts as a \textit{gravitational quantum friction}, opposing the formation of trapped regions while simultaneously imprinting the information carried by the collapsing matter onto the emitted quanta. We discuss the implications of this mechanism for the black hole information-loss problem and speculate about the possible endpoints of collapse, potentially preventing horizon and singularity formation.

</details>


### [171] [Ruling out matter-induced bumps in black-hole effective potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09284)
*Matteo Della Rocca,Romeo Felice Rosato,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文指出，黑洞周围各向异性流体所产生的有效势次级隆起，往往伴随奇异物质分布或发散的流体特征速度，因而是非物理的；且次级隆起并不意味着时空中存在额外光环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质场可能在黑洞周围局域分布，使黑洞扰动有效势出现小的次级隆起，进而可能引发准正规模谱不稳定性与晚期回波。本文旨在检验这类次级隆起所对应的物质构型是否物理，并厘清次级隆起与额外光环之间的关系。

Method: 采用物理上合理的各向异性流体模型，对球对称黑洞周围的物质分布进行分析，考察有效势出现次级隆起时流体的物质分布与特征速度行为，并检验其与光环结构的关系。

Result: 有效势次级隆起的出现总是伴随奇异物质分布或流体特征速度发散，表明相应物质构型为非物理；同时，次级隆起并不必然对应时空中存在额外光环。

Conclusion: 基于各向异性流体模型，产生次级隆起的物理物质构型难以实现，且次级隆起与额外光环之间不存在必然联系，这对以往将次级隆起与谱不稳定性、晚期回波乃至额外光环相关联的论证构成重要约束。

Abstract: Matter fields localized around black holes can produce small secondary bumps in the effective potentials governing black-hole perturbations, potentially leading to quasinormal-mode spectral instabilities and late-time echoes. Considering physically motivated models of anisotropic fluids surrounding a spherically symmetric black hole, we show that the appearance of such a secondary bump is accompanied by either singular matter profiles or divergent characteristic speeds in the fluid. These features indicate that the corresponding matter configurations are unphysical. We further demonstrate that a secondary bump in the effective potential does not necessarily imply the existence of additional light rings in the spacetime.

</details>


### [172] [Gravitational Radiation from Shockwave Scattering in the Self-Force EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09378)
*Emanuele Rosi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究自作用有效场论（Self-Force EFT）框架下两无自旋天体的高能碰撞，将其中一体视为无质量高能粒子并产生引力冲击波（GSW）背景，另一体作为微扰。作者选取使EFT充分展开的参考系，将计算推进至1SF阶并猜想特定规范下反冲算符的全体阶消失。结果给出冲击波背景上引力子传播子的精确形式，并在无质量-无质量碰撞情形下计算了动量空间1SF阶在壳波形，证明其在超普朗克区领头阶的重求和形式为零，进而证明与冲击波共线方向辐射能量角谱的紫外有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能双体碰撞中，当一个天体极轻且极高能时会产生引力冲击波背景，是研究普朗克尺度附近引力散射与辐射的重要场景。然而自作用展开依赖参考系，且如何在自作用有效场论中系统处理冲击波背景、获得可在牛顿常数各阶成立的结果并揭示其重求和结构，仍是需要解决的问题。

Method: 在自作用有效场论框架下，取一体为无质量高能粒子以生成引力冲击波背景，另一微扰体满足 E2/E1≪1（无质量）或 m2/E1≪1（有质量）。选取使EFT充分展现威力的参考系，计算推进至1SF阶，并猜想特定规范下反冲算符的全体阶消失；求解冲击波背景上的精确引力子传播子，用于计算动量空间1SF阶在壳波形，并专注于无质量-无质量碰撞情形。

Result: 获得冲击波背景上引力子传播子的精确表达式，与已有结果一致；在无质量-无质量碰撞中计算出动量空间1SF阶在壳波形，该波形在Post-Minkowskian展开的所有阶成立，并证明其重求和形式在超普朗克区领头阶消失；利用该结果证明在与冲击波共线方向、能量为E1的区域中辐射能量角谱的紫外有限性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了自作用有效场论在处理引力冲击波碰撞中的有效性，通过合适参考系选择获得牛顿常数各阶结果并揭示重求和规律；反冲算符在给定规范下的全体阶消失猜想、传播子的精确解以及波形的紫外有限性结论，为高能引力散射与辐射的精确计算提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We investigate the high-energy collision of two spinless bodies within the Self-Force Effective Field Theory, taking one of the two bodies to be massless and highly energetic, such that it generates a gravitational shockwave (GSW) background. The GSW is perturbed by a second body, either massless or massive, satisfying respectively $E_2/E_1\ll1$ or $m_2/E_1\ll1$. Although the Self-Force expansion for shockwaves is frame-dependent, we choose a frame in which this EFT manifests its full power, obtaining results valid to all orders in Newton's constant that make the underlying resummation patterns manifest. Specifically, we push the expansion to 1SF order and conjecture the all-order vanishing of the so-called recoil operators in a given gauge. We obtain the exact graviton propagator on the shockwave background, matching previous results, and use it to compute the on-shell waveform at 1SF order in momentum space, specialising to the massless-massless collision. The waveform is computed to all orders in the Post-Minkowskian expansion, and its resummed form proved to vanish at the leading order in the super-Planckian regime. We use this result to prove the UV finiteness of the angular spectrum of radiated energy in the region collinear to the shockwave with energy $E_1$.

</details>


### [173] [Perturbations of Charged Black Holes with Higher-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09419)
*Oleksandr S. Stashko,Roman A. Konoplya,Mark P. Hertzberg*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究含高阶导数修正（黎曼张量与电磁场强的四导数Horndeski组合）下带电磁荷黑洞的线性引力扰动与准正规模谱，系统分析稳定性条件，数值确认物理允许参数区内无指数增长模式，并揭示高阶相互作用导致的谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验高阶曲率-电磁耦合修正下黑洞解的稳定性，确定经典不稳定性（指数增长模式）是否出现，并刻画其准正规模谱。

Method: 在Einstein-Hilbert作用量中加入黎曼张量与电磁场强的唯一Horndeski四导数组合（保持运动方程二阶），对带电磁荷黑洞的线性引力扰动做数值求解，得到准正规模谱并扫描物理允许参数空间。

Result: 给出完整稳定性条件，数值证明在整个物理允许且理论无病态的参量区内不存在指数增长模式；准正规模谱呈现若干由高阶相互作用引起的独特特征。

Conclusion: 该类高阶曲率-电磁耦合在物理允许参数空间内不引入经典不稳定性，但显著改变准正规模谱，为引力波ringdown观测检验高阶引力修正提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study linear gravitational perturbations and the corresponding quasinormal-mode spectra of electrically and magnetically charged black holes in the presence of higher-order corrections to the Einstein-Hilbert action. In particular, we consider a four-derivative interaction that couples the Riemann tensor to the electromagnetic field strength in the unique (Horndeski) combination that preserves second-order equations of motion. We provide a comprehensive analysis of the stability conditions. We numerically confirm the absence of exponentially growing modes throughout the physically admissible region of parameter space where the theory remains free of pathologies. We then investigate in detail the properties of the quasinormal-mode spectra and identify several distinctive features induced by the higher-order interaction.

</details>


### [174] [Space-Based Lock-In Determination of Newton's Gravitational Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09485)
*Ulrich D. Jentschura*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种优化空间引力常数测量方案，结合无扰动环境、锁相技术、零坐标与协方差方法，用于双通道数据统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期突破性空间牛顿引力常数测量启发，旨在设计优化的空间实验布局，融合无扰动环境与锁相、零坐标、协方差方法优势以提高测量精度。

Method: 采用协方差与交叉协方差方法对来自两个不同实验通道的数据进行优化统计分析；第一通道基于静态源质量引力场中近似恒定加速度，第二通道采用基于测试质量与源质量振荡坐标的锁相测量；并分析任务参数。

Result: 分析了源质量最优尺度与现役空间发射系统发射能力相关的可能任务参数，并确定了地球同步轨道上一个基本无扰动的候选位置。

Conclusion: 该方案为优化空间牛顿引力常数测量提供了可行布局，协方差方法与双通道设计结合，配合地球同步轨道候选位置，具备实现潜力。

Abstract: Inspired by a recent ground-breaking space-based measurement of Newton's gravitational constant, we outline a proposal for a general layout of an optimized space-based approach that combines the advantages of a largely disturbance-free environment with lock-in techniques, null coordinates and the method of covariances. The method of covariances, and cross-covariances, is used for an optimized statistical analysis of data originating from two distinct experimental channels. One of two distinct measurement channels relies on an approximately constant acceleration in the gravitational field of static sources masses, while the second channel involves a lock-in measurement based on test and source masses with oscillating coordinates. Conceivable mission parameters are analyzed in terms of the optimum scale for the source masses and launch capabilities of currently available space launch systems. A candidate location for a largely disturbance-free position in geostationary orbit is identified.

</details>


### [175] [Shadows and gravitational perturbations of black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09487)
*Alana C. L. Santos,Leandro A. Lessa,Roberto V. Maluf,Gonzalo J. Olmo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究各向异性流体支持的black-bounce几何的轴向引力扰动与阴影：有视界解与黑洞几乎不可区分；无视界对称解可产生回波和多光子环，无视界非对称解则接近Reissner–Nordström。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验black-bounce（正则化黑洞/虫洞）几何在引力波与光学观测上是否可与标准黑洞区分，明确视界、对称性与反弹参数对扰动谱和阴影的影响。

Method: 在广义相对论框架下，对各向异性流体支持的两类解（对称Simpson–Visser型black bounce与Reissner–Nordström非对称变形）计算轴向引力扰动，分析有效势垒结构、准正规模、引力波回波与光子球/阴影，并讨论eikonal对应关系。

Result: 有视界（对称或非对称）组态呈单势垒，准正规模与标准黑洞几乎无法区分；无视界对称组态可出现多势垒并产生回波，其振幅与间隔敏感依赖反弹参数a与密度参数ρ0；无视界非对称解势垒正则化、无回波，现象学接近Reissner–Nordström。光学上，有视界解仅有一个相关光子球，阴影与黑洞几乎一致；无视界对称解可有多个光子环，无视界非对称解可有单个或无光子环。

Conclusion: 有视界情形下标准eikonal对应（准正规模与圆零测地线不稳定性）成立；无视界情形因多势垒阻碍该关系直接推广，但波与光学部门的强相似性提示仍可能存在有意义的唯象对应。

Abstract: We investigate axial gravitational perturbations and shadow formation in black-bounce geometries supported by anisotropic fluids within general relativity. Two distinct classes of solutions are analyzed: a symmetric Simpson--Visser-like black bounce and an asymmetric deformation of the Reissner--Nordström metric, which can feature either a bounded interior region or an unbounded wormhole-like extension. Our findings indicate that configurations with horizons, whether symmetric or asymmetric, exhibit single-barrier effective potentials and quasinormal frequencies nearly indistinguishable from those of standard black holes. In contrast, horizonless symmetric configurations may develop multiple potential barriers, giving rise to gravitational-wave echoes whose amplitude and separation depend sensitively on the bounce parameter $a$ and the density parameter $ρ_0$. Horizonless asymmetric solutions, on the other hand, exhibit regularized effective potentials that do not produce echoes and remain phenomenologically close to the Reissner--Nordström case. From the optical perspective, both classes of solutions with horizons exhibit a single relevant photon sphere and shadow profiles nearly indistinguishable from those of their black-hole counterparts, while horizonless symmetric configurations can sustain multiple photon rings. Horizonless asymmetric configurations may instead support either a single photon ring or none. Finally, we recover the standard eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and the instability of circular null geodesics for configurations with horizons. For horizonless geometries, however, the emergence of multiple potential barriers prevents a straightforward extension of this relation, although the strong similarity between the wave and optical sectors suggests that a meaningful phenomenological correspondence may still persist.

</details>


### [176] [From driven oscillations to free ringdown: a particle plunging into Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09531)
*Colin Weller,Andrew Laeuger,Fulin Li,Neil Lu,Rowina S. Nathan,Sizheng Ma,Yanbei Chen,Ling Sun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明ringdown波形可呈现常系数QNM叠加，尽管各QNM极点贡献仍被源驱动；基于Kerr格林函数的理性近似，发现各极点贡献先跟随源的瞬时复频率，源衰减更快后才解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为ringdown中QNM叠加意味着各模式已从源解耦，即"自由"振荡。作者质疑这一物理图像，探究能否在单个QNM极点贡献仍被源驱动的情况下，波形却表现为常系数QNM叠加。

Method: 从Kerr格林函数的QNM极点、物理留数及视界频率零点构造first-principles理性近似；对自ISCO向内旋进的粒子，取Kerr自旋0.5-0.9，逐项分析各QNM极点贡献随时间演化。

Result: 每个QNM极点贡献先跟随源的瞬时复频率，仅当源衰减快于对应模式后才解耦；晚期源趋于第三及更高视界频率的衰减振荡之和；格林函数零点在相干波形中抵消这些源频率成分，而持续驱动生成QNM频率振荡。

Conclusion: 看似自由的QNM叠加可在各贡献尚未与源动态解耦前浮现，说明ringdown中常系数QNM表象不必然对应模式已物理解耦。

Abstract: We show that a ringdown waveform can admit a constant-coefficient quasinormal-mode (QNM) representation while its individual QNM-pole contributions remain driven. To establish this result, we construct a first-principles rational approximation to the Kerr Green's function from its QNM poles, physical residues, and horizon-frequency zeros. For particles plunging from the innermost stable circular orbit into Kerr black holes with spins 0.5-0.9, each QNM-pole contribution initially follows the source's instantaneous complex frequency and decouples only after the source decays faster than the corresponding mode. At late times, the source approaches a sum of damped oscillations at the third and higher horizon frequencies. The corresponding zeros in the Green's function cancel these source-frequency components in the coherent waveform, while the continuing drive generates oscillations at QNM frequencies. Thus, an apparently free QNM superposition can emerge before its individual contributions dynamically decouple from the source.

</details>


### [177] [Probing quantum chaos near a wormhole throat with a circular string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09613)
*Ai-chen Li,Xin-Fei Li,Xuanting Ji*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究环形探针弦穿越虫洞喉部时量子涨落是否呈现量子混沌响应，通过世界面作用量二阶展开、正则量子化与OTOC计算，在Ellis–Bronnikov虫洞及整体单极子扩展中提取有限时间内的指数增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弯曲时空中量子探针能否直接刻画量子混沌，并构建不依赖全息对偶的可迁移诊断框架，检验虫洞喉部穿越过程中横向极化模的动力学敏感性。

Method: 环形弦嵌入经典上周期且径向稳定，但两个物理横向极化经历含时潮汐势；将世界面作用量展开至二阶后对模正则量子化，由不等时对易子构造OTOC振幅，并在Ellis–Bronnikov虫洞与整体单极子扩展中数值提取增长率。

Result: Ellis–Bronnikov虫洞中两种极化在首次穿越喉部附近均出现有限区间的近似指数OTOC增长；无量纲增长率在参数范围内为正，且随探针能量相对喉部尺度增大而总体下降。整体单极子扩展中，立体角亏损增大压缩局部可放大模带并抑制增长率，强缺陷可近乎淬灭径向信号；能量-喉部尺度比小时角向通道保留极化相关的非单调结构。

Conclusion: 所得量刻画的是高斯涨落扇区中的有限时间动力学敏感性，不应等同于渐近多体混沌或热力学相变；该构造仅依赖协变世界面涨落与实时对易子，为在弯曲时空中直接对量子探针施加量子混沌诊断提供了可迁移的非全息框架。

Abstract: We investigate whether quantum fluctuations of a circular probe string develop a quantum-chaotic response while traversing a wormhole throat. The classical circular-string embedding is periodic and radially stable, but its two physical transverse polarizations experience time-dependent tidal potentials. Expanding the world-sheet action to quadratic order, we canonically quantize these modes and construct out-of-time-ordered correlator(OTOC) amplitudes from their unequal-time commutators. For the Ellis--Bronnikov wormhole, both polarizations exhibit finite intervals of approximately exponential OTOC growth associated with the first throat passage. The corresponding dimensionless rate measured with respect to physical time is positive over the parameter range studied and generally decreases as the probe energy is increased relative to the throat scale. In the global-monopole extension, increasing the solid-angle deficit narrows the band of locally amplifiable modes and suppresses the extracted rates; a sufficiently strong defect can nearly quench the radial signal, while the angular channel retains a polarization-dependent non-monotonic structure when the energy-to-throat-scale ratio is small. These quantities characterize finite-time dynamical sensitivity in the Gaussian fluctuation sector and should not be identified with asymptotic many-body chaos or a thermodynamic phase transition. Although the numerical analysis uses two representative wormhole geometries, the construction depends only on covariant world-sheet fluctuations and real-time commutators. It therefore provides a transferable, non-holographic framework for applying quantum-chaos diagnostics directly to quantum probes in curved spacetimes.

</details>


### [178] [Long-lived resonances of massive scalar fields in the Reissner-Nordström black-hole spacetime: Analytic treatment in the large-mass regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10521)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在无量纲大质量 Mμ≫1 区域，对复合的 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞-大质量标量场系统进行解析研究，证明存在准共振（寿命任意长的线性化微扰模），并解析给出黑洞-场临界质量谱 {Mμ_crit(Q̄)}。


<details>
