  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电黑洞（Reissner-Nordström）与大质量标量场组成的复合系统在大质量区间的物理与数学性质，特别是长寿命（准共振）模及临界质量谱的刻画。

Method: 采用解析技术，在无量纲大质量 Mμ≫1 区域分析系统的线性化微扰模；固定无量纲电荷参数 Q̄≡Q/M，解析确定临界质量谱 {Mμ_crit(Q̄)}。

Result: 证明对给定 Q̄，系统存在准共振——寿命任意长的线性化微扰模；并解析求得描述长寿命共振的黑洞-场临界质量谱 {Mμ_crit(Q̄)}。

Conclusion: 带电黑洞-大质量标量场复合系统在大质量区具有准共振特征，其长寿命共振由临界质量谱 {Mμ_crit(Q̄)} 刻画。

Abstract: The physical and mathematical properties of the composed Reissner-Nordström-black-hole-massive-scalar-field system are studied {\it analytically} in the dimensionless large-mass $Mμ\gg1$ regime [here $\{M,μ\}$ are respectively the mass of the central black hole and the proper mass of the scalar field]. It is proved that, for a given value ${\bar Q}\equiv Q/M$ of the dimensionless charge parameter of the central black hole, the system is characterized by the presence of quasi-resonances, linearized perturbation modes with arbitrarily long lifetimes. In particular, using analytical techniques, we determine the black-hole-field critical mass spectrum $\{Mμ_{\text{crit}}({\bar Q})\}$ which characterizes the long-lived resonances of the composed physical system.

</details>


### [179] [Ovcharenko-Podolsky Einstein-Maxwell gravitational instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09694)
*Hari K. Kunduri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类三参数toric Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子，为满足Riemannian Einstein-Maxwell方程的完备、标量曲率消失的Riemannian流形，具有两个渐近端及体内non-collapsing二圈（bolt）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子这类完备Riemannian流形解，探索其整体几何结构与渐近行为。

Method: 通过对Ovcharenko-Podolsky构造的局部Lorentzian黑洞几何族进行解析延拓，并适当延拓，得到整体度规。

Result: 给出显式的三参数toric Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子族；度规可延拓至具有两个渐近端的空间，渐近端微分同胚于$\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$，体内存在non-collapsing二圈（bolt）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了新的显式Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子解，揭示了其两端渐近结构与bolt的拓扑特征。

Abstract: We present an explicit three-parameter family of toric Einstein-Maxwell gravitational instantons. These are complete Riemannian manifolds with vanishing scalar curvature which satisfy the Riemannian Einstein-Maxwell equations. We argue that the metric can be extended to a space with two asymptotic ends diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$ with a non-collapsing two-cycle (bolt) in the bulk. The global metric is produced by suitably extending an analytic continuation of a local family of Lorentzian black hole geometries constructed by Ovcharenko-Podolsky.

</details>


### [180] [Analytic motions of spinning particles in Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09755)
*Rui-Hui Li,Xing-You Zhou,Chao-Jun Feng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在MPD形式框架下（自旋线性阶），研究静态球对称带宇宙学常数时空中自旋试验粒子的运动，通过自旋形变五次多项式的实根分类，得到SAdS与SdS时空中允许轨道类型，并用Lauricella超几何函数给出径向运动解析表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙学常数与粒子自旋对试验粒子轨道结构的联合影响，特别是超越Stuchlík极限（无自旋粒子不存在束缚轨道）时自旋粒子是否仍可束缚。

Method: 采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon (MPD) 形式，保留自旋线性项；利用背景对称性将径向与纬向动力学约化为一阶方程，由自旋形变五次多项式控制；对五次多项式实根结构作完全分类；径向运动分段用Lauricella超几何函数表示，以正实根为分支割线；纬向运动为赤道面附近小振动，其相位由同一五次多项式给出解析表示。

Result: 给出五次多项式实根结构的完整分类，直接导出Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter与Schwarzschild-de Sitter几何中自旋粒子的允许轨道类型；分析根结构与稳定圆轨道区间对参数的依赖；发现当宇宙学常数超过Stuchlík极限时，自旋粒子仍可保持束缚轨道。

Conclusion: 自旋会显著改变带宇宙学常数时空中的轨道结构，可在无自旋情形无束缚轨道的参数区域维持束缚运动；径向与纬向运动均可借助五次多项式的根与Lauricella超几何函数获得解析描述。

Abstract: The motion of a spinning test particle in a static, spherically symmetric spacetime with a cosmological constant is studied within the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon formalism, working to linear order in the particle's spin. By exploiting the symmetries of the background, the radial and latitudinal dynamics are reduced to first-order equations governed by a spin-deformed quintic polynomial. A complete classification of the real root structure of this quintic is carried out, from which the allowed orbital types for spinning particles in the Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter and Schwarzschild-de Sitter geometries follow directly. The parameter dependence of the root structure and of the stable circular orbit interval is analyzed. In particular, for values of the cosmological constant beyond the Stuchlík limit, where spinless particles admit no bound orbits, spinning particles can still remain bound. The radial motion is expressed piecewise in terms of Lauricella hypergeometric functions, with the simple positive real roots of the quintic acting as branch cuts. The latitudinal motion consists of small oscillations about an equatorial plane, and its phase, governed by the same quintic, admits an analogous analytic representation.

</details>


### [181] [Thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09816)
*Zelin Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将中性及特殊带电Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞的热力学分析推广至一般Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族，其中电场参数与外场参数相互独立，利用协变表面荷与正则可积方法确定了角动量、质量及热力学势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞的热力学研究仅涵盖中性或特殊带电情形，电场参数与外场参数未独立。为建立更一般的描述，需要将分析推广至电场与外场参数彼此独立的一般Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族，以检验热力学定律与Smarr公式在该情形下是否仍然成立。

Method: 采用协变表面荷（covariant surface charges）与正则可积性（canonical integrability）方法。总角动量由引力与电磁表面荷的联合贡献解析给出；由于与坐标时间平移对应的无穷小荷在解空间中不可积，质量需关联于更一般的对称生成元。对该生成元施加正则可积条件，并以Kerr-Newman质量作为零场边界条件，从而确定质量与相应热力学势。

Result: 角动量可由引力与电磁表面荷的组合解析求得；质量生成元的无穷小荷不可积，故质量须由更一般的生成元给出。可积性条件结合Kerr-Newman零场边界条件唯一地选定Christodoulou-Ruffini质量，并确定相应热力学势。第一定律与Smarr公式形式与通常Kerr-Newman情形相同，两个先前研究的Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson情形作为特殊极限被重现。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞热力学成功推广至电场与外场参数独立的一般Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族，证明第一定律与Smarr公式仍保持Kerr-Newman形式，并统一包含了先前的中性与特殊带电结果作为特例，表明Christodoulou-Ruffini质量在该广义背景下具有正则可积性基础。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the thermodynamic analyses of the neutral and specially charged Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes to the general Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson family, in which the electric and external-field parameters are independent. Using covariant surface charges and canonical integrability methods, we determine the total angular momentum and the canonical mass. The angular momentum follows analytically from the combined gravitational and electromagnetic surface charges. However, the infinitesimal charge associated with coordinate time translations is not integrable in solution space, so the mass must be associated with a more general symmetry generator. Imposing the canonical integrability conditions on this generator, together with the Kerr-Newman mass as the zero-field boundary condition, selects the Christodoulou-Ruffini mass and determines the associated thermodynamic potentials. The first law and Smarr formula take the same form as the ones in usual Kerr-Newman case, and the two previously studied Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson cases are recovered as special limits.

</details>


### [182] [Clock-noise propagation and calibration for phase-locking configurations in space-based gravitational-wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09820)
*Pan-Pan Wang,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将时钟噪声传播与校准直接建立在主从式激光锁相构型上，从载波与边带读出推导时钟噪声耦合，构造保序边带校准规则，并在冻结等臂模型下比较锁相与非锁相实现的残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测中，激光锁相把弱接收光的相位转移到本地激光，并维持星间外差频率在相位计带宽内。星上超稳振荡器的涨落会向这些外差相位测量引入时钟噪声，TDI中需额外校准。现有基于六路独立单向测量的表述没有显式刻画主从锁相的共参考时钟结构，因此需要针对该构型重新建立时钟噪声传播与校准框架。

Method: 从载波与边带读出出发，直接针对主从锁相构型推导时钟噪声耦合，构造有序的边带校准规则；该规则保留非对易延迟，适用于满足传播闭合与时钟系数闭合的两支路TDI组合。对相互独立、分数频率噪声谱相同的星上时钟，比较锁相与非锁相下的匹配实现。

Result: 在冻结等臂模型中，最终时钟校准前，所考察的最多16条链路、45个第二代组合在0.1–10 mHz全频段内锁相实现的时钟噪声残差更小。两个代表性16链路组合的数值模拟复现了解析谱，并验证了预测的噪声降低与校准效果。

Conclusion: 锁相实现可在最终校准前降低时钟噪声贡献，但主激光相位噪声的消除仍依赖TDI。

Abstract: In space-based gravitational-wave detectors, laser phase locking transfers the phase of weak received light to a local laser and maintains the interspacecraft heterodyne frequencies within the phasemeter bandwidth. Fluctuations of the onboard ultrastable oscillators introduce clock noise into these heterodyne phase measurements, requiring additional calibration in time-delay interferometry (TDI). Existing formulations based on six independently formed one-way measurements do not explicitly describe the common-reference clock structure of a master--slave phase-locking configuration. In this work, we formulate clock-noise propagation and calibration directly for this configuration. Starting from carrier and sideband readouts, we derive the clock-noise couplings and construct an ordered sideband-calibration rule. The rule retains noncommuting delays and applies to two-branch TDI combinations satisfying propagation and clock-coefficient closure. For mutually independent onboard clocks with identical fractional-frequency noise spectra, we compare matched realizations with and without phase locking. In the adopted frozen equal-arm model, 45 second-generation combinations with up to 16 links considered here have smaller clock-noise residuals in the phase-locking realization throughout 0.1--10~mHz, before final clock calibration. Numerical simulations of two representative 16-link combinations reproduce the analytical spectra and verify the predicted reduction and calibration. These results show that the phase-locking realizations reduce the clock-noise contribution before final calibration, while the cancellation of master-laser phase noise still relies on TDI.

</details>


### [183] [Prospects of electromagnetic follow-up of neutron star-black hole mergers in the LIGO-India era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09926)
*Yogita Kumari,Kanchan Soni,Sanjit Mitra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究LIGO-India时代NSBH并合的千新星电磁对应体探测前景，提出基于事件定制的Rubin曝光时间优化策略，可将GW+EM联合探测率提升约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NSBH并合是重要的多信使源，但仅部分能产生可探测电磁对应体，依赖于质量比、黑洞自旋和中子星物态方程。LIGO-India加入后网络 duty cycle 与定位精度提升，需要评估其对千新星探测率的增益，并优化Rubin后续观测策略。

Method: 基于GW230529启发的NSBH族群，考虑两个有效潮汐形变范围；估算LHV与LHVA（含LIGO-India）网络的引力波探测数；用Rubin建模千新星可探测性；对比固定曝光策略并提出基于预期对应体亮度和定位面积的事件定制曝光时间优化策略。

Result: 加入LIGO-India约使EM后续观测数翻倍，主要因duty cycle提升与中位定位面积减小，且大幅增加远距离探测。固定曝光在数百秒后EM探测率饱和。定制策略下LHVA的GW+EM年探测率：乐观族群4.19(-3.58,+9.66)、保守族群0.79(-0.68,+1.83)；LHV对应为1.46(-1.25,+3.37)和0.27(-0.23,+0.62)。所需Rubin后续时间在ToO配额内。

Conclusion: 未来十年内有望实现少数NSBH后续观测，其中部分位于红移约0.1–0.2，可用于测量哈勃常数并探测哈勃参数，LIGO-India与事件定制曝光策略是关键增益来源。

Abstract: Neutron star-black hole (NSBH) mergers are promising multimessenger sources, but only a subset is expected to produce detectable electromagnetic (EM) counterparts, depending on the binary mass ratio, black hole (BH) spin, and neutron star (NS) equation of state (EoS). We investigate the prospects for detecting kilonova counterparts to NSBH mergers in the LIGO-India era using the Vera C. Rubin Observatory (Rubin). We simulate GW230529-motivated NSBH populations with two effective tidal deformability ranges, estimate gravitational-wave detections for the LHV (LIGO-Livingston, LIGO-Hanford, Virgo) and LHVA (LHV with LIGO-India at Aundha) detector networks, and model the detectability of the associated kilonova emission with Rubin. We find that adding LIGO-India approximately doubles the number of EM follow-ups, primarily by increasing the duty cycle and reducing the median sky-localization area, and notably increases the number of such detections at large distances. For fixed-exposure strategies, the EM detection rate saturates at exposure times of a few hundred seconds, reflecting the trade-off between depth and sky coverage. We therefore introduce an event-specific exposure-time optimization strategy based on the expected counterpart brightness and sky-localization area. With this strategy, the expected NSBH GW+EM detection rate per year is 4.19(-3.58,+9.66) for the optimistic and 0.79(-0.68,+1.83) for the conservative population with the LHVA network, compared to 1.46(-1.25,+3.37) and 0.27(-0.23,+0.62), respectively, for LHV. The required Rubin follow-up time remains within the expected Target-of-Opportunity allocation. These results show that in the next decade, a few such NSBH follow-ups may take place, with a fraction of them in the redshift range of about 0.1-0.2, which will not only be useful for measuring the Hubble constant but can also help us in probing the Hubble parameter.

</details>


### [184] [Revealing the Quantum Signature of Gravity via Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09931)
*Partha Nandi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立统一理论框架，证明经典引力波只能使探测器退相干，而量子化引力波可诱导纠缠等非经典关联，从而作为引力量子性的操作性探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨传播中的引力波能否作为探测引力量子本质的操作性探针，并需要在同一物理框架内直接比较引力辐射的经典与量子描述。

Method: 从线性化广义相对论中的测地偏离方程出发，由时空几何导出有效探测器哈密顿量，构建探测器子系统与经典及量子化引力波场相互作用的完整量子动力学，统一融合时空几何、量子信息与引力波物理。

Result: 经典引力波背景会使探测器态产生混合性，但无法在探测器子系统间生成真正的量子关联；量子化引力波则可相干地媒介引力诱导纠缠、量子相干性、量子记忆与非经典关联。

Conclusion: 这些非经典关联为传播引力辐射的量子本质提供了清晰的操作性signature，并指出介观量子机械振子是有望实验探测这些效应的途径，从而确立了基于几何与量子信息的框架来研究引力量子性。

Abstract: Can propagating gravitational waves serve as operational probes of the quantum nature of gravity? We address this question by developing a unified theoretical framework that combines spacetime geometry, quantum information, and gravitational-wave physics. Starting from the geodesic deviation equation in linearized General Relativity, we derive the effective detector Hamiltonian directly from spacetime geometry and construct the complete quantum dynamics for detector subsystems interacting with both classical and quantized propagating gravitational-wave fields. This unified formulation enables a direct comparison between classical and quantum descriptions of gravitational radiation within the same physical framework. We demonstrate that classical gravitational-wave backgrounds can induce mixedness in the detector state but cannot generate genuine quantum correlations between the detector subsystems. In contrast, quantized gravitational waves coherently mediate gravity-induced entanglement, quantum coherence, quantum memory, and nonclassical correlations, providing clear operational signatures of the quantum nature of propagating gravitational radiation. We further discuss how mesoscopic quantum mechanical oscillators offer a promising route towards experimentally probing these effects. Our results establish a geometric and quantum-information-based framework for exploring quantum gravity through propagating gravitational waves.

</details>


### [185] [Critical Scalarization for a Self-Gravitating Bosonic Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09991)
*Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan,De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在一个自引力玻色凝聚体与标量响应场耦合的模型中，建立了标量化的最小非相对论实现，揭示了由局域有效质量移动与非线性饱和产生的双重相变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量化通常出现在相对论致密天体语境中，本文旨在寻找其非相对论对应实现，并统一理解不同物理系统中的共同机制。

Method: 构建自引力玻色凝聚体耦合标量响应场的非相对论模型，引入局域有效质量移动与非线性饱和项，分析多分支结构及两类转变路径的动力学。

Result: 在线性稳定区，有限扰动驱动具有I型对数标度的第一类相变；越过线性临界点后，小扰动呈快子式增长，响应时间同时控制增长与随后的呼吸振荡动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了相对论致密天体与非相对论凝聚体中标量化的共同机制，并指向相干介质中的实验室模拟。

Abstract: We establish a minimal nonrelativistic realization of scalarization in a self-gravitating bosonic condensate coupled to a scalar response field. A local effective-mass shift and nonlinear saturation generate a multibranch structure with two distinct transition routes. In the linearly stable regime, a finite perturbation drives a first-order transition with type-I logarithmic scaling near threshold. Beyond the linear onset, small perturbations grow tachyonically, and the response time controls both the growth and the subsequent breathing dynamics. These results identify a common mechanism for scalarization across relativistic compact objects and nonrelativistic condensates, and point toward laboratory analogues in coherent media.

</details>


### [186] [Black hole and wormhole branches in gravitational decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10102)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Y. Gomez-Leyton,Vitalii Vertogradov,Jean Baez Cuevas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在最小几何形变(MGD)框架下，固定解耦函数h(r)后形变由耦合强度k控制；当形变出现越过种子视界的简单最外根r*时，临界耦合两侧对应两个互斥的全局完备化分支，分别给出黑洞外部与双端虫洞，并用二阶同调群区分二者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MGD方法由Schwarzschild黑洞种子生成单参数形变族F(r;k)=1+k h(r)，但形变根的位置可能改变时空的全局结构与因果性质。作者希望澄清：当形变根越过种子视界时，形变度规是否仍是黑洞的"内部修正"，以及不同k值究竟对应何种全局完备化。核心动机是为黑洞与虫洞两个分支寻找严格的拓扑/几何判据，避免对单一时空拓扑变化的模糊表述。

Method: 采用静态球对称MGD：用θ-扇区场方程加物态方程解出解耦函数h(r)，固定h(r)后以k为唯一参数。分析F(r;k)=1+k h(r)的最外简单根r*，考察其相对种子视界r=2M的位置。对r*>2M情形，证明区间(2M,r*)上洛伦兹号差丢失，从而不存在穿越种子视界的光滑外延；通过切除(2M,r*)并沿最小球面T={r=r*}对r≥r*区域作加倍，构造光滑洛伦兹完备（双端虫洞）。最后计算两个完备化相对视界/整体流形的二阶同调群作为离散不变量。

Result: 证明当最外简单根r*>2M时，区间(2M,r*)上洛伦兹号差必然丢失，因此无法从种子视界r=2M光滑延拓外部度规。据此，静态球对称类内对应的光滑洛伦兹完备为双端虫洞：切除(2M,r*)，将r≥r*区域在最小球面T处加倍得到。k>k_c与k<k_c对应两个不同的、非微分同胚的流形，而非单一时空的拓扑变化。二阶同调群给出离散判据：黑洞外部相对视界H_2(Σ_BH,ℋ)=0，虫洞流形H_2(Σ_WH)≅ℤ。

Conclusion: 固定解耦函数h(r)时，临界耦合k_c两侧的形变属于两个互斥的全局完备化：一侧保持种子视界为黑洞，另一侧因最外根落在外部而迫使区间(2M,r*)失去洛伦兹号差，只能以切除加加倍的方式得到双端虫洞。度规本身（Lemma 1）决定给定k下哪个完备化是容许的；无需声明任何单一时空的拓扑变化。H_2(Σ_BH,ℋ)=0与H_2(Σ_WH)≅ℤ构成区分两分支的离散拓扑不变量。

Abstract: Minimal Geometric Deformation (MGD) applied to a static Schwarzschild black hole seed generates a single decoupler function $h(r)$, obtained by solving the $θ$-sector field equations together with an equation of state. Once $h(r)$ is fixed, the resulting one-parameter family is controlled by the coupling strength $k$ through $F(r;k)=1+k\,h(r)$. We show that, whenever the deformation develops a simple outermost root that crosses the seed horizon, the same fixed decoupler leads to two mutually exclusive branches associated with different global completions: on one side of the critical coupling the deformed metric preserves the seed horizon as a black hole, whereas on the other side the root $r_*>2M$ lies in the exterior and cannot be interpreted as an interior modification of the black hole geometry. We prove that this root forces a loss of Lorentzian signature on the interval $(2M,r_*)$, so that no smooth extension of the exterior metric through the seed horizon $r=2M$ exists once $r_*$ lies outside it. Within the static, spherically symmetric class considered here, the corresponding smooth Lorentzian completion is a two-ended wormhole obtained by excising $(2M,r_*)$ and doubling the region $r\geq r_*$ across the minimal sphere $\mathcal T=\{r=r_*\}$. No topology change of any single spacetime is claimed or required: $k>k_c$ and $k<k_c$ simply correspond to two different, non-diffeomorphic manifolds, and Lemma~1 below shows that the metric itself dictates which of the two is the admissible completion for a given $k$. We compute the second homology group of both completions explicitly, $H_2(Σ_{\rm BH},\mathcal H)=0$ for the black hole exterior relative to its horizon and $H_2(Σ_{\rm WH})\cong\mathbb Z$ for the completed wormhole manifold, giving a discrete invariant that distinguishes the two branches.

</details>


### [187] [A Differentiable Parametric Model of Binary-Black-Hole Initial Data: I. Conformally flat Bowen-York punctures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10173)
*Frederik De Ceuster,Tjonnie G. -F. Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出参数化、可微的初值数据模型，将稀疏网格谱插值仅用于为椭圆求解器提供热启动，再用少量牛顿迭代将约束残差精修至离散约束容差内，并在JAX中实现解析导数以支持参数定位、优化与敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞约束满足初值数据需要在物理参数空间中反复求解非线性椭圆边值问题，计算代价高；需要一种能高效遍历参数空间、同时保证约束精度的参数化初值模型。

Method: 结合稀疏网格谱插值与牛顿精修：插值仅对底层椭圆求解器提供warm start而非替代求解器；少量牛顿迭代将插值解精修至逐行均衡的约束残差‖R‖∞≤1e-10；JAX实现提供关于物理参数的解析导数，支持梯度参数定位、优化和敏感性分析。算例采用共形平坦Bowen–York puncture，构建四维对齐自旋与八维一般自旋准圆轨道双黑洞初值模型。

Result: 精修后的解与插值误差无关，均达到离散约束残差≤1e-10；通过与TwoPunctures对比验证初值数据；实现ADM质量和角动量的可信定位，并利用专门的分隔–切向动量模型进行有效势偏心率降低。

Conclusion: 该参数化可微初值模型在保持约束满足精度的同时，实现高效参数空间遍历、梯度驱动参数定位与优化，适用于双黑洞初值数据的质量/角动量定位及偏心率降低等任务。

Abstract: Constraint-satisfying initial data for binary black holes require the repeated solution of a nonlinear elliptic boundary-value problem throughout the physical parameter space. We present a para\-metric, differentiable initial-data model that combines sparse-grid spectral interpolation with Newton refinement over a family of binary configurations. Interpolation is used only to provide warm starts for the underlying elliptic solver, rather than to replace it. A small number of Newton iterations refines each interpolated solution to a discrete, row-equilibrated constraint residual $\|R\|_\infty\le10^{-10}$, independent of the interpolation error, so that every returned initial-data set satisfies the discretized constraint to that tolerance. Our JAX implementation provides analytic derivatives with respect to the physical parameters, enabling gradient-based parameter targeting, optimization, and sensitivity analysis. We demonstrate the method for quasi-circular binary-black-hole initial data using conformally flat Bowen--York punctures by constructing four-dimensional aligned-spin and eight-dimensional general-spin models. The resulting initial data are validated against TwoPunctures, and the method is applied to certified targeting of the physical Arnowitt--Deser--Misner mass and angular momentum and to effective-potential eccentricity reduction using a dedicated separation--tangential-momentum model.

</details>


### [188] [Testing Source Dependence in Gravitational-Wave Tests of General Relativity with Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10220)
*Yi-Fan Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息的非参数框架，用于在群体层面检验引力波广义相对论检验中的偏离参数是否依赖于源属性，并用模拟和真实数据验证其诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波检验广义相对论中的现象学偏离参数不一定代表源的普适基本属性，而可能是依赖源属性的派生量；波形系统误差或数据质量问题导致的表观偏离也可能依赖源属性。因此除单事件分析外，还需在群体层面识别系统性趋势。

Method: 引入一种非参数框架，利用互信息检验偏离参数是否依赖源属性。先用大质量引力子波形模拟进行验证（此时参数化后爱因斯坦系数预期依赖光度距离和源质量），再分析GWTC-4.0和4-OGC的公开真实数据产品，包括现象学参数化检验、修改色散检验和宇称破坏检验。

Result: 模拟显示，在当前第四次观测运行的代表性探测灵敏度下，当引力子质量≥1×10⁻²² eV时，质量与距离依赖关系变得清晰；而对照的广义相对论模拟得到零结果。真实数据分析中，现象学偏离参数与有效自旋存在初步相关性，修改色散或宇称破坏参数与源质量的相关性可见。

Conclusion: 群体目录层面的依赖性可识别模式，从而用于诊断广义相对论检验中偏离的来源。该框架补充了单事件分析和参数化层级群体检验。

Abstract: Phenomenological deviation parameters used in gravitational-wave tests of general relativity need not represent universal fundamental properties of the sources. Instead, a deviation parameter can be a derived quantity that depends on source properties. Apparent violations of general relativity arising from waveform systematics or data-quality issues can also depend on source properties. In addition to diagnosing individual events, it is helpful to identify systematic trends at the population level. We introduce a nonparametric framework that uses mutual information to test whether the deviations depend on source properties. We validate the framework using simulations with massive graviton waveforms. Assuming we do not have prior knowledge of the underlying theory, massive graviton effects can be captured by a parameterized post-Einstein coefficient that is expected to depend on the luminosity distance and source mass. The results show that the mass and distance dependencies become clear for graviton masses of $\geq 1\times10^{-22}\,\mathrm{eV}$ at detector sensitivities representative of the current fourth observing run, while the controlled general relativity simulations yield null results. We then analyze public real-data products for tests of general relativity from GWTC-4.0 and 4-OGC, including phenomenological parameterized tests, modified dispersion tests, and parity violation tests. The phenomenological deviation parameters have tentative correlations with effective spin, and correlations of modified-dispersion or parity-violation parameters with source mass are visible. These results demonstrate that catalog-level dependence can identify patterns and thus be used to diagnose the origin of deviations in testing general relativity. The framework therefore complements single-event analyses and parameterized hierarchical population tests.

</details>


### [189] [Pulsar Timing Response and Spatial Correlations of Shear Modes in Torsionless Palatini Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10251)
*Tian-Shi Li,Yu-Mei Wu,Chang Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini时空中非度规性产生的剪切模式（shear-x、shear-y）对脉冲星计时残差的响应，推导了单脉冲星红移、频域两点关联函数及空间相关性，发现其归一化重叠约化函数在短波极限下为纯偶极形式cosζ，与太阳系星历误差产生空间简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳赫兹频段，脉冲星计时残差的空间相关性是刻画随机引力波背景及其偏振内容的关键观测量。Palatini时空中的非度规性可产生额外的剪切模式，但其在脉冲星计时阵列中的可观测特征尚不明确，需要系统研究其响应信号以判断能否识别此类新物理。

Method: 假设电磁场与物理度规最小耦合，且地球与脉冲星具有不可忽略的有效超动量响应，推导剪切模式引起的脉冲星与地球运动所产生的脉冲星计时响应。具体计算单脉冲星红移、频域两点关联函数以及相应的空间相关性；针对稳态、各向同性的随机剪切背景，在短波极限下导出归一化重叠约化函数（ORF）。

Result: 对不同脉冲星，归一化重叠约化函数在短波极限下化为纯偶极形式Γ_ab^sh(ζ)=cosζ，其中ζ为两颗脉冲星的角间距。该偶极相关亦可由各向同性的太阳系星历误差产生，因此剪切信号与星历误差信号之间存在空间简并。

Conclusion: Palatini剪切模式在脉冲星计时中表现为纯偶极空间相关，与太阳系星历误差引起的相关形式相同，二者存在空间简并。这一简并表明，仅凭角度相关不足以识别Palatini剪切信号，需要引入角度相关之外的信息（如不同频率依赖、偏振或天空分布特征）来区分。

Abstract: In the nanohertz band, spatial correlations between pulsar timing residuals provide a key observable for characterizing stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds and probing their polarization content. In torsionless Palatini spacetime, nonmetricity can generate two additional shear modes, referred to as the shear-$x$ and shear-$y$ modes. In this work, we investigate the pulsar timing response produced by the shear-induced motions of the emitting pulsar and the receiving Earth. Assuming that the electromagnetic field is minimally coupled to the physical metric and that the Earth and pulsar possess non-negligible effective hypermomentum responses, we derive the single-pulsar redshift, the frequency-domain two-point correlation function, and the corresponding spatial correlation. For a stationary and isotropic stochastic shear background, the normalized overlap reduction function (ORF) for distinct pulsars reduces in the short-wavelength limit to the pure dipolar form $Γ_{ab}^{\mathrm{sh}}(ζ)=\cosζ$, where $ζ$ is the angular separation between the two pulsars. The same dipolar correlation can also be produced by isotropic Solar System ephemeris errors, leading to a spatial degeneracy between the two signals. This degeneracy highlights the importance of information beyond the angular correlation for identifying Palatini shear signatures in PTA data.

</details>


### [190] [Kaniadakis Holographic Dark Energy with Particle Horizon as IR Cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10320)
*A. Asvar,M. Mohammadi,A. Sheykhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于Kaniadakis熵、以粒子视界为红外截断的全息暗能量模型，发现该变形能使原本无法加速的粒子视界HDE产生晚期加速膨胀，且该转变在非相互作用情形下已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦引力下，非相互作用的粒子视界全息暗能量无法产生晚期加速膨胀所需的足够负压，因此需要引入新的物理机制来改变这一行为。

Method: 采用Kaniadakis熵修正全息暗能量密度与Friedmann背景，保持粒子视界作为红外截断，推导自洽演化方程、有效暗能量状态方程与减速参数，并分析绝热声速平方（稳定性诊断）和statefinder变量（偏离ΛCDM的程度）。

Result: 取K=1.90×10⁻³⁶、Ω_DE0=0.7、c²=0.64时，非相互作用情形减速参数在z≈0.585变号；引入相互作用b²=0.03和0.06后转变点仅轻微移动至z≈0.586和0.589。K→0极限连续恢复标准爱因斯坦引力粒子视界HDE（非相互作用下仍减速）。

Conclusion: 在该参数组下，加速转变并非由相互作用产生，而是Kaniadakis变形本身在非相互作用模型中已导致，相互作用仅带来转变时间的微小偏移。

Abstract: We construct a holographic dark-energy model using Kaniadakis entropy with the particle horizon as the infrared cutoff, consistently modifying both the holographic density and the Friedmann background. In standard Einstein gravity, noninteracting particle-horizon holographic dark energy (HDE) does not produce the sufficiently negative pressure required for late-time accelerated expansion. We show that the Kaniadakis deformation changes this behavior while the particle horizon is retained. For the representative parameter choice $K=1.90\times10^{-36}$, with $Ω_{\rm DE0}=0.7$ and $c^2=0.64$, the deceleration parameter changes sign at $z\simeq0.585$ in the noninteracting case. Including the interaction strengths $b^2=0.03$ and $0.06$ changes the transition only slightly, giving $z\simeq0.586$ and $0.589$, respectively. Thus, for the parameter set considered here, the interaction does not generate the acceleration; rather, the transition is already present in the noninteracting Kaniadakis model and the interaction produces only a small shift in its timing. We derive the autonomous evolution equations, the effective dark-energy equation of state, and the deceleration parameter. The adiabatic squared sound speed is also examined as a diagnostic of the effective-fluid stability, while statefinder variables are used to characterize deviations from $Λ$CDM. Finally, we verify that the $K\to0$ limit continuously recovers standard Einstein-gravity particle-horizon HDE, for which the noninteracting model remains decelerating.

</details>


### [191] [Generalized Black holes with Fully Non-aligned Electromagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10332)
*Hideo Furugori,Shinya Tomizawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维Einstein-Maxwell理论中广义黑洞的新族，扩展了Ovcharenko-Podolský七参数解至八参数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ovcharenko-Podolský解的参数化存在一个额外限制，而该限制在位移坐标下并非场方程所必需。作者希望移除该限制，从而获得更一般的解族，并系统研究其黑洞性质、极限及与已知解（如Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson）的对应关系。

Method: 采用Plebański-Demiański适配的参数化方法处理一般Ovcharenko-Podolský解；在Griffiths-Podolský形式下施加角向条件以保证Killing视界截面为球拓扑；分析剩余归一化条件（一般为四次代数方程，定义四个分支）；给出非扭转及l=0扭转族的显式度规与电磁场；使用真正的Griffiths-Podolský形式建立对应关系。

Result: 得到八参数类（扩展七参数类），保留独立电荷参数q（实数或纯虚数），q^2≥0时存在光滑对齐极限至Λ=0、e^2+g^2=q^2的带电Plebański-Demiański解；显式给出非扭转与l=0扭转族的度规和电磁场，分析其黑洞与加速视界、极端性、锥性及各类极限；确定了有效无NUT分支及其退化情形；建立了非加速有效无NUT子类与Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族的显式对应并表达电磁通量荷；q^2<0区段包含新近识别的纯无荷Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson解。

Conclusion: 该八参数类为四维Einstein-Maxwell理论中具有非零、完全不齐次电磁场的Petrov D型时空提供了更一般的框架，统一并扩展了多个已知黑洞族，并通过Griffiths-Podolský形式揭示了与Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson解的内在联系。

Abstract: We develop a Plebański--Demiański-adapted parametrization of the general Ovcharenko--Podolský solution and construct new families of generalized black holes in four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory. These Petrov type~D spacetimes possess non-null, fully non-aligned electromagnetic fields. A restriction imposed in the previous parametrization is not required by the field equations in shifted coordinates. Removing it retains an independent charge parameter $q$, which may be real or purely imaginary. For $q^2\geq0$, both the metric and electromagnetic field admit a smooth aligned limit to the charged Plebański--Demiański solution with $Λ=0$ and $e^2+g^2=q^2$. We impose angular conditions in the Griffiths--Podolský form allowing Killing horizon cross sections with spherical topology. The resulting eight-parameter class extends the seven-parameter class of Ovcharenko and Podolský [arXiv:2508.04850]. The remaining normalization condition is generically quartic, defining four algebraic branches. We give explicit metrics and electromagnetic fields for the non-twisting and $l=0$ twisting families and analyze their black hole and acceleration horizons, extremality, conicity, and further limits. We determine the effective-NUT-free branches of the latter family and their degenerate cases. Using the genuine Griffiths--Podolský form, we establish an explicit correspondence between the non-accelerating effective-NUT-free subclass and the Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson family and express its electromagnetic flux charges in our parameters. The $q^2<0$ sector includes the genuine uncharged Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson solution recently identified by Ovcharenko and Podolský [arXiv:2608.21672].

</details>


### [192] [Parity symmetry as a diagnostic for spin-precessing binary-black-hole waveform models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10345)
*Héctor Estellés,Sascha Husa,Eleanor Hamilton,Cecilio García-Quirós,Panagiota Kolitsidou,Joan Llobera-Querol*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用广义相对论的宇称不变性，导出双黑洞波形与残余性质在面内自旋反号及观测方向反转下的精确关系，并用其检验七种主流自旋进动波形与残余模型的宇称一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的宇称不变性要求物理上等价的构型（面内自旋反号、观测方向反转）具有严格关联的波形与残余性质。现有自旋进动波形模型多基于标定与唯象拟合，可能隐含破坏该对称性，因此需要廉价、模型无关的一致性检验，并界定其对参数估计与天体物理解释的影响。

Method: 基于宇称不变性构造波形与残余量之间的精确对称关系，提出保守的、未最大化重叠（overlap）诊断量，对七种最先进波形模型及残余模型进行一致性检验，并追踪违反的来源；对可局部修正的模型施加修正，对需要重构的模型进行分析；最后重分析八个 GWTC-5.0 事件，用 Jensen-Shannon 散度量化后验变化。

Result: 由对齐自旋标定波形与宇称协变进动动力学构建的模型在数值精度上满足该对称性；原始 IMRPhenomXO4a 与 IMRPhenomXPNR 的中位宇称残差约 1.6×10⁻³且上尾较宽；NRSur7dq4 在高质量比与大自旋量级处违反最大；SEOBNRv5PHM 的非对称扩展存在较小且局部的违反；NRSur7dq4Remnant 的残差在反冲速度上大于末态质量与自旋。违反来源为 IMRPhenom 反对称模中依赖观测者的相位锚定、NRSur7dq4 代理拟合的非不变参数化、以及 SEOBNRv5PHM 非对称扩展中一个非不变的数值相对论标定系数。局部修正可使 IMRPhenom 与 SEOBNRv5PHM 恢复至数值精度，NRSur7dq4 则需重新拟合。重分析八个 GWTC-5.0 事件未改变天体物理定性结论，但 IMRPhenom 修正使单参数边缘后验的 Jensen-Shannon 散度最高达 5×10⁻²。

Conclusion: 宇称等精确对称性提供了低成本、模型无关的一致性检验手段，应在标定波形模型中通过构造本身予以强制满足，以避免对参数估计引入偏差。

Abstract: Parity invariance of general relativity imposes exact relations between waveforms and remnant properties of binary-black-hole configurations related by reversal of the in-plane spins and observer direction. We summarize these relations and construct consistency tests for spin-precessing waveform and remnant models. Applying a conservative, unmaximized overlap diagnostic to seven state-of-the-art waveform models, we find that models built from aligned-spin calibrated waveforms and parity-covariant precession dynamics satisfy the symmetry to numerical precision. The original \texttt{IMRPhenomXO4a} and \texttt{IMRPhenomXPNR} implementations instead exhibit median parity residuals of approximately $1.6\times10^{-3}$ with broad upper tails, while \texttt{NRSur7dq4} shows its largest violations at high mass ratio and large spin magnitude. The asymmetric extension of \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} exhibits a smaller, localized violation, while \texttt{NRSur7dq4Remnant} shows larger residuals in the recoil than in the final mass and spin. We trace these violations to an observer-dependent phase anchor in the \texttt{IMRPhenom} antisymmetric mode, non-invariant parameterization of the \texttt{NRSur7dq4} surrogate fits, and one non-invariant NR-calibrated coefficient in the asymmetric \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} extension. Local corrections restore waveform parity to numerical precision for the \texttt{IMRPhenom} and \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} cases; restoring it in \texttt{NRSur7dq4} requires refitting the surrogate. Reanalyses of eight GWTC-5.0 events show no qualitative change in astrophysical interpretation, although the \texttt{IMRPhenom} correction yields Jensen-Shannon divergences up to $5\times10^{-2}$ in individual marginalized posteriors. Exact symmetries thus provide inexpensive, model-independent consistency tests and should be imposed by construction in calibrated waveform models.

</details>


### [193] [A Novel Scheme for Inter-Satellite Integrated Laser Ranging and Communication in Space-Based GW Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10354)
*Zihao Shao,Siyuan Xie,Kai Luo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种星间一体化激光链路方案：将编码PRN信号先相位调制到USO时钟信号上，再调制到激光载波，从而显著抑制数据编码PRN引起的激光相位测量噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测中采用PRN码进行星间测距，数据编码后的PRN信号在现有集成方案下会引入过大的激光相位测量噪声，需加以抑制。

Method: 提出星间一体化激光链路方案，将用于星间绝对距离测量与通信的数据编码PRN信号先相位调制到超稳振荡器（USO）时钟信号上，再相位调制到激光载波上；并进行理论分析与仿真验证。

Result: 理论分析与仿真结果表明，与现有方案相比，新方案中噪声被显著抑制，且数据编码PRN信号对时钟噪声传递所引入的噪声被限制在指标要求之内。

Conclusion: 该一体化激光链路方案可有效抑制数据编码PRN带来的相位测量噪声，同时满足时钟噪声传递的约束要求。

Abstract: Adopting pseudo-random noise (PRN) codes for inter-satellite ranging, the resulting data-encoded PRN signal induces excessive laser phase measurement noise under the currently adopted integrated scheme in space-based gravitational wave (GW) detection systems. To suppress the noise, a scheme of inter-satellite integrated laser link is presented in this Letter, in which the data-encoded PRN signal for inter-satellite absolute distance measurement and communication is phase-modulated onto the ultra-stable oscillator (USO) clock signal before phase modulation onto the laser carrier. Theoretical analysis and simulation results show that in the proposed scheme the noise is significantly suppressed compared with the currently-adopted one, while the noise stemming from the data-encoded PRN signal on clock noise transfer is limited within the requirement.

</details>


### [194] [Universal Secular External Leg Corrections for Gauge Independent Scalar Self-Mass on de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10365)
*D. Glavan,S. P. Miao,T. Prokopec,R. P. Woodard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在de Sitter背景下，通过增加两类额外图和对外部波函数做secular修正，消除1圈引力子修正有效场方程中残留的局域secular规范依赖，从而导出完全规范无关的有效场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宇宙学中，引力子对无质量最小耦合标量有效场方程的修正存在规范依赖问题，而通常散射振幅方法所需的渐近极限在宇宙学中不适用，因此需要一套能消除规范依赖、且不依赖渐近极限的处理程序。

Method: 按t通道交换质量标量散射振幅的方式组合图，但不取有问题的渐近极限。平直空间1圈需五类图加朴素交换图组合以消除规范固定泛函依赖；推广到de Sitter后，该组合只消除最重要的非局域secular规范依赖，残留与外部腿相关的局域secular依赖，需再增加两类图并对secular外部波函数进行修正。

Result: 平直空间1圈下，五类图加朴素交换图的组合可消除对引力子规范固定泛函的依赖；在de Sitter下，该组合消除非局域secular规范依赖，但外部腿相关局域secular规范依赖仍残留，加入两类额外图和外部波函数secular修正后可将其中和。

Conclusion: 通过纳入额外两类图及对外部波函数的secular修正，可在de Sitter背景1圈阶得到完全规范无关的有效场方程。

Abstract: This work concerns a procedure for removing gauge dependence from graviton corrections to the effective field equations for a massless, minimally coupled scalar on de Sitter background. The procedure involves combining diagrams in the same way as the scattering amplitude for the $t$-channel exchange of the massless scalar between two massive particles, but {\it without} taking the asymptotic limits which are problematic in cosmology. Implementing this at 1-loop on flat space background requires five classes of diagrams, in addition to the naive exchange, and the combination of those diagrams does eliminate dependence on the graviton gauge fixing functional. When those same diagrams are generalized to de Sitter background, their combination cancels the most important (``nonlocal'') secular gauge dependence, but it leaves (``local'') secular gauge dependence that is associated with external legs. Local secular gauge dependence can be removed by adding two additional classes of diagrams, and making secular corrections to the external wavefunctions. The purpose of this paper is to include these additional corrections on de Sitter background in order to derive fully gauge independent effective field equations at 1-loop order.

</details>


### [195] [Field quantization in rotating frames: coordinate covariance and the circular-detector response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10390)
*Sidney Natzuka Junior,Carlos Augusto Domingues Zarro,Matheus dos Santos Soares*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在无界闵可夫斯基时空中统一分析刚性旋转与Trocheris-Takeno(TT)旋转标架下的标量场量子化与探测器响应：区分了态的坐标表象、旋转时间演化是否选择全局基态、以及旋转探测器对真空的响应三个问题，结论是Bogoliubov混合为零、无对称性选择的旋转真空与探测器激发三者相互一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常混淆三个不同问题：(i) 固定量子态在旋转坐标中的表示；(ii) 旋转时间演化是否选出全局基态；(iii) 旋转探测器如何响应真空。作者希望澄清这些问题并统一分析刚性旋转与Trocheris-Takeno两种描述之间的关系及其真空/探测器预言。

Method: 在无界闵可夫斯基时空中比较刚性旋转与Trocheris-Takeno(TT)两种标架描述；计算坐标变换后模式的Bogoliubov系数、模式求和与Wightman函数；考察旋转时间生成元是否全局类时（刚性）或是否为Killing对称（TT）；对圆周运动的Unruh-DeWitt探测器响应进行显式Hadamard减除，并与负共旋频率关联。

Result: 刚性模式与TT模式经坐标变换后与惯性模式张成相同的正频子空间，其Bogoliubov beta系数为零，模式求和重现Minkowski Wightman函数；两种旋转时间流都不选择全局标量基态（刚性生成元非全局类时且无下界，TT时间平移不是Killing对称）；然而在Minkowski真空中圆周运动的Unruh-DeWitt探测器有稳定的非零响应，该响应与负共旋频率相关，经Hadamard减除后为非热谱。

Conclusion: Bogoliubov混合的消失、缺乏对称性选择的旋转真空、与探测器激发三者并不矛盾，可以相互一致；旋转探测器响应体现的是坐标系选择与探测器运动学效应，而非旋转真空的存在。

Abstract: We provide a unified analysis of scalar-field quantization and detector response in relativistic rotating frames, comparing rigid and Trocheris-Takeno (TT) descriptions in unbounded Minkowski spacetime. We distinguish three questions that are often conflated: (i) how a fixed quantum state is represented in rotating coordinates, (ii) whether rotating time evolution selects a global ground state, and (iii) how a rotating detector responds to the vacuum. Coordinate-transformed rigid and TT modes span the same positive-frequency subspace as inertial modes: their Bogoliubov beta coefficients vanish and their mode sums reproduce the Minkowski Wightman function. Neither rotating time flow selects a global scalar ground state, although for different reasons: the rigid generator is not globally timelike and is unbounded below, whereas TT time translation is not a Killing symmetry. Nevertheless, a circular Unruh-DeWitt detector in the Minkowski vacuum has a stationary, nonzero response. We relate this response to negative corotating frequencies, evaluate it after an explicit Hadamard subtraction, and show that it is nonthermal. Thus vanishing Bogoliubov mixing, the absence of a symmetry-selected rotating vacuum, and detector excitation are mutually consistent.

</details>


### [196] [Point particles in general relativity: beyond linear perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10521)
*Jørgen Musaeus,Adam Pound,Samuel D. Upton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将二阶微扰理论中通过匹配渐近展开定义的质点场方程改写为更实用的"skeletonized"形式，用于推导运动方程，并证明二阶计算可避开奇异的puncture方法，采用Hadamard正则化等现成方法，为黑洞双星二阶自力计算开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点粒子在完全非线性广义相对论中一般定义不明确，仅在线性微扰理论中自然成立。作者此前已证明可在一般背景时空的二阶微扰中通过匹配渐近展开严格定义点粒子，但所得场方程形式不便于实际应用，需要更实用的表述。

Method: 采用匹配渐近展开方法处理二阶微扰理论，将所得场方程重写为更完全"skeletonized"的形式；利用该形式推导运动方程；在二阶微扰计算中用Hadamard正则化等现成正则化方法替代奇异的puncture方法。

Result: 场方程被成功改写为更实用的skeletonized形式；该形式使运动方程的推导变得简单；二阶微扰计算可绕过奇异puncture，严格使用Hadamard正则化等现成方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将二阶微扰中的点粒子场方程实用化，简化了运动方程推导，并证明可用标准正则化方法替代puncture，从而为黑洞双星的二阶自力计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Point particles are generically ill defined in fully nonlinear general relativity, although they are naturally well defined in linear perturbation theory. In a previous paper, two of us showed how they can be rigorously defined from matched asymptotic expansions at second order in perturbation theory on a generic background spacetime. Here we show how the resulting field equations can be recast in a more practical, more fully ``skeletonized'' form. We also show that this form of the field equations enables simple derivations of equations of motion. Finally, we show how calculations in second-order perturbation theory can bypass the use of singular ``punctures'' and make rigorous use of off-the-shelf regularization methods such as Hadamard regularization, opening new avenues to second-order self-force calculations in black hole binaries.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Spacetime closedness theorem for homogeneous Lorentzian foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09181)
*Robert Monjo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在洛伦兹层面直接建立了一个时空闭合性定理：从大爆炸与时间控制假设推出有限切片体积，再借助齐性与完备性升级为紧致性；定理覆盖任意维数，并附有 Lean 4 形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间形式分类定理只能组织齐性、各向同性的空间几何，本身不能给出关于环境时空的洛伦兹陈述。作者受齐性宇宙模型的几何解释启发，希望把闭合性结论直接从洛伦兹层面表述，而非仅停留在空间几何分类上。

Method: 核心是从真正洛伦兹性的叶状结构控制假设推出有限切片体积。强版本针对具有有限时间大爆炸和齐性完备类空切片的极大正则整体双曲 (n+1) 维时空；弱版本用累积膨胀率的时间可积性替代极大正则性。论证分为两步：分析步骤从大爆炸与时间控制得到有限切片体积；几何步骤由齐性与完备性把有限体积升级为紧致性。

Result: 在强版本与弱版本两种假设下均得到时空闭合性结论，定理适用于任意时空维数。作者还给出了强、弱抽象陈述的 Lean 4 形式化。

Conclusion: 该工作将空间形式分类提升为直接的洛伦兹层面闭合性定理，并把分析性体积估计与几何性紧致化步骤清晰分离；Lean 4 形式化增强了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Motivated by the geometric interpretation of spatially homogeneous cosmological models, we formulate a spacetime closedness theorem directly at the Lorentzian level. A classical space-form classification theorem organizes homogeneous and isotropic spatial geometries, but by itself it does not yield a Lorentzian statement about the ambient spacetime. The main technical step is to derive finite slice-volume from genuinely Lorentzian control hypotheses on the foliation. This is achieved in a strong version, for maximally regular globally hyperbolic $(n+1)$--spacetimes with a finite-time Big Bang and homogeneous complete spacelike slices, and in a weaker version in which maximal regularity is replaced by time-integrability of the accumulated expansion rate. In both cases, the argument separates an analytic step, deriving finite slice-volume from the Big Bang and temporal control, from a geometric step, upgrading finite volume to compactness by homogeneity and completeness. The theorem is stated in arbitrary spacetime dimension and is accompanied by a Lean~4 formalization of the strong and weak abstract statements.

</details>


### [198] [Area-constrained Willmore surfaces in Lorentzian manifolds: curvature inequalities and the Hawking quasi-local energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09416)
*Alejandro Peñuela Diaz*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在四维洛伦兹流形中发展了面积约束Willmore理论，引入"沿指定法方向的方向临界性"，证明连接相容方向下临界曲面的尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫|H|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)，并由此导出Hawking准局域能量的正性、刚性、稳定性与无穷小单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭类空曲面在洛伦兹流形中的面积约束Willmore变分，其Euler–Lagrange方程取值于法丛（normal-bundle-valued），无法直接化为标量方程，因此经典Willmore理论框架难以适用；同时Hawking准局域能量缺乏相应的正性与刚性刻画。

Method: 定义沿给定法方向的"方向临界性"，并借助法联络识别出一类"连接相容"方向；在Einstein张量符号条件下对该类方向建立尖锐不等式；进一步证明零法方向情形下的类似尖锐不等式；通过超曲面表述将已有Hawking面问题纳入该框架；并在球对称、Kerr与FLRW时空中构造显式例子。

Result: 主要结果为尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫_Σ|H|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)；在优势能量条件与λ≥0下推出 ∫_Σ|H|²dμ ≤ 16π。等号情形具刚性：Σ为圆球且满足Minkowski因果菱形刚性；零法向临界方向亦有相应尖锐不等式。显式例子区分了方向临界性与完全临界性，并说明刚性理论中符号假设的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为Hawking准局域能量提供了正性、刚性、驻定性（stationarity）与无穷小单调性结论，并将先前研究的Hawking面问题统一为空间法方向上的临界性，从而建立洛伦兹面积约束Willmore理论与准局域能量之间的联系。

Abstract: We develop an area-constrained Willmore theory for closed spacelike surfaces in four-dimensional Lorentzian manifolds. Since the Euler--Lagrange equation is normal-bundle-valued, we introduce directional criticality along a prescribed normal direction and identify a class of connection-compatible directions determined by the normal connection. Our main result is the sharp inequality $2λ|Σ|+\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq 8πχ(Σ)$ for criticality in a connection-compatible spacelike direction under an Einstein-tensor sign condition. Under the dominant energy condition and $λ\geq0$, this yields $\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq16π$. Equality is rigid: $Σ$ is round and satisfies Minkowski causal-diamond rigidity. We also prove an analogous sharp inequality for null critical directions.
  These results give positivity, rigidity, stationarity, and infinitesimal monotonicity statements for the Hawking quasi-local energy. A hypersurface formulation recovers the previously studied Hawking-surface problem as criticality in the spatial normal direction. Explicit examples in spherical symmetry, Kerr, and FLRW spacetimes distinguish directional from full criticality and show the necessity of the sign assumptions in the rigidity theory.

</details>


### [199] [Conformal boundary rigidity for simple Finsler metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09527)
*Kelvin Lam,Hanming Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了单连通（simple）Finsler流形具有共形稳定性。作者通过刻画共形因子诱导的边界距离函数的奇性，定义合适的H²范数，并在此Sobolev范数与L²范数下得到稳定性估计，方法是将Mukhometov的经典分部积分技术推广到Finsler情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界距离函数（boundary distance function）在Riemannian与Finsler几何中是不变量，其稳定性问题（微小扰动是否引起边界距离函数的微小变化）具有重要的反问题意义。经典Mukhometov结果主要针对Riemannian流形，本文旨在将共形稳定性推广至Finsler流形。

Method: 首先对simple Finsler流形上的共形因子类，刻画其诱导的边界距离函数的奇性；据此定义适当的H²范数。然后证明关于该Sobolev范数与L²范数的稳定性估计，核心技术是将Mukhometov的经典分部积分技术适配到Finsler几何框架。

Result: 证明了simple Finsler流形是共形稳定的，并在共形因子类上获得了相对于H²范数与L²范数的稳定性估计。

Conclusion: simple Finsler流形的共形稳定性成立；Mukhometov分部积分方法可有效推广至Finsler情形，为Finsler几何中的边界距离反问题提供了稳定性理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we prove that simple Finsler manifolds are conformally stable. Given a simple Finsler manifold and a class of conformal factors, we characterize the singularity of their induced boundary distance functions which allows us to define an appropiate $H^2$ norm. We then obtain a stability estimate with respect to this Sobolev norm and the $L^2$ norm on the class of the conformal factors. To prove the main theorems, we adapt the classical integration by parts technique employed by Mukhometov \cite{Muhometov} to the Finsler setting.

</details>


### [200] [Obata-type equations under the Bakry-Émery Ricci curvature conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09712)
*Mijia Lai,Yiwei Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 基于Robin边界条件下Obata型方程所确定的warped product结构，在适当的Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率条件及其他假设下，建立了带光滑边界的紧致流形的若干刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边界紧致流形在加权曲率（Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率）约束下的刚性刻画问题，将经典Obata型方程与Robin边界条件相结合，以推广已有的无边界或Dirichlet/Neumann情形结果。

Method: 利用Robin边界条件下的Obata型方程所诱导的warped product（warped 乘积）结构作为主要工具，在适当的Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率条件下，并结合其他辅助假设进行几何分析论证。

Result: 在相应曲率与边界假设下，得到了带光滑边界的紧致流形的若干刚性定理，即满足条件的流形必等距于特定的warped product结构。

Conclusion: 带光滑边界紧致流形在Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率与Robin边界条件下呈现刚性特征，其几何结构由Obata型方程确定的warped product所决定。

Abstract: In this paper, based on the warped product structures determined by the Obata-type equations with Robin boundary condition, we establish some rigidity results for compact manifolds with smooth boundary under appropriate Bakry-Émery Ricci curvature conditions and some other assumptions.

</details>


### [201] [Product structures and decompositions of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09750)
*Jin Matsui*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 建立Γ≅ℤ₂×ℤ₂-对称空间的分解与乘积理论，完成非平凡类型的半单黎曼ℤ₂×ℤ₂-对称空间的局部分类，并揭示两个特有现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Γ-对称空间（Γ为有限群）的分解、乘积结构与分类已有系统理论，但当Γ≅ℤ₂×ℤ₂（非循环、非单群）时，其对应理论尚不完整，缺乏非平凡类型的局部分类及特殊结构现象的刻画。

Method: 建立Γ≅ℤ₂×ℤ₂情形下的分解定理与乘积理论，分析不可分解因子及乘积结构的构造；在此基础上对非平凡类型的半单黎曼ℤ₂×ℤ₂-对称空间进行局部分类，并构造/识别特例以考察无穷小等向表示的退化情形。

Result: 给出非平凡类型半单黎曼ℤ₂×ℤ₂-对称空间的局部分类；发现ℤ₂×ℤ₂-对称空间特有的两类现象：存在不同的乘积结构，以及存在无穷小等向表示为平凡的实例。

Conclusion: Γ≅ℤ₂×ℤ₂情形下分解与乘积理论可行且导致完整的局部分类，同时该框架区别于循环或更低阶对称空间，产生非唯一乘积分解与平凡无穷小等向表示等新现象。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the decomposition and product theory of $Γ$-symmetric spaces, where $Γ$ is isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$. These results lead to the local classification of semisimple Riemannian $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces of nontrivial type. Furthermore, we identify notable phenomena specific to $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces: the existence of distinct product structures and that of an example with the trivial infinitesimal isotropy representation.

</details>


### [202] [Multiply warped products with harmonic Weyl tensor and zero radial Weyl curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09759)
*M. A. R. M. Horácio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究一维基底的多次扭曲积在调和Weyl张量与零径向Weyl曲率条件下的局部几何，证明其纤维数至多为三，并给出度量的分类与例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究调和Weyl张量与零径向Weyl曲率这两个Weyl条件对多次扭曲积流形结构的限制，明确纤维数量的上界及其局部几何形式。

Method: 采用共形变换将扭曲函数化为二次多项式，并结合Ricci张量给出的相容方程进行证明与分类。

Result: 证明此类流形最多具有三个纤维；所得度量要么由局部共形平坦的多次扭曲积将其空间形式纤维替换为Einstein流形得到，要么属于具有相等纤维维数与正Einstein常数的三纤维族。例子表明该上界是sharp的，且两个Weyl条件互不蕴含。

Conclusion: 刻画了一维基底多次扭曲积在上述Weyl条件下的局部几何，纤维数上界为三且界限最优，并进一步讨论了soliton型方程带来的更强限制。

Abstract: In this article, we study multiply warped products with a one-dimensional base, harmonic Weyl tensor and zero radial Weyl curvature. We prove that these manifolds have at most three fibers and describe their local geometry. The proof uses a conformal change which makes the warping functions quadratic polynomials, together with the compatibility equations imposed by the Ricci tensor. The resulting metrics are either obtained from locally conformally flat multiply warped products by replacing their space-form fibers with Einstein manifolds, or belong to a three-fiber family with equal fiber dimensions and positive Einstein constants. Examples show that the bound is sharp and that neither Weyl condition implies the other. We also discuss the stronger restrictions arising from soliton-type equations.

</details>


### [203] [On the Asymptotics of the Volume of Hitchin Moduli Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09795)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文计算了固定奇次行列式的秩二无迹Higgs丛模空间在Hitchin度量下测地球体积的精确渐近展开，并确定Hamiltonian子水平体积与指数加权体积的主导渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgs丛模空间上的Hitchin度量（L²度量）是超凯勒几何中的核心对象，但其体积增长行为尚不完全清楚。作者希望精确给出测地球体积的渐近公式及其系数，从而刻画模空间的大尺度几何。

Method: 证明结合超凯勒体积形式的齐次性与正则Hitchin轨迹上的度量渐近；利用辛约化与Prym极化计算系数，并通过等变局部化独立验证该系数。

Result: 对任意p∈M，得到Vol_{g_{L²}}B_{L²}(p,R)=（2^{4g-3}π^{9g-9}/(3g-3)!）R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6})；同时确定Hamiltonian子水平体积与指数加权体积的主导渐近。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Hitchin模空间测地球体积渐近的显式系数，提供了辛约化与等变局部化两种相互独立的计算途径，深化了对Hitchin度量大尺度几何的理解。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a compact Riemann surface of genus $g\geq2$, and let $\M$ be the moduli space of rank-two trace-free Higgs bundles with fixed determinant of odd degree. For the normalization of the Hitchin metric used in this paper, we prove that the volume of geodesic ball in the Hitchin moduli space is given by $$ \Vol_{g_{L^2}}B_{L^2}(p,R) =\frac{2^{4g-3}π^{9g-9}}{(3g-3)!}\,R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6}) $$ for every $p\in\M$. We also determine the leading asymptotics of Hamiltonian sublevel volumes and exponentially weighted volumes. The proof combines homogeneity of the hyperkähler volume form with metric asymptotics on the regular Hitchin locus. Symplectic reduction and the Prym polarization evaluate the coefficient, which is independently recovered by equivariant localization.

</details>


### [204] [Outward minimizing $p$-capacity, horizons, and Schwarzschild rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09813)
*Sehong Park*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在非负标量曲率、渐近平坦端的三维流形 $\R^3\setminus\{0\}$ 上，证明了正向 ADM 质量被 Schwarzschild 归一化 $p$-容量下确界所下界控制，并刻画了等号情形与检测视界现象的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 ADM 质量与分离两端之曲面容量之间的关系，寻求质量下界、等号刚性（必为 Schwarzschild 外侧）以及由容量间隙检测视界的判据，推广/深化经典的正质量与 Penrose 型不等式思想。

Method: 对每个 $p\in(1,3)$ 定义 $c_{O,p}$ 为分离两端、外向最小化有限周长边界的 Schwarzschild 归一化 $p$-容量的下确界；以 $A(g)$ 为分离两端边界面积下确界，结合容量比较与面积下界论证 $m_+\ge c_{O,p}$（在 $A(g)>0$ 时）；并通过等号分析研究刚性。

Result: 证明当 $A(g)>0$ 时 $m_+\ge c_{O,p}$；单个指数处取等蕴含存在最小面积视界，其外部必为质量 $m_+$ 的 Schwarzschild 外部，且所有指数处均取等。等号情形下若另一端也渐近平坦，则 $m_-\ge m_+$，取等当且仅当为双侧空间 Schwarzschild 流形。此外，$c_{O,p}$ 与无约束容量下确界 $c_{M,p}$ 之间的严格间隙可检测视界存在。

Conclusion: ADM 质量受容量型几何量控制，等号具强刚性并刻画 Schwarzschild 解；容量约束有无之差可作为视界存在的检测判据。

Abstract: Let $(M^3,g)$ be a complete Riemannian manifold diffeomorphic to $\R^3\setminus\{0\}$, with nonnegative scalar curvature. Assume that a distinguished end is asymptotically flat, with ADM mass $m_+$. For each $p\in(1,3)$, define $c_{O,p}$ as the infimum of the Schwarzschild-normalized $p$-capacity over outward-minimizing finite-perimeter boundaries separating the two ends. We prove that $m_+\ge c_{O,p}$ whenever $A(g)>0$, where $A(g)$ is the infimum of the areas of boundaries separating the two ends. Equality at a single exponent produces a least-area horizon, forces its exterior to be the Schwarzschild exterior of mass $m_+$, and yields equality at every exponent. In the equality case, if the second end is also asymptotically flat, its mass satisfies $m_-\ge m_+$, with equality precisely for the two-sided spatial Schwarzschild manifold. We also show that a strict gap between $c_{O,p}$ and the unconstrained capacity infimum $c_{M,p}$ detects a horizon.

</details>


### [205] [Holomorphic functions on complete Hermitian manifolds with flat Chern connection, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10159)
*Duoxin Li,Hanyu Wu,Bo Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究完备Chern平坦流形（Chern曲率为零的完备Hermitian流形）的函数论，在无挠率增长假设下证明了Gromov意义下的椭圆性、全纯函数维数估计及与复欧氏空间的双全纯等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作假设挠率次线性增长，本文旨在去除该增长限制，在更一般的完备Chern平坦流形上建立函数论结果。

Method: 核心分析工具是沿由平行酉标架生成的全纯流轨道的Cauchy估计版本，结合Gromov椭圆性与挠率多项式增长条件进行分析。

Result: 证明任意完备单连通Chern平坦流形是Gromov椭圆的；若挠率多项式增长则双全纯于复Lie群；得到无挠率增长假设下多项式增长全纯函数的尖锐维数估计；若存在构成某点局部全纯坐标系的这类函数，则流形双全纯于复欧氏空间。

Conclusion: 结果推广了此前关于挠率次线性增长的工作，表明完备Chern平坦流形的函数论性质在更弱假设下依然成立。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove several results concerning the function theory of complete Hermitian manifolds with vanishing Chern curvature, which we refer to as complete Chern-flat manifolds. First, we prove that any complete simply connected Chern-flat manifold is elliptic in the sense of Gromov. Moreover, it is biholomorphic to a complex Lie group if the torsion has polynomial growth. Next, we obtain sharp dimension estimates for holomorphic functions of polynomial growth on complete Chern-flat manifolds, with no growth assumption on the torsion. Finally, we prove that any complete Chern-flat manifold admitting polynomial-growth holomorphic functions that form a local holomorphic coordinate system at some point is biholomorphic to complex Euclidean space. These results generalize our previous work, in which sublinear growth of the torsion was assumed. The new analytic tool is a version of Cauchy estimates along orbits of holomorphic flows generated by parallel unitary frames on such manifolds.

</details>


### [206] [Integral of Scalar Curvature under Volume Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10160)
*Guoyi Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $\R^3$ 上构造了具有严格正 Ricci 曲率的光滑完备度量，使得标量曲率的渐进积分发散，从而否定回答了 Yau 的渐进标量曲率积分问题，并给出 Naber 局部 Yau 猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Shing-Tung Yau 关于渐进标量曲率积分的问题以及 Naber 提出的局部 Yau 猜想，旨在检验正 Ricci 曲率流形上标量曲率增长与体积增长之间的约束关系。

Method: 在 $\R^3$ 上显式构造光滑完备度量，使其 Ricci 曲率严格为正，同时满足 $\lim_{s\to\infty}s^{-1}\int_{B_p(s)}\Sc\,d\mu=\infty$；并通过类似构造在紧致流形上给出 Naber 局部猜想的反例。

Result: 得到满足条件的非紧度量，其渐进体积比为零；同时在紧致流形上构造出反例，其体积趋于零。

Conclusion: 结果否定了 Yau 的渐进标量曲率积分问题，并对 Naber 的局部 Yau 猜想给出反例，表明正 Ricci 曲率条件下标量曲率积分可无界增长。

Abstract: We construct a smooth complete metric on $\R^3$ with strictly positive Ricci curvature and $\displaystyle \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty}s^{-1}\int_{B_p(s)}\Sc\ddμ= \infty$. This gives a negative answer to Shing-Tung Yau's question on the asymptotic scalar-curvature integral. We also give counterexamples to Naber's local Shing-Tung Yau conjecture on compact manifolds. The noncompact example has zero asymptotic volume ratio, and the compact counterexamples have volumes tending to zero.

</details>


### [207] [Immortal Curve Shortening Flow on Analytic Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10197)
*Aprameya Girish Hebbar,Guanhua Shao,Qi Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在闭可定向实解析黎曼曲面上，任何光滑浸入的闭合永恒曲线收缩流都收敛到唯一的非常数闭测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线收缩流（curve-shortening flow）在曲面上的长期渐近行为。经典结果多在更强假设（如凸性、特定曲率条件）或不保证曲线"永恒存在"（可能有限时间收缩消失）下讨论；本文关注"永恒曲线"（immortal curve）这一情形，探究其极限是否由非常数闭测地线构成，从而推广并强化已有的收敛性结论。

Method: 在一类特殊的光滑范畴（实解析闭可定向黎曼曲面）上，将曲线收缩流视为几何演化方程，对任意光滑浸入且永恒（长期存在、不发散、不在有限时刻消失）的闭合曲线进行分析，借助实解析性与曲面的拓扑/几何约束刻画流的渐近极限。

Result: 证明了在任意闭可定向实解析黎曼曲面上，任意光滑浸入的闭合永恒曲线收缩流都收敛到一条唯一的非常数闭测地线。

Conclusion: 永恒闭合曲线收缩流的终态必然是单条非常数闭测地线且极限唯一，表明实解析曲面上的长期极大解具有良好的几何刚性/收敛性。

Abstract: We show that on any closed orientable real-analytic Riemannian surface, any smoothly immersed closed immortal curve-shortening flow converges to a unique non-constant closed geodesic.

</details>


### [208] [The measure contraction property on Grushin spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10208)
*Michael Albert,Samuël Borza*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 确定了两类Grushin型度量测度空间的精确测度收缩指数（MCP指数）。径向Grushin空间 $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ 满足 $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ 当且仅当 $N\geq n+4m$ 且 $K\leq 0$；$\alpha$-Grushin平面满足 $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ 当且仅当 $K\leq 0$ 且 $N\geq N_\alpha$，其中 $N_\alpha$ 由变分公式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grushin型空间是次黎曼几何中的重要模型，其曲率-维数条件的精确指数长期未定。此前工作（arXiv:2010.16350）提出了关于Grushin平面MCP指数的猜想，且已知经典Grushin平面的曲率指数为5，缺乏对更一般族的sharp刻画与非整数曲率指数实例。

Method: 利用测度收缩性质（MCP(K,N)）分析两类Grushin结构的测地线与体积元，通过对广义极坐标下测度收缩率的估计，精确计算满足MCP条件的最优维数下界；对径向Grushin空间给出 $N=n+4m$，对$\alpha$-Grushin平面通过变分优化 $N_\alpha=1+\max_{L>1}\frac{(2\alpha+1)L}{(L-1)^{2\alpha+1}+1}$ 求得sharp指数。

Result: 证明 $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ 满足 $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ 当且仅当 $N\geq n+4m$, $K\leq 0$；证明 $\alpha$-Grushin平面满足 $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ 当且仅当 $K\leq 0$, $N\geq N_\alpha$。对整$\alpha\geq2$给出首批具有非整数曲率指数的实解析次黎曼结构。当 $n=m=1$ 及 $\alpha=1$ 时分别恢复经典Grushin平面已知的曲率指数5。

Conclusion: 该结果确定了Grushin型度量测度空间的sharp测度收缩指数，解决了arXiv:2010.16350提出的猜想，并首次构造出具有非整数曲率指数的实解析次黎曼结构，统一并推广了经典Grushin平面的曲率指数5的结论。

Abstract: We determine the sharp measure contraction exponents of two families of Grushin-type metric measure spaces. The radial Grushin space $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ is $\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}$, equipped with Lebesgue measure and generated by $X_i=\partial_{x_i}$ and $Y_j=|x|\partial_{y_j}$, for $1\leq i\leq n$ and $1\leq j\leq m$. We prove that $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ satisfies $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ if and only if $N\geq n+4m$ and $K\leq 0$. We also show that, for $α\geq1$, the $α$-Grushin plane generated by $X=\partial_x$ and $Y_α=|x|^α\partial_y$ satisfies $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ if and only if $K\leq0$ and $N\geq N_α$, where \[ N_α:= 1+\max_{L>1} \frac{(2α+1)L}{(L-1)^{2α+1}+1}. \] This resolves the conjecture posed in arXiv:2010.16350 and, for integer $α\geq2$, provides the first examples of real-analytic sub-Riemannian structures with noninteger curvature exponent. Both results recover the known curvature exponent $5$ of the classical Grushin plane, corresponding respectively to $n=m=1$ and $α=1$.

</details>


### [209] [On a question by Firey concerning uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10238)
*Carlos Cabezas-Moreno*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了曲率方程在非负对数凹且无内零的系数下给出闭 C²₊ 超曲面的平移唯一性，肯定回答了 Firey 的唯一性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Firey 曾提出曲率方程解的唯一性问题，即在 C²₊ 类中是否仅相差平移。本文旨在给出该问题的肯定回答。

Method: 考虑曲率方程 ∑ α_j E_j(τ_M)=∑ α_j E_j(τ_N)，其中 τ 为形状算子（第二基本形式），E_j 为高阶平均曲率，系数 (α_1,…,α_n) 非负、非零、对数凹且无内部零点；证明对任意两个闭 C²₊ 超曲面 M,N⊂ℝ^{n+1} 解只能相差一个平移。

Result: 在系数对数凹且无内零的条件下，曲率方程的解在平移意义下唯一。

Conclusion: 该结果在 C²₊ 类中肯定地回答了 Firey 的唯一性问题。

Abstract: We prove that the curvature equation $\sum_{j=1}^n α_j E_j(τ_{\mathcal{M}})=\sum_{j=1}^n α_j E_j(τ_{\mathcal{N}})$ yields uniqueness up to translation for any two closed $C^2_+$ hypersurfaces $\mathcal{M}, \mathcal{N}\hookrightarrow\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ whenever $(α_1,\ldots,α_n)\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^n\setminus\{0\}$ is log-concave and has no internal zeros. This gives an affirmative answer to a uniqueness question posed by Firey in the $C^2_+$ class.

</details>


### [210] [Local Topology of Riemannian Manifolds with Lower Intermediate Ricci Curvature Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10268)
*Philipp Reiser,Masoumeh Zarei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了一类闭黎曼流形上局部Betti数的消没定理，条件为体积下界、直径上界与中间Ricci曲率下界，并推广至开流形得到全局Betti数消没定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Betti数消没结果分别依赖截面曲率下界或Ricci曲率下界，二者之间存在间隙；本文希望用介于两者之间的中间Ricci曲率统一并插值这些结果。

Method: 在闭n维黎曼流形上，结合体积下界、直径上界与中间Ricci曲率下界进行论证；所采用的方法进一步拓展到开流形情形。

Result: 证明了闭流形局部Betti数的消没定理，该定理推广并插值了截面曲率下界与Ricci曲率下界下的已知结果；对开流形得到对应的全局Betti数消没定理。

Conclusion: 中间Ricci曲率可作为统一框架，将经典曲率条件下的Betti数消没现象自然连接起来，并适用于非紧（开）流形。

Abstract: We prove a vanishing theorem for the local Betti numbers of closed, $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifolds with a lower volume bound, an upper diameter bound, and a lower intermediate Ricci curvature bound. This generalizes and interpolates between known results under lower sectional and Ricci curvature bounds. Moreover, the methods extend to open manifolds, yielding a corresponding vanishing theorem for the global Betti numbers.

</details>


### [211] [Alexandrov-Type Rigidity for Minimal Capillary Hypersurfaces on Rotational Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10318)
*Caiyan Li,Chao Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在生成剖面的适当条件下，支撑于欧氏空间中单端旋转超曲面上的连通紧致嵌入极小毛细超曲面必为水平切片；构造反例说明主斜率条件不可省略；对钝接触角在反向剖面不等式下给出悬链面带的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极小毛细超曲面（minimal capillary hypersurface，即自由边界位于支撑超曲面上、且与支撑面成固定接触角的极小超曲面）在旋转支撑面上的刚性与分类问题，明确水平切片这一结论所需的充分条件及其必要性。

Method: 在生成剖面上施加适当条件（含"主斜率条件"，main slope condition），结合毛细边值问题的稳定性/单调性分析进行刚性证明；通过显式构造反例检验条件最优性；对钝接触角情形引入"反向剖面不等式"进行悬链面带（catenoid-band）的分类讨论。

Result: 1) 证明了在生成剖面满足适当条件时，所有连通、紧致、嵌入的极小毛细超曲面均为水平切片；2) 构造出一个光滑单端旋转支撑面，其上存在非水平的平面毛细 n-球，表明主斜率条件一般不可去掉；3) 对钝接触角，在反向剖面不等式下得到悬链面带的分类。

Conclusion: 水平切片刚性在"主斜率条件"下成立且该条件在一般情况下是本质的；钝接触角情形需借助反向剖面不等式才能获得悬链面带的完整分类，说明接触角与剖面几何共同决定解的结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that, under suitable conditions on the generating profile, every connected, compact embedded minimal capillary hypersurface supported on a one-ended rotational hypersurface in the Euclidean space is a horizontal slice. We also construct a smooth one-ended rotational support carrying a non-horizontal planar capillary $n$-ball, showing that the main slope condition cannot in general be omitted. For obtuse contact angles, we further obtain a catenoid-band classification under a reverse profile inequality.

</details>


### [212] [Unbounded normalized scalar curvature integrals in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10323)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在所有维度 n≥4 中构造反例，否定 Yau 关于归一化标量曲率积分的猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 曾提出关于归一化标量曲率积分是否有界的问题；本文旨在通过构造反例来回答该问题。

Method: 对每个 n≥4，在 R^n 上构造具有非负 Ricci 曲率和极点的光滑完备黎曼度量 g，并分析其测地球 B_q(R) 上标量曲率积分的行为。

Result: 对每个 n≥4 及任意固定 q∈R^n，均有 lim_{R→∞} R^{2−n} ∫_{B_q(R)} Scal_g dV_g = +∞。

Conclusion: Yau 的归一化标量曲率积分问题在一般情形下不成立，其对应上界估计不成立。

Abstract: We give counterexamples to Yau's question on normalized scalar curvature integrals in every dimension $n\geq4$. For each such $n$, there exists a smooth complete Riemannian metric $g$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with nonnegative Ricci curvature and a pole such that \[
  \lim_{R\to\infty}R^{2-n}\int_{B_q(R)}\mathrm{Scal}_g\,\mathrm dV_g=+\infty \] for every fixed $q\in\mathbb{R}^n$. Here $B_q(R)$ denotes the geodesic ball of radius $R$ centered at $q$, and $\mathrm{Scal}_g$ is the scalar curvature of $g$.

</details>


### [213] [Nonexistence of complete metrics with uniformly positive scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10442)
*Tsz-Kiu Aaron Chow*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于万有覆盖为spin的连通可定向偶数维流形，在无穷相对K-面积或相对上同调条件下，不存在具有一致正标量曲率的完备度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正标量曲率度量的存在性障碍，将经典紧流形结果推广到非紧流形上，刻画无穷远端的几何与拓扑条件对正标量曲率度量的限制。

Method: 在带边紧流形的spin覆盖上比较扭曲Dirac算子，结合基本域上的热核论证与长颈估计。

Result: 若沿逃逸到无穷的紧集序列上的无穷相对K-面积或相对上同调条件成立（在固定且补集紧的开子集上），则流形不存在一致正标量曲率的完备度量。

Conclusion: 给出了非紧偶数维spin流形上正标量曲率度量不存在性的新判据，揭示了无穷远端的K-面积与相对上同调条件是重要障碍。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a connected oriented even-dimensional manifold whose universal cover is spin. We prove that $X$ admits no complete metric with uniformly positive scalar curvature when either infinite relative $K$-area or a relative cohomological condition holds along compact sets escaping to infinity in a fixed open subset with compact complement. The proof compares twisted Dirac operators on spin covers of compact manifolds with boundary and combines a heat-kernel argument on fundamental domains with a long-neck estimate.

</details>


### [214] [Rigidity, sharp inequalities, and stability for $σ_2$-curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10523)
*Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文利用含变参考曲率的共形散度恒等式，在半球面共形度量、正Einstein共形类及非球面背景上证明了四类关于σ_2-曲率、H_2数据与刚性/稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广并加强已有共形几何中的刚性、积分不等式与稳定性结果：去除Case-Wang的H_g挤压条件，将Viaclovsky、Gursky-Streets、Li-Li、Guan-Wang、Frank-Peteranderl等人的结果从常数数据或球面背景推广到非增指定数据与正Einstein背景，并覆盖n≥5含非零Weyl曲率情形。

Method: 使用带变参考曲率的经典共形散度恒等式。

Result: 一、对圆半球面上A_g∈cl(Γ_2^+)且H_2指定为正的共形度量建立一般平均曲率估计；当σ_2(A_g)=0且边界数据非增时得到刚性，去除Case-Wang挤压条件；n≥5常数数据情形分类了与其sharp σ_2 Sobolev迹猜想相关的光滑临界度量。二、在正Einstein共形类中，将Viaclovsky与Gursky-Streets的常数σ_2刚性推广到非增指定数据，含n≥5非零Weyl曲率背景。三、将Li-Li球面σ_2/σ_1刚性与Guan-Wang sharp积分不等式推广到正Einstein背景，后者在正数量曲率下对n≥5成立。四、将Frank-Peteranderl球面σ_2稳定性推广到固定非圆正Einstein背景（n≥5），在正数量曲率下保持H^1与W^{1,4}控制。

Conclusion: 变参考曲率共形散度恒等式是统一处理σ_2型共形几何刚性、不等式与稳定性的有效工具，可将多项经典结果推广至非增指定数据、正Einstein及非零Weyl曲率背景，并覆盖高维n≥5情形。

Abstract: Using classical conformal divergence identities with a variable reference curvature, we prove four main results. First, we establish a general mean-curvature estimate for conformal metrics on the round hemisphere with $A_g\in\overline{Γ_2^+}$ and positive prescribed $H_2$ data. When $σ_2(A_g)=0$ and the boundary data are nonincreasing, the estimate yields rigidity, removing Case-Wang's pinching condition $\sup_ΣH_g\le 3\inf_ΣH_g$. For $n\ge 5$, the constant-data case also classifies the smooth critical metrics associated with their sharp $σ_2$ Sobolev trace conjecture. Second, within positive Einstein conformal classes, we extend the constant-$σ_2$ rigidity results of Viaclovsky and Gursky-Streets to nonincreasing prescribed data, including backgrounds with nonzero Weyl curvature for $n\ge 5$. Third, we extend Li-Li's spherical $σ_2/σ_1$ rigidity and Guan-Wang's sharp integral inequality to positive Einstein backgrounds, with the latter holding for $n\ge 5$ under positive scalar curvature. Fourth, we extend Frank-Peteranderl's spherical $σ_2$ stability to fixed nonround positive Einstein backgrounds in dimensions $n\ge 5$, retaining $H^1$ and $W^{1,4}$ control under positive scalar curvature.

</details>


### [215] [A sharp threshold for mixed $Q$-curvature rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10527)
*Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在正爱因斯坦度量的共形类中，若标量曲率非负且 $I_a(g)=Q_g+a\sigma_2(A_g)$ 为常数，则当 $a\ge-4$ 时度量必为爱因斯坦；阈值 $a=-4$ 是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共形几何中曲率不变量 $I_a(g)=Q_g+a\sigma_2(A_g)$ 的刚性（rigidity）问题。$Q$-曲率与 Schouten 张量的二阶项组合是共形几何中的自然对象，作者希望确定使“常 $I_a$ + 非负标量曲率 + 正爱因斯坦背景”推出爱因斯坦的临界参数阈值。

Method: 刚性证明结合逐点 Obata 恒等式（pointwise Obata identity）与以标量曲率最小值为参考曲率的牛顿恒等式（Newton identity）。为证明阈值最优性，构造局部极点方程（local pole equation）并进行积分估计，通过径向打靶法（radial shooting）将颈部（neck）与近球形帽（nearly round caps）连接；重标度后的颈部收敛到黎曼 Schwarzschild 度量，并确定渐近颈部尺度。此外对 $I_a(g)=\Lambda R_g^\theta$ 情形在 $R_g>0$、$a\ge-4$、$\theta\le1$ 时证明刚性。

Result: 在 $n\ge4$ 的闭连通流形上，对正爱因斯坦 $g_0$，任何与其共形、标量曲率非负且 $I_a(g)$ 为常数的光滑度量，在 $a\ge-4$ 时均为爱因斯坦。阈值 $a=-4$ 在每一维都是最优的：对任意足够小的 $\eta>0$，圆球面存在光滑非爱因斯坦共形度量，具正标量曲率且 $I_{-4-\eta}(g)$ 为常数。四维时，$-4\le a\le-4/3$ 的常 $I_a(g)$ 刚性无需标量曲率符号假设。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了曲率不变量 $I_a(g)=Q_g+a\sigma_2(A_g)$ 刚性成立的参数阈值 $a\ge-4$，并证明该阈值在任意维数下不可改进；同时推广至 $I_a(g)=\Lambda R_g^\theta$ 型不变量，并在四维放宽了标量曲率符号条件。

Abstract: Let $I_a(g)=Q_g+aσ_2(A_g)$, where $A_g$ is the Schouten tensor and $Q_g$ is Branson's $Q$-curvature. On a closed connected manifold of dimension $n\ge4$ with a positive Einstein metric $g_0$, we prove that every smooth metric conformal to $g_0$ with nonnegative scalar curvature and constant $I_a(g)$ is Einstein for $a\ge-4$. This lower threshold is sharp in every dimension: for each sufficiently small $η>0$, the round sphere admits a smooth non-Einstein conformal metric with positive scalar curvature and constant $I_{-4-η}(g)$. The rigidity proof combines the pointwise Obata identity with a Newton identity whose reference curvature is the minimum of the scalar curvature. A local pole equation and integral estimates yield a radial shooting construction joining a neck to nearly round caps. The rescaled necks converge to the Riemannian Schwarzschild metric, and we determine the asymptotic neck scale. We also prove rigidity for $I_a(g)=ΛR_g^θ$ when $R_g>0$, $a\ge-4$, and $θ\le1$. In dimension four, constant-$I_a(g)$ rigidity holds for $-4\le a\le-4/3$ without a scalar-curvature sign assumption.

</details>
